### [1] [Large deviations, effective dynamics, and dynamical phase transitions in random maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26704)
*Ángela Tajuelo-Valbuena,Jorge Hidalgo,Ricardo Gutiérrez,Carlos Pérez-Espigares*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将Doob变换（Doob transform）从确定性混沌映射推广至随机映射（random maps），构造使稀有事件变为典型行为的有效动力学（effective dynamics），并结合谱大偏差方法（spectral large-deviation methods）刻画动力学相变（dynamical phase transitions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大偏差理论（large deviation theory）可刻画时间平均可观测量的稀有涨落，但原始动力学下稀有轨迹指数难采样；已有工作仅针对确定性混沌映射建立Doob变换，随机映射情形尚待扩展。

Method: 将Doob变换推广到随机映射，构造稀有事件成为典型行为的有效动力学；并对倾斜算子（tilted operator）采用谱大偏差方法分析其谱结构。

Result: 有效动力学可直接重现原始动力学下指数难采样的稀有轨迹；倾斜算子的谱结构揭示动力学相变的机制，并可识别选择某一动力学相的合适初始态（initial states）。

Conclusion: 研究把大偏差理论、谱方法与有效动力学之间的联系扩展至随机混沌映射，为研究此类系统中的稀有事件与动力学相变提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Large deviation theory provides a natural framework to characterize rare fluctuations of time-averaged observables in deterministic and stochastic systems. Building on previous results for deterministic chaotic maps, here we extend the Doob transform to random maps, constructing an effective dynamics in which rare events become typical. This makes it possible to directly reproduce the dynamics of rare trajectories that are exponentially difficult to sample under the original dynamics. We further apply spectral large-deviation methods to characterize dynamical phase transitions in random maps. The spectral structure of the tilted operator provides insight into the mechanisms underlying these transitions and, in particular, allows us to identify the appropriate initial states that select one dynamical phase or another. Our results extend the connection between large deviation theory, spectral methods, and effective dynamics to random chaotic maps, providing a general framework to study rare events and dynamical phase transitions in these systems.

</details>


### [2] [Fully Automated Discovery of Phase Diagrams Using Agentic Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26749)
*Robert Amelung,Maximilian Nägele,Florian Marquardt*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出 Phasonaut，一个基于大语言模型（large language models, LLMs）工具调用能力的双智能体（two-agent）框架，通过主动学习（active learning）循环从零开始自主构建物理系统的相图（phase diagram）并对所发现的相（phase）给出定性解释；在经典与量子自旋系统（classical and quantum spin systems）上以较高精度重现了已知相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习相分类方法对已发现相缺乏定性洞见、通常仅适用于特定系统类别，且本身不能高效绘制完整相图；在无系统先验知识条件下仅凭测量自主构建相图并同时获得相的定性理解仍是未解问题。

Method: 提出 Phasonaut 双智能体框架，利用 LLMs 的工具使用（tool-use）能力与广博知识驱动主动学习循环：从零开始迭代地选择并执行测量、分析所得数据，并将参数域（parameter domain）分割为所发现的各个相。

Result: Phasonaut 以较高精度（substantial precision）重现了经典与量子自旋系统的相图。

Conclusion: 该框架未来有望用于快速探索相图未知的系统，包括在自主驱动实验（autonomously driven experiments）中的应用。

Abstract: Classifying phases of matter and mapping out phase diagrams are central problems in physics. Recently, machine learning techniques have emerged as promising tools for phase classification, ranging from supervised learning to various unsupervised methods. However, these techniques provide limited qualitative insight into the discovered phases, typically work only for specific system classes, and do not by themselves efficiently map out the phase diagram. Autonomously constructing the phase diagram of a general system purely from measurements, without prior knowledge of the system, while also yielding qualitative understanding of the phases, remains an open task. In this work, we introduce Phasonaut, a two-agent framework that leverages the tool-use capabilities and broad knowledge of large language models (LLMs) to drive an active learning loop that constructs phase diagrams of physical systems and provides interpretations of the discovered phases. Starting from scratch, it iteratively selects and performs measurements, analyzes the resulting data, and segments the parameter domain into the phases it discovers. Phasonaut recovers the phase diagrams of classical and quantum spin systems to substantial precision. In the future, this framework could enable rapid exploration of systems with unknown phase diagrams, including in autonomously driven experiments.

</details>


### [3] [Geometric properties of Wilsonian flows of periodic potentials in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26753)
*Daniel G. Barci,Nathan O. Silvano*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文为一维之外推广的二维周期势建立了Wilsonian重整化群（renormalization group）泛函流方程，几何地刻画粗粒化过程中势能景观极小值与势垒的演化，指出离散平移对称性守恒、极值数目只减不增。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期相互作用具有多个相互竞争的极小值与势垒，其粗粒化演化可由几何视角刻画，以补充傅里叶谐波（Fourier harmonics）标度行为的不足。

Method: 对一般光滑周期势在常动能投影（constant-kinetic projection）下推导二维Wilsonian重整化群泛函流及其running sine-Gordon频率，并借助反应-扩散（reaction-diffusion）与Burgers表示分析该流的结构性质。

Result: 流方程满足两项关键性质：离散平移对称性（discrete translation symmetries）被保持，极值数目只能不变或减少；因此傅里叶谐波的非线性生成可伴随势能景观的逐步简化。示例中，π-平移对称性的弱破缺使极值由四减至二，而保留该对称性的非正弦相互作用由八极值演化为周期性所保护的四个极值。

Conclusion: 对称性与粗粒化（coarse-graining）共同重组周期相互作用中相互竞争的极小值与势垒，揭示了谐波生成与景观简化可并行的几何机制。

Abstract: Periodic interactions can possess multiple competing minima and barriers, whose evolution under coarse-graining offers a geometric perspective complementary to the scaling of Fourier harmonics. We study this evolution in two dimensions by deriving a functional Wilsonian renormalization-group flow for a general smooth periodic potential and its running sine-Gordon frequency within a constant-kinetic projection. Reaction--diffusion and Burgers representations reveal two key properties of the flow: discrete translation symmetries are preserved, and the number of extrema can only remain constant or decrease. Consequently, the nonlinear generation of Fourier harmonics may accompany a progressive simplification of the potential landscape. We illustrate the role of symmetry with two complementary examples. A weak breaking of the $π$-translation symmetry allows a reduction from four extrema to two, whereas a non-sinusoidal interaction retaining this symmetry evolves from eight extrema to the four protected by its periodicity. These results highlight how symmetry and coarse-graining jointly reorganize competing minima and barriers in periodic interactions.

</details>


### [4] [Simple Routes to Turing-Like Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26814)
*Zhan Zhao,Xiang-Ning Li,Wen-Wen Li,Ji-Chong Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出"局部放大（local amplification）加广域抑制（suppression over a wider region）"这一机制可解释斑点、条纹与网络图案在不同微观动力学系统中的普遍重现，并在元胞自动机与自引力模型中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斑图、条纹和网络在微观动力学差异极大的系统中反复出现，暗示图灵型图案（Turing-like patterns）背后的竞争过程可能易于实现；作者因此检验是否可在化学反应-扩散（reaction-diffusion）模型之外，以简单系统重现这些几何形态。

Method: 研究在两类系统中检验：一是通过在康威生命游戏（Conway's Game of Life）中加入远邻计数得到的二元元胞自动机（binary cellular automaton）；二是由粒子运动与两个连续场描述的自引力玩具宇宙（self-gravitating toy universe）。分析使用方向相关性与内在维度（intrinsic dimension）刻画取向序区域。

Result: 自动机在其控制平面上产生斑点、条纹与共存态，而仅保留两个相反计数的规则产生岛屿、条纹与空洞；方向相关性与内在维度识别出一致的取向序区域。引力系统中，汇聚提高局部密度而质量输运耗尽周边；在原始力律下、标度因子（scale factor）a=1 时两场与粒子密度的平滑空间相关为 0.962，增强长波成分使两种描述均趋向更少的致密凝聚体。

Conclusion: 结果表明，简单计数规则与守恒量的再分配（redistribution of a conserved quantity）即可实现竞争性增长与抑制，为图灵型几何在不同动力学间重现提供两条具体路径。

Abstract: Spots, stripes, and networks recur in systems with very different microscopic dynamics, suggesting that the competing processes behind Turing-like patterns may be easy to realise. We ask whether local amplification coupled to suppression over a wider region can generate these geometries in simple systems beyond chemical reaction--diffusion models. We test this idea in two settings, a binary cellular automaton obtained by adding a distant neighbour count to Conway's Game of Life and a self-gravitating toy universe described by particle motion and two continuous fields. The automaton produces spots, stripes, and coexistence across its control plane, while a rule retaining only the two opposing counts produces islands, stripes, and holes. Directional correlations and intrinsic dimension identify the same region of orientational order. In the gravitational system, convergence increases local density while mass transport depletes the surroundings. The two-field and particle densities have a smoothed spatial correlation of $0.962$ at scale factor $a=1$ under the original force law, and strengthening its long-wavelength component drives both descriptions toward fewer dense condensations. Together, these results show how simple counting rules and redistribution of a conserved quantity can realise competing growth and suppression, providing two concrete routes by which Turing-like geometries can recur across different dynamics.

</details>


### [5] [Disk 2-point functions for conformal loop ensemble: a derivation from the SLE loop CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27767)
*Shengzhi Jin,Xin Sun,Baojun Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文基于共形回路系综（conformal loop ensembles, CLE）与 Baverez–Jego 发展的 SLE 回路测度共形场论，推导了圆盘中二维临界回路模型的体谱两点函数（bulk two-point functions），并将其统一表示为绝对收敛的围道积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维临界回路模型的体谱两点函数此前缺乏基于 CLE 与 SLE 回路测度 CFT 的系统推导；且需一个同时适用于无理与有理中心荷（central charge）的统一关联函数表示，以检验既有解析自举（analytic bootstrap）预言。

Method: 结合局部 Ward 恒等式（local Ward identities）与 SLE 回路 CFT 的谱分解（spectral resolution），将两点函数写成涉及已知 CLE 三点结构常数（three-point structure constants）与 Virasoro 共形块（conformal blocks）的围道积分，并选取合适围道保证绝对收敛。

Result: 所得积分表示对无理与有理中心荷均有效，构成一大类关联函数的统一表示；对无理中心荷，通过留数计算（evaluating by residues）确定离散闭道谱（closed-channel spectrum），其展开与 Downing–Jacobsen–Nivesvivat–Ribault–Saleur 的解析自举预言一致。

Conclusion: 该工作以 CLE 与 SLE 回路 CFT 为基础，为二维临界回路模型的两点函数提供了统一且收敛的围道积分框架，并复现了解析自举结果，支持该 CFT 框架的有效性。

Abstract: We give a derivation of the bulk two-point functions of two-dimensional critical loop models in the disk based on conformal loop ensembles (CLE) and the conformal field theory of SLE loop measures developed by Baverez--Jego. Combining local Ward identities with the spectral resolution of the SLE-loop CFT, we express the two-point functions as contour integrals involving known CLE three-point structure constants and Virasoro conformal blocks. With a suitable choice of contour, these integrals are absolutely convergent and provide a unified representation of a broad class of correlation functions, valid for both irrational and rational central charges. For irrational central charges, evaluating these integrals by residues determines the discrete closed-channel spectrum and yields expansions that agree with the analytic bootstrap predictions of Downing--Jacobsen--Nivesvivat--Ribault--Saleur.

</details>


### [6] [Pushy Random Walk on the Bethe lattice: a Galton-Watson perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27386)
*Jérémie Klinger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文证明在 Bethe 格（Bethe lattice）上，pushy random walk 可行走的动态结构可先验地表示为一个显式的多类型 Galton-Watson 树（multi-type Galton-Watson tree），从而将原问题化为静态随机树上的简单随机游走（simple random walk）；借助生成函数（generating function）工具，系统求得逃逸性质与被困行走者的轨迹统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pushy random walk 是形变介质（deformable media）中输运的极小模型，行走者会推开而非被障碍物阻挡，其在动态生成结构上的逃逸与轨迹统计缺乏可解析处理的框架，已有关于临界密度（critical density）的猜想也待证明。

Method: 在 Bethe 格上，将 pushy walker 动态创建的可行走结构先验地构造为多类型 Galton-Watson 树，使行走者等价于静态随机树上的简单随机游走，逃逸到无穷远对应该分支过程（branching process）的存活；再用标准生成函数工具导出逃逸概率与轨迹统计量，其中访问体积与死端数目的生成函数满足显式函数方程并做数值反演，另以蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）验证。

Result: 对只推单个障碍物的 Sokoban walk，重现已知临界密度 ρ_c = 1-(z-1)^{-2} 及临界点附近逃逸概率的行为，并给出小密度渐近；对可推动两障碍物串的二阶 pushy walk，证实猜想 ρ_c = 1-(z-1)^{-3}，并计算所有密度下的逃逸概率。对被困行走者，最大深度的分布由灭绝概率（extinction probability）给出，访问体积与死端（dead end）数的分布由生成函数的显式函数方程刻画。所有预测与蒙特卡洛模拟一致。

Conclusion: 将 pushy random walk 映射为静态随机树上的简单随机游走，可将逃逸与轨迹统计统一纳入分支过程与生成函数框架，且理论结果经数值模拟验证；该框架把动态介质中的输运问题转化为可解析处理的分支过程问题。

Abstract: Pushy random walks are minimal models of transport in deformable media, in which the walker displaces the obstacles it bumps into rather than being blocked by them. On the Bethe lattice, we show that the walkable structure dynamically created by a pushy walker can be generated a priori as an explicit multi-type Galton-Watson tree, so that the walker reduces to a simple random walk on a static random tree, and its escape to infinity coincides with the survival of the underlying branching process. Standard generating-function tools then give direct access to the escape properties of these walks. For the Sokoban walk, which pushes a single obstacle, we recover the known critical density $ρ_c = 1-(z-1)^{-2}$ and the behavior of the escape probability close to $ρ_c$, and derive its small-density asymptotics. For the second-order pushy walk, which can push trains of two obstacles, we establish the conjectured $ρ_c = 1-(z-1)^{-3}$, and compute the escape probability at all densities. Beyond escape, the same framework yields the statistics of the trace of trapped walkers: the distribution of the maximal depth follows from the extinction probabilities, while the distributions of the visited volume and of the number of dead ends are encoded in explicit functional equations for their generating functions, which we invert numerically. All predictions agree with Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


### [7] [Hall effect in electrolyte solutions: Self-consistent Debye-Huckel-Onsager theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27392)
*Yury Budkov,Nikolai Kalikin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作将自洽 Debye-Hückel-Onsager（SCDHO）框架拓展至交叉电磁场，构建了电解质溶液霍尔效应（Hall effect）的自洽统计力学（statistical mechanics）理论，对22种稀水溶液霍尔常数的平均绝对相对偏差约17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SCDHO 理论仅适用于纯电场情形，缺乏对电解质在交叉电磁场下磁输运（magnetotransport）的自洽统计力学描述，需明确离子迁移率、有效霍尔系数、电荷分布与溶剂流体力学各自的贡献。

Method: 以 Slater 型形状因子（Slater-type form factors）和离子特异性系数刻画非局域离子电荷分布与有效洛伦兹响应（Lorentz response），并在随机相近似（random-phase approximation）下将霍尔电导率分解为平均场、关联与电泳三类贡献，两类相互作用通道均包含完整电场诱导离子速度的洛伦兹旋转。

Result: 在抑制流体力学（hydrodynamics）的点电荷极限下，弛豫修正与独立的奇宇称随机密度泛函理论（odd stochastic density functional theory）及 Friedman 经典结果的弛豫部分一致；22种稀水电解质的霍尔常数与霍尔数平均绝对相对偏差约17%。有限浓度下预言 NaBPh4 的霍尔数呈非单调行为，NaI 与 HCl 的电泳修正出现符号变化，HCl 与 LiOH 由关联效应主导，KOH 与咪唑鎓盐（imidazolium salts）中两类通道量级相当。

Conclusion: 该理论阐明了离子迁移率、有效霍尔系数、电荷分布与溶剂流体力学如何共同决定电解质磁输运，并为有限浓度下霍尔数的非单调性与符号变化提供统一解释。

Abstract: A self-consistent statistical-mechanical theory of the Hall effect in electrolyte solutions is developed by extending the self-consistent Debye-Huckel-Onsager (SCDHO) framework to crossed electric and magnetic fields. The theory incorporates non-local ionic charge distributions through Slater-type form factors and ion-specific coefficients describing the effective Lorentz response, with experimental input for protons and hydroxide ions. Within the random-phase approximation, the Hall conductivity is expressed as mean-field, correlation, and electrophoretic contributions. Both interaction channels include the Lorentz rotation of the complete electric-field-induced ionic velocity. In the point-charge limit with hydrodynamics suppressed, the relaxation correction agrees with independent odd stochastic density functional theory and the relaxation part of Friedman's classical result. For 22 dilute aqueous electrolytes, the mean absolute relative deviations from measured Hall constants and Hall numbers are about 17 percent. At finite concentrations, the theory predicts a nonmonotonic Hall number for NaBPh4 and sign changes of the electrophoretic correction for NaI and HCl. Correlation effects dominate for HCl and LiOH, whereas the two interaction channels become comparable for KOH and aqueous imidazolium salts. The results clarify how ionic mobilities, effective Hall coefficients, charge distributions, and solvent hydrodynamics determine electrolyte magnetotransport.

</details>


### [8] [How many states does an exact relaxation model need? Sharp state complexity and geometric obstructions for reversible chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27406)
*Murad Aznagulov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究可逆马尔可夫模型（reversible Markov model）精确再现两个指定弛豫模式（relaxation modes）所需的最少状态数问题，通过在给定凸区域上最大化两个稳态坐标方差中的较小者，将细致平衡（detailed balance）转化为仅受拉力的索网（tension-only cable network）平衡问题，并对Wright-Fisher扩散的矩曲线给出了状态数亏损的精确渐近常数与最优态密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是回答"一个可逆链需要多少个状态才能精确再现两个给定的弛豫模式"，即有限状态逼真的状态预算（state budget）问题。作者希望在任意转移图（transition graph）下给出下界，并刻画最优有限链的结构与极限行为。

Method: 将问题表述为在固定凸区域内、坐标弛豫率为1与ρ的有限可逆链上最大化较小稳态方差；借助细致平衡（detailed balance）把优化转化为载荷依赖稳态质量的仅受拉索网平衡问题。对Wright-Fisher扩散矩曲线（moment curve）分析最大化序列的收敛律，并用单调向量场（monotone vector fields）构造精确上界证书（upper certificates），辅以精确算术验证。

Result: 对Wright-Fisher矩曲线不存在最优有限链，最大化序列收敛到唯一beta律（beta law），N状态最优亏损为O(N^{-2})，并给出曲线与其凸包（convex hull）上的精确首项常数及最优立方根态密度。凸包常数为曲线常数乘以c/(1+c)，c=(ρ-2)/ρ；ρ=5/2时凸包所需状态数少√6倍，但极限律相同；ρ>8/3时凸包向上扩大反而降低常数。固定状态数下优化器存在；七边形上最小最优链从三状态跳到四状态而最优值不变，并存在多边形族使状态数随边数线性增长；对上界证书精确求解了非对称四边形及381个多边形-速率实例。

Conclusion: 论文建立了有限可实现性（finite-realizability）问题与多边形的有限字典猜想（finite-dictionary conjecture），据此导出有限动力学模型的状态预算与排除检验（exclusion tests）。所有下界对任意转移图成立，为判定给定松弛模式能否由有限可逆模型精确实现提供了理论工具。

Abstract: How many states does a reversible Markov model need to reproduce two prescribed relaxation modes exactly? We maximize the smaller of the two stationary coordinate variances over all finite reversible chains whose states lie in a given convex region and whose coordinates relax at rates $1$ and $ρ$. Detailed balance turns this into the equilibrium of a tension-only cable network whose loads depend on the stationary masses.
  For the moment curve of the Wright-Fisher diffusion no finite chain is optimal: maximizing sequences converge to a unique beta law, and the best deficit with $N$ states is of order $N^{-2}$. We find the exact leading constant for states on the curve and for states in its convex hull, together with the optimal cube-root density of states. The hull constant is the curve constant times $c/(1+c)$, $c=(ρ-2)/ρ$; at $ρ=5/2$ the hull needs $\sqrt{6}$ times fewer states, although the limiting law is the same. For $ρ>8/3$ any upward enlargement of the hull lowers the constant again. All lower bounds hold for arbitrary transition graphs.
  At every fixed number of states an optimizer exists. On a seven-gon the smallest optimal chain jumps from three to four states while the optimal value stays constant, and a family of polygons forces the state count to grow linearly with the number of sides. Monotone vector fields give exact upper certificates, which solve an asymmetric quadrilateral at all rates and 381 polygon-rate instances in exact arithmetic. We formulate a finite-realizability problem and a finite-dictionary conjecture for polygons, and derive state budgets and exclusion tests for finite kinetic models.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出自洽德拜-休克尔-昂萨格理论（SCDHO），以Slater型电荷形状因子（charge form factor）刻画非局域电荷分布，导出短程修正库仑势与电导率解析式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典德拜-休克尔-昂萨格理论（Debye-Hückel-Onsager theory）对溶液中带电粒子行为的描述存在局限，尤其难以处理非局域电荷分布的影响。

Method: 构建自洽德拜-休克尔-昂萨格理论（SCDHO），引入Slater型电荷形状因子模型（Slater-type charge form factor model）；基于经典密度泛函理论（classical Density Functional Theory, DFT）的局域化学势梯度，并结合Ornstein-Zernike关联函数形式，推导解析方程。

Conclusion: 弥散电荷的Slater型形状因子模型构成一种准确的预测工具，可适用于宽范围的离子价态、浓度与温度，涵盖水性与非水性电解液（aqueous and non-aqueous electrolyte solutions）。

Abstract: This study presents a novel approach to addressing the limitations in understanding the behavior of charged particles in solutions. The approach is based on the development of the Self-Consistent Debye-Hückel-Onsager (SCDHO) theory, which integrates the effects of non-local charge distributions using a Slater-type charge form factor model. This results in a modified Coulomb potential at short distances, unlike traditional approaches such as the classical Debye-Hückel-Onsager theory. The proposed method relies on explicit expressions derived from gradients of local chemical potentials rooted in classical Density Functional Theory (DFT) and the Ornstein-Zernike formalism for correlation functions. Our theoretical framework enables the derivation of analytic equations that describe both correlation (aka relaxation) and electrophoretic contributions to total electric conductivity. Within the established theoretical framework, the model of smeared charges with Slater-type charge form factors provides an accurate predictive tool that can be applied to a wide range of ion valences, concentrations, and temperatures, including both aqueous and non-aqueous electrolyte solutions.

</details>


### [10] [Adaptive obstacle deposition drives dynamical localization in chase-and-escape dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27470)
*R. G. Rossatto,Amin Bakhshandeh,H. Arial Alvarez,C. Manuel Carlevaro,José Rafael Bordin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究在二维格点上引入“逃逸者被追捕时沉积永久障碍”机制，将捕获动力学与追捕者所经历的空间几何耦合起来，揭示障碍沉积可使追捕者输运由扩散型衰减为零、轨迹局域化，并在中等参数下产生移动与局域亚群共存的多重动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数追逃（pursuit–evasion）模型假定环境固定，但防御行为会留下持续存在的结构并影响后续遭遇，因此需要考察逃逸者改造环境与捕获动力学之间的反馈。

Method: 构建二维格点（two-dimensional lattice）模型：受威胁的逃逸者（escapers）以一定沉积概率（deposition probability）留下永久障碍，并测量追捕者（chasers）的均方位移（mean-squared displacement, MSD）指数、轨迹半径、局域化比例及时间分辨的障碍密度。

Result: 提高沉积概率可增强逃逸者存活并显著抑制追捕者输运；稀释追捕者条件下MSD指数由接近1降至趋近0，轨迹收缩至数个格距，局域化比例陡增，而追捕者丰度上升会削弱该局域化响应；中等参数下轨迹半径与MSD指数呈宽分布或双峰分布，表明移动与局域亚群并存。

Conclusion: 机制可由动力学解释：快速捕获会在限制性环境形成前耗尽产障逃逸者，故更强的即时追捕压力通过缩短环境被改造的时段而削弱局域化；产障群体的寿命是自适应局域化（adaptive localization）的关键控制量，并以移动、异质、捕陷主导三类区域的经验图景总结，解释为有限时间渡越（finite-time crossovers）。

Abstract: Most pursuit--evasion models assume a fixed environment, although defensive actions can leave persistent structures that affect subsequent encounters. We study a two-dimensional lattice model in which threatened escapers deposit permanent obstacles, coupling capture kinetics to the geometry explored by their pursuers. Increasing the deposition probability enhances escaper survival and strongly suppresses chaser transport. For dilute chasers, the mean-squared-displacement exponent decreases from values near unity towards zero, trajectories contract to a few lattice spacings and the localized fraction rises sharply. The localization response becomes weaker as chaser abundance increases. Time-resolved obstacle densities support a kinetic explanation: rapid capture depletes obstacle-producing escapers before a comparably confining environment can develop. At intermediate parameters, broad or bimodal distributions of trajectory radii and MSD exponents reveal simultaneous mobile and localized subpopulations. Thus deposition redistributes trajectories between distinct dynamical behaviors, producing heterogeneity beyond a uniform slowdown. We summarize the coupled survival and transport response in an empirical map of mobile, heterogeneous and trapping-dominated regimes, interpreted as finite-time crossovers. These results identify the lifetime of the obstacle-producing population as a key control of adaptive localization: a stronger instantaneous pursuit pressure can weaken confinement by shortening the period over which the environment is modified.

</details>


### [11] [Pseudo-entropy in Thouless Pumps and Majorana Topological Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27846)
*Nabaraj Khatri,Shuvam Chudal,Ashish Khanal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究 Thouless 泵浦下 Rice-Mele 模型（Rice-Mele model）的平均超额赝熵（excess pseudo entropy）ΔS₁₂，发现其能区分泵浦的平庸、单体与拓扑阶段，但依赖边界条件、驱动方案与参考时间；在开边界 Kitaev 链中进一步表明赝熵依赖于参考-目标态对与子系统划分，而非拓扑相的参考无关特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在检验赝熵（pseudo entropy）能否作为拓扑泵浦与拓扑相（topological phase）的可靠诊断量，并厘清其对边界条件、驱动方案及参考态选择的依赖性。

Method: 在 Rice-Mele 模型中对两种驱动方案（control-freak 与 smooth pumps）分别在开边界与周期边界条件下计算平均超额赝熵 ΔS₁₂；并在开边界 Kitaev 链中，将固定偶宇称目标态与五种参考态族（偶自由费米子、仅配对、真空及两种固定有能隙态）进行比较。

Result: ΔS₁₂ 可区分泵浦的平庸、单体与拓扑阶段，但依赖边界条件、驱动方案与参考时间；非零交错势（staggered potential）破坏手征对称性（chiral symmetry）并打开 SSH 临界点的能隙，从而抹平相界处的赝熵峰。Kitaev 链中赝熵响应依赖参考态及子系统尺寸宇称，μ=0 时仅配对参考在 L=4m+2 给出精确结果 S_pr=ln2，L=4m 序列具有解析确定的有限尺寸修正。

Conclusion: 在两个模型中，赝熵均为参考-目标态对与子系统划分的共同属性，而非拓扑相的参考无关标志，故其作为拓扑诊断量需谨慎界定参考态与划分。

Abstract: We study the average excess pseudo entropy, $ΔS_{12}$, in the Rice-Mele model under Thouless pumping. We compute it for two driving schemes, the control-freak and smooth pumps, under open and periodic boundary conditions. We find that $ΔS_{12}$ distinguishes the trivial, monomer, and topological stages of the pump, and that it depends on the boundary condition, the driving scheme, and the reference time. We also find that a nonzero staggered potential breaks chiral symmetry and gaps out the SSH critical point, thereby flattening the pseudo-entropy peak at the phase boundary. We also study reference-state dependence in the open Kitaev chain, comparing a fixed even-parity target with five reference families: even free-fermion, pairing-only, vacuum, and two fixed gapped states. We find that the pseudo-entropy response depends on the reference and on the parity of the subsystem size, even when the reference remains gapped. At $μ=0$, the pairing-only reference gives the exact result $S_{\mathrm{pr}}=\ln 2$ for $L=4m+2$, while the $L=4m$ sequence has analytically determined finite-size corrections. These results show that, in both models, pseudo entropy is a property of the reference-target pair and the subsystem partition, rather than a reference-independent signature of the topological phase.

</details>


### [12] [The Fiscal and Economic Implications of Green Mandates: a Boltzmann Approach to Public Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27923)
*Katerina Savvaidou,Georgia Foudoukidou-Louka,Ho-Ryun Chung,Orestis Loukas*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将统计物理（statistical physics）的大尺度视角引入公共治理，构建一种不依赖对未知过程主观假设的事前影响评估（ex-ante impact assessment）框架，并以世界银行基础设施项目的绿色转型为原理验证应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共部门需将多维政策目标转化为财政与投资选择，而可信的事前评估应仅报告由历史基线（historical baseline）与既定政策目标所决定的下游影响，不应引入对未知行政、社会经济或物理过程的自由裁量假设。

Method: 面向以有限个可观测量广义矩（generalized moments）为条件的政策目标，在包含大量项目或公共规划的历史基线上，借助先前导出的粗粒化组合概率（coarse-grained combinatorial probabilities）闭式表达式，进行可处理的统计与下游评估。

Result: 以能源发电部门绿色转型为原理验证，评估了历史基线与不同绿色电力盈余水平的统计相容性（statistical compatibility），并表明同一构造可估计任何能表示为基线观测量广义矩的任意函数的下游指标。

Conclusion: 在缺乏关于基线形成过程更详细知识时，当众多未知影响对集体现象的作用可忽略，大尺度普适性（large-scale universality）为自洽的事前影响评估提供了有原则的路径。

Abstract: Public authorities increasingly need to translate multifaceted policy objectives into concrete fiscal and investment choices while accounting for their socioeconomic consequences. A credible ex-ante impact assessment should report downstream implications attributable to the historical baseline and specified policy targets, without introducing discretionary assumptions about unknown administrative, socioeconomic or other physical processes. Bringing the large-scale perspective of statistical physics to public governance, we construct such an ex-ante impact assessment. Its scope encompasses policy objectives that condition a finite number of generalized moments of observables in the historical baseline, which comprises a sufficiently large collection of projects or public programmes. As a proof-of-principle application, we consider the green transition in the energy-generation sector using the historical baseline of many infrastructure projects from the World Bank. Our earlier results deriving closed-form expressions for coarse-grained combinatorial probabilities enable a tractable statistical and downstream assessment. We assess the statistical compatibility of the historical baseline with different levels of green-power surplus and demonstrate how the same statistical-physics construction can estimate any downstream metric expressible through arbitrary functions of generalized moments of the baseline observables. In the absence of more detailed knowledge about the processes forming the historical baseline, large-scale universality, whenever many unknown influences on collective phenomena become negligible, unambiguously offers a principled route to a self-contained ex-ante impact assessment.

</details>


### [13] [Molecular structure breaks universality in screening behavior of electrolyte solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27955)
*Anna T. Bui,Connie J. Fairchild,Dominic P. W. Thomas,Stephen J. Cox*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文利用机器学习经典密度泛函理论，对分子复杂度递增的电解质模型计算数值精确的屏蔽衰减长度，发现分子细节会定性重塑屏蔽行为，因而浓电解质中不存在统一标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀电解质由普适的Debye–Hückel理论描述，而浓电解质屏蔽长度实验上似呈普适标度律并引发"反常欠屏蔽（anomalous underscreening）"之争；液体态理论无法判定溶剂与离子的分子细节是否维持该普适性。

Method: 采用机器学习经典密度泛函理论（machine-learned classical density functional theory），在一系列分子复杂度递增的电解质模型上数值精确地计算屏蔽衰减长度（decay lengths）。

Result: 分子细节定性改变屏蔽：溶剂吸引作用产生新的单调衰减模式，溶剂极性导致重入行为（re-entrant behavior），仅离子结构本身即可显著改变屏蔽长度指数。

Conclusion: 在这些分子层面各异的电解质之间不存在单一普适标度律，因而对浓电解质实验普适性的平衡态解释构成挑战。

Abstract: Electrostatic screening is one of the most fundamental phenomena in electrolyte solutions. While dilute electrolytes are described by the universal Debye--Hückel theory, recent experiments have reported an apparently universal scaling law for screening lengths in concentrated electrolytes, fueling ongoing debate over anomalous underscreening. Yet, liquid state theory has been unable to determine whether molecular details of the solvent, the ions, or both should preserve or break such universality. Using machine-learned classical density functional theory, we compute numerically exact decay lengths across a hierarchy of electrolyte models of increasing molecular complexity. We find that molecular details qualitatively reshape screening: solvent attraction generates a new monotonic decay mode, solvent polarity produces re-entrant behavior, and ionic structure alone can drastically alter screening-length exponents. Consequently, no single scaling law describes screening universally across these molecularly distinct electrolytes. Our results challenge equilibrium interpretations of experimentally observed universality in concentrated electrolytes.

</details>

### [14] [Unveiling plastic rearrangements in silica glass through persistent homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26970)
*Fuya Katsuyama,Kumpei Shiraishi,Emi Minamitani*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究借助持续同调（persistent homology）方法，将二氧化硅玻璃（silica glass）在无热准静态剪切（athermal quasistatic shear）下的塑性重排（plastic rearrangement）分解为四面体内畸变与网络变形两种模式，并对二者进行了连续量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶固体塑性变形源于局部原子重排，现有研究多关注重排发生位置，而对重排过程中局部结构如何变化了解不足；在二氧化硅玻璃中，SiO4四面体内畸变与连接四面体的网络变形各自的贡献鲜有定量区分。

Method: 采用持续同调（persistent homology）从原子构型中提取环（rings），将持续图（persistence diagram）的变化与实空间中的单个环对应；依据键配位数（bond-coordination）变化把重排事件分为两类，并通过逆向分析（inverse analysis）识别拓扑变化的主要来源三角形。

Result: Si中心三角形、跨两个共角四面体的铰链三角形（hinge triangles）及非铰链O3三角形是拓扑变化的主要来源；Si中心与铰链三角形的诞生时间（birth time）变化与最长边对角变化密切相关，非铰链三角形还反映四面体间O–O距离变化；诞生时间变化大的三角形局域于重排区域；配位数改变事件的三角形诞生时间变化比值大于配位数保持事件，表明前者涉及更多四面体内变形。

Conclusion: 持续同调能够在单一框架内分离并连续量化四面体内变形与网络变形，为理解非晶固体塑性重排的结构机制提供了新的定量工具。

Abstract: Plastic deformation of amorphous solids proceeds through localised atomic rearrangements. While much effort has been devoted to identifying where such rearrangements occur, how the local structure changes during a rearrangement is less well understood. In silica glass, a rearrangement can involve both distortion within SiO4 tetrahedra and deformation of the network connecting them, but the contributions of these two modes have rarely been quantified. Here, we characterise plastic rearrangements in silica glass under athermal quasistatic shear using persistent homology, which extracts rings from atomic configurations, and link the changes in the persistence diagrams to individual rings in real space. We classify rearrangement events into two types according to bond-coordination changes. By performing inverse analysis, we reveal that Si-centred triangles within tetrahedra, hinge triangles spanning two corner-sharing tetrahedra, and the other (non-hinge) O3 triangles are the dominant sources of topological changes upon rearrangements. The birth-time changes of Si-centred and hinge triangles closely follow the change in the angle opposite the longest edge, whereas that of non-hinge triangles also reflects changes in inter-tetrahedral O--O distances. Further, we clarify that triangles with large birth-time changes are localised in the rearrangement region. Comparing the two types of triangles, we find that the ratio of their birth-time changes is larger for coordination-changing events than for coordination-preserving events, indicating that such events involve relatively more intra-tetrahedral deformation compared with network deformation. These results demonstrate that persistent homology separates intra-tetrahedral and network deformation and quantifies each as a continuous quantity within a single framework.

</details>


### [15] [Ultraslow vorticity modes in colloidal fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27172)
*Satyam Raheja,Noman Hanif Barbhuiya,Tsvi Tlusty,Chandan K. Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过高速共聚焦显微技术，在中等密度胶体悬浮液中重建了布朗位移引发的条件平均流体动力学响应，发现存在一种远比非定常斯托克斯（unsteady-Stokes）动力学预测更慢衰减的长寿命、空间结构化涡量场（vorticity field）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非定常斯托克斯图像假定溶剂静止，认为扰动一旦停止，涡量便通过黏性扩散（viscous diffusion）迅速弛豫。但在胶体和生物流体中，热运动持续扰动周围流体，由此引出问题：平衡涨落（equilibrium fluctuations）能否通过流体动力学耦合（hydrodynamic coupling）在介观尺度与时间上维持涡量。

Method: 采用中等密度胶体悬浮液的高速共聚焦显微技术（high-speed confocal microscopy），重建对给定大小与方向的布朗位移（Brownian displacements）的条件平均流体动力学响应。并基于分数布朗运动（fractional Brownian motion）、以独立测得的单粒子亚扩散（subdiffusion）为约束，构建粗粒化唯象模型。

Result: 观测到长寿命、空间结构化的涡量场，其衰减速度远慢于无外力非定常斯托克斯动力学的预测。所提出的分数布朗运动粗粒化模型在定量上再现了该场的时间与空间衰减特征。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在涨落的低雷诺数流动（low-Reynolds-number flows）中，流体动力学记忆（hydrodynamic memory）可在场层面得到体现，对复杂流体中的输运与集体动力学（collective dynamics）具有启示意义。

Abstract: In the conventional unsteady-Stokes picture, vorticity generated by a transient disturbance relaxes rapidly through viscous diffusion once the forcing ceases. This view presumes a quiescent solvent. In colloidal and biological fluids, thermal motion continually perturbs the surrounding fluid, raising a natural question: can equilibrium fluctuations, mediated by hydrodynamic coupling, sustain vorticity over mesoscopic scales and times? Here, using high-speed confocal microscopy of moderately dense colloidal suspensions, we reconstruct the conditional mean hydrodynamic response to Brownian displacements of prescribed magnitude and direction.   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 结果揭示了颈缩恢复的空间机制，并将取向有序聚合物网络中的可恢复应变局域化（recoverable strain localization）与力学可逆性（mechanical reversibility）区分开来。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Nanoparticle-induced local interface regions significantly enhance the bulk mechanical behavior of polymer nanocomposites, yet their energetic contribution in crosslinked elastomer networks remains poorly understood. *Johannes Möller,Wonhyuk Jo,Trey Guest,Lars Fröhlich,Matthias Scholz,Marc Guetg,Felix Brauße,Aliaksandr Leonau,James Wrigley,Ulrike Boesenberg,Lea Brings,Jörg Hallmann,Jan-Etienne Pudell,Angel Rodriguez-Fernandez,Roman Shayduk,Alexey Zozulya,Nimmi Das Anthuparambil,Michelle Dargasz,Sonja Timmermann,Maddalena Bin,Foivos Perakis,Michael Paulus,Felix Lehmkühler,Christian Gutt,Anders Madsen*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爆发模式X射线自由电子激光（burst-mode XFELs）可实现亚微秒时间分辨的X射线光子相关谱（XPCS），但实际性能受光子统计（photon statistics）与样品剂量耐受度（dose tolerance）双重限制，缺乏同时纳入二者的定量优化依据。

Method: 构建脉冲XPCS测量信噪比（SNR）的解析框架，其中计入入射强度的脉冲间涨落（pulse-to-pulse fluctuations），并将该描述与剂量约束结合进行优化分析；随后在欧洲XFEL（European XFEL）实测数据上验证框架，并提出与实验演示每串三脉冲、脉冲间距可变的滚动脉冲串（rolling bunch pattern）方案。

Conclusion: 结果为优化剂量高效的MHz-XPCS实验提供实用指南，并可用于评估未来XFEL运行模式；所提框架与脉冲串方案对提升XPCS性能具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: Burst-mode X-ray free-electron lasers (XFELs) provide a powerful platform for X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy (XPCS) experiments with sub-microsecond temporal resolution, but the achievable performance is constrained in practice by both photon statistics and the sample's dose tolerance. We combine this description with dose constraints and show that, for a fixed maximum dose, acquiring fewer frames at higher per-pulse intensity yields a higher SNR than distributing the dose over a larger number of low-intensity frames. The framework is validated against measurements at the European XFEL, where the intensity fluctuations of the investigated operation mode are found to cause only a negligible reduction in SNR. We further introduce and experimentally demonstrate a rolling bunch pattern with three pulses per train and variable pulse spacings. This scheme enables more than an order-of-magnitude increase in the accessible per-pulse intensity while maintaining equilibrium dynamics. Our results provide practical guidelines for optimizing dose-efficient MHz-XPCS experiments and for evaluating future XFEL operation modes.

</details>


### [20] [Saddle Buckling of a Multicellular Alga Driven by Differential Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27563)

Main category: cond-mat.soft

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多细胞生物发育中细胞生长与细胞外基质（extracellular matrix, ECM）构建之间存在复杂的相互作用，但其定量机制尚不清楚；该文试图借助一个无细胞分裂与重排的简单体系厘清这一生长—力学耦合问题，并探讨其对多细胞性演化转变的意义。

Result: 测量显示12个外周细胞比中央4个细胞生长更快，产生环向应力（hoop stress），从而选择鞍形模态（saddle mode）；该模态的振幅呈现带有经典平方根奇异性（square root singularity）的连续分岔（continuous bifurcation）。这些结果与Föppl–von Kármán理论下的差异生长连续介质模型一致，并由此获得关于ECM力学性质的信息。

Conclusion: 该体系表明差异生长与ECM力学共同决定了简单多细胞组织的形态转变，其分岔行为可由经典薄壳理论定量描述；作者进一步讨论了这一转变在多细胞性演化过渡中的意义。

Abstract: During development of many simple multicellular organisms there is a complex interplay between cell growth and generation of an extracellular matrix that confers structure. Cell-level measurements and 3D reconstructions show that the $12$ peripheral cells grow faster than the central $4$, producing hoop stress that selects the saddle mode, whose amplitude displays a continuous bifurcation with classical square root singularity. These results are consistent with a continuum model of differential growth within Föppl--von Kármán theory, which yields information on the mechanical properties of the ECM. Implications of this transformation in evolutionary transitions to multicellularity are discussed.

</details>


### [21] [Lacrymaria-like dynamics: compression and length change in active slender bodies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27577)
*Clément Moreau,Yukinori Nishigami,Johann Hérault,Kenta Ishimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一种可压缩的N连杆（compressible N-link）平面活性细丝模型，将弯曲角与段长同时作为动力学变量并与局部水动力阻力（resistive force theory）耦合，系统考察被动与主动轴向伸缩对游动和爬行的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微观运动弹性流体动力学（elastohydrodynamic）模型多将细丝视为不可伸长（inextensible），而长度变化对许多活性细长体（active slender bodies）的行为至关重要，故需构建可处理轴向柔度的建模框架。

Method: 基于阻力理论（resistive force theory）建立可压缩N连杆平面活性细丝模型，弯曲角与段长均为动力学变量；先检验被动可伸展性（passive extensibility）对弯曲驱动游动与爬行的作用，再施加主动静止长度波（active rest-length waves），并针对Lacrymaria olor可伸展颈部动力学，研究受振荡随动力（oscillatory follower forces）驱动的夹持与自由细丝。

Result: 在所研究的参数范围内，被动可压缩性通常降低运动速度，表明仅靠轴向柔度（axial compliance）并非增强推进的稳健机制；主动静止长度波则可依据其相对弯曲的相位、波长与频率增强、抑制或改变运动方向。

Conclusion: 弹性压缩—弯曲耦合（compression-bending coupling）能够重组强变形活性细丝的游动、探索与运动行为，说明轴向动力学是活性细长体建模中不可忽略的自由度。

Abstract: Length change is central to the behaviour of many active slender bodies, yet most elastohydrodynamic models of microscopic locomotion treat filaments as effectively inextensible. Here we introduce a compressible $N$-link formulation for planar active filaments, in which both bending angles and segment lengths are dynamical variables coupled through local hydrodynamic drag, modelled using resistive force theory. We first use the model to test whether passive extensibility can improve bending-driven swimming and crawling. Across the parameter ranges studied, passive compressibility usually reduces locomotion speed, indicating that axial compliance alone is not a robust mechanism for propulsion enhancement. We then prescribe active rest-length waves and show that compression can either enhance, suppress, or redirect locomotion depending on its phase, wavelength, and frequency relative to bending. Finally, motivated by the extensible neck dynamics of \textit{Lacrymaria olor}, we apply the method to clamped and free filaments driven by oscillatory follower forces. These examples show that elastic compression-bending coupling can reorganise swimming, exploration, and locomotion in strongly deforming active filaments.

</details>


### [22] [Disentangling Lengthscales in Disordered Fracture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27581)
*Matthaios Chouzouris,Marcelo A. Dias*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文在二维随机熔断器模型（random fuse model, RFM）中区分了两类涌现长度尺度（emergent lengthscales）：裂纹尖端屏蔽长度与Bazant准脆性尺寸效应律（size-effect law, SEL）中的正则化长度，并指出后者可能对应微裂纹的渗流过程（percolation process）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬火无序（quenched disorder）与集体相互作用在损伤过程中会产生多个涌现长度尺度，但其在决定非均质材料强度中的作用尚未被充分理解，尤其缺乏对尺寸效应律中长度尺度物理来源的辨析。

Result: 数值结果将屏蔽长度识别为统计断裂过程区（fracture process zone）尺寸，并表明其与Bazant尺寸效应律中的正则化长度具有不同的无序依赖性，二者并非同一量；同时发现SEL长度尺度与破坏前裂纹扩展（pre-failure crack extension）呈可比标度关系。

Conclusion: 研究解耦了RFM中若干重要涌现长度尺度，否定了将屏蔽长度与SEL正则化长度简单等同的既有联系，并提出以裂纹尖端微断裂的渗流过程作为解释准脆性断裂宏观尺寸效应的候选物理机制。

Abstract: The interplay between quenched disorder and collective interactions can generate multiple emergent lengthscales in damage processes, yet their roles in determining the strength of heterogeneous materials remain incompletely understood. We further uncover a comparable scaling between the SEL and pre-failure crack extension, suggesting the former may instead be more naturally associated with a percolation process of micro-fractures around the crack-tip. These findings disentangle important emergent lengthscales within the RFM, and highlight a candidate physical mechanism for explaining macroscopic size effects in quasibrittle fracture.

</details>


### [23] [Capillary partitioning of solutes on conformally coated colloidal monolayers: a universal geometric threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27658)
*Renaud A. L. Vallée,Carlos Drummond*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解并预测薄膜厚度如何决定旋涂溶质在微球阵列中的最终空间分布，即溶质是进入球间谷底还是留在球冠表面，并为任意球半径、膜厚与润湿液体提供第一性原理（first principles）的定量描述。

Method: 从第一性原理推导谷底溶质分数；以膜厚/球半径为控制参数，将球间喉道（throat）近似为近三角形管道（nearly triangular duct）给出闭式填充速率（closed-form filling rate），并用精确流动计算验证；进而比较填充与干燥的时间尺度，采用精确空隙体积（void volumes）的单参数比值刻画分配，并以薄膜模拟和实验测得的 Purcell 因子（Purcell factors）进行检验。

Result: 阈值以下三球间隙为开放杯状结构、经球赤道处窄喉道填充，约九成溶质进入谷底；阈值以上喉道闭合、仅余浅表面凹坑，该分数下降逾两倍且不可恢复。旋涂时填充速度快于干燥最多可达五个数量级，仅阈值极小邻域例外。以乙醇中染料/五氧化二钽包覆聚苯乙烯胶体晶（polystyrene lattices）为例，预测的球冠上分数（on-cap fraction）跨阈值由 0.06 升至 0.76，与由测量 Purcell 因子推断的值在两端一致。

Conclusion: 溶质分配由容量（capacity）单独决定，可通过精确空隙体积的一参数比值描述，且对所有球半径通用；几何阈值约 0.155 是调控溶质空间分布的关键设计参数，薄膜模拟证实了液体输运方向。

Abstract: Depositing a conformal solid film on a hexagonal monolayer of spheres progressively closes the interstitial pores, and this closure controls where a dilute solute spin-coated from a volatile solvent ends up after drying: buried in the interstitial valleys or spread over the exposed caps. We derive the fraction of solute that ends in the valleys from first principles, for any sphere radius, film thickness and wetting liquid. The control parameter is the film thickness divided by the sphere radius, and a purely geometric threshold, about 0.155, separates two regimes for all sphere sizes. Below it, the three-sphere interstice is an open cup that the liquid fills through a narrow throat at the sphere equator, and about nine tenths of the solute end in the valleys. Above it, the throat pinches shut, sealing the cup and leaving only a shallow surface dimple, so this fraction drops more than twofold, never to recover. Treating the throat as a nearly triangular duct gives a closed-form filling rate, confirmed by exact flow calculations. During spin-coating, filling outpaces drying by up to five orders of magnitude, except within a fraction of a nanometre of the threshold. Partitioning is therefore set by capacity alone, through a one-parameter ratio of exact void volumes, identical for all sphere sizes. Thin-film simulations confirm the direction of liquid transport. For dye in ethanol on tantalum-pentoxide-coated polystyrene lattices, the predicted on-cap fraction rises from 0.06 to 0.76 across the threshold, agreeing at both ends with values inferred from measured Purcell factors.

</details>


### [24] [Silver/alumina-capped Janus particles: a dispersible hyperbolic metamaterial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27736)
*Samhita Kattekola,Raymond R. Dagastine,Alexander Couzis,Ilona Kretzschmar*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作将交替银/氧化铝薄膜蒸镀于聚苯乙烯微球单层上，制备出超构材料帽的Janus粒子（HMMJPs），并借助荧光与共振成像显微镜（RIM）验证单颗粒的超构材料特性及光传播路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超构材料（hyperbolic metamaterials, HMMs）多依附于硅片或玻璃等刚性、固态基底，难以分散与应用于医学场景；将其转移至胶体（colloidal）基底有望赋予分散性并拓展至曲面、离散颗粒几何。

Method: 在含/不含荧光性质的聚苯乙烯（polystyrene）颗粒单层上蒸镀交替的银（金属）与氧化铝（介电）薄膜，膜厚与层数按4P-HMM（四个交替周期的银/氧化铝，403 nm以上呈超构材料特性）设计，制备超构材料帽Janus粒子（HMMJPs）；利用颗粒荧光观察团簇中光传播路径，并用HaloElement的共振成像显微镜（resonance imaging microscopy, RIM）独立验证单颗粒的HMM性质。

Result: 实现了球形颗粒上的交替金属/介电多层帽结构，并考察了颗粒曲率对帽内层结构的影响；荧光信号揭示了HMMJP团簇中光传播路径随团簇尺寸与几何的变化，RIM结果独立确认了单个HMMJP的超构材料性质。

Conclusion: 将HMM结构转移至胶体球形颗粒在实验上可行，所得HMMJPs兼具分散性与可表征的超构材料特性，为医学与离散颗粒几何下的应用提供了潜在途径。

Abstract: Hyperbolic metamaterials (HMMs) made of alternating layers of metal and dielectric have been heralded as promising materials for super-resolution imaging and spontaneous emission enhancement sought after in biological applications. However, the practical utilization of HMMs has been limited, primarily by the solid and stiff nature of the underlying substrate, usually a silicon wafer or glass substrate. An ability to transfer HMMs to colloidal substrates would introduce dispersibility to HMMs and potentially enable their use in medical applications while extending the curved HMM geometry to discrete particles. Here, spherical particles with alternating metal and dielectric layers are used to fabricate hyperbolic metamaterial-capped Janus particles (HMMJPs). Alternating layers of silver (metal) and alumina (dielectric) films are evaporated onto polystyrene particle monolayers with and without fluorescent properties to study the impact of particle curvature on the layer structure in the cap. The thickness and number of layers are chosen such that the final cap film resembles the structure of a 4P-HMM film, four periods of alternating silver/alumina, previously characterized as an HMM at wavelengths above 403 nm. The fluorescent character of the polystyrene particles is used to illuminate the pathway of light in the HMMJP clusters as a function of cluster size and geometry. Subsequently, resonance imaging microscopy (RIM) with the HaloElement is used to independently verify the HMM nature of a single HMMJP.

</details>


### [25] [Corrections to the Fick-Jacobs Description of Diffusion across Pickering Droplet Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27818)
*Rohit Khandoori,Wim Thielemans,Joost de Graaf*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究比较了Fick-Jacobs（Fick-Jacobs formalism）近似与二维Pickering液滴（Pickering droplets）的全解析模拟，发现界面颗粒拥挤会加剧模型偏差，并可用简单几何论证解释所需修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒负载界面（particle-laden interfaces）在Pickering-Ramsden体系与双连续界面堵塞乳液凝胶（bicontinuous interfacially jammed emulsion gels, bijels）中提供结构稳定性和可控扩散输运，因而需要理解跨界面输运；而常用的Fick-Jacobs描述依赖缓变几何与横向快速平衡假设。

Method: 将Fick-Jacobs模型的预测与二维Pickering液滴的全解析模拟（fully resolved simulations）结果进行对比，考察界面颗粒拥挤程度对模型预测偏差的影响，并以简单几何论证（geometrical arguments）解释所需修正。

Result: 模型预测与模拟结果之间的差异随界面颗粒拥挤（particle crowding）程度增加而增大，揭示了Fick-Jacobs近似的适用极限；所需修正可由简单几何论证理解。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个描述跨颗粒负载界面输运的有效框架，并为复杂几何中扩散（diffusion in complex geometries）的更一般描述奠定基础。

Abstract: Particle-laden interfaces give rise to important classes of multiphase materials, including Pickering-Ramsden systems and bicontinuous interfacially jammed emulsion gels (bijels). In these systems, colloidal particles adsorb at fluid-fluid interfaces and form semipermeable layers that provide structural stability and controlled diffusive transport. These properties make them attractive for encapsulation applications and motivate understanding transport across particle-laden interfaces. Diffusion through geometries with varying cross-section, including those found at particle-laden interfaces, is commonly described using the Fick-Jacobs formalism. However, this approach relies on the assumptions of slowly varying geometries and rapid equilibration in the transverse direction. Subsequent studies have developed corrections based on higher-order geometric derivatives, but an elegant closed-form description remains elusive. Here, we compare predictions of the Fick-Jacobs model with fully resolved simulations of 2D Pickering droplets. We find that discrepancies in model predictions increase with interfacial particle crowding, revealing the limits of the Fick-Jacobs approximation. We show that the required correction can be understood through simple geometrical arguments. Our results provide an effective framework for describing transport across particle-laden interfaces and lay the foundation for a more general description of diffusion in complex geometries.

</details>

### [26] [Moonshine Conjecture Formalized](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27001)
*Jin-Cheng Guu*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文以在 Lean 中形式化月光猜想（Moonshine Conjecture）的实践为案例，指出内核验证（kernel check）通过并不等于数学意义得到保证，形式化大型库的可信复用需依赖专家语义审查，而非仅靠机器检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月光猜想在 Lean 中被形式化，易使人误以为库足够大便完成主要数学工作；作者希望纠正这一乐观预期，说明形式化证明之外仍存在未被解决的问题。

Method: 以连接 VOA 与 CFSG 两个库以形式化月光猜想的项目为案例研究（case study），分析形式化规模扩大时可靠复用（reliable reuse）所面临的两类反复出现的问题。

Result: 识别出两个问题：(1) 形式陈述是否真正表达了人类预期的数学；(2) 当代码库及其依赖演化时，该意义能否得以保持；二者均无法仅凭已验证的证明解决。

Conclusion: 不存在可将智能体（agent）生成的大型库转化为社区可信数学的“灵丹妙药”；需回归常规信任模型，依赖维护者与专家社区的数学判断，否则会累积“语义债务（semantic debt）”，损害可靠复用及 Tao 所设想的形式数学愿景。

Abstract: The celebrated and intimidating Moonshine Conjecture has now been formalized in Lean. This achievement might suggest that, once a sufficiently large library compiles, the principal mathematical work is complete. This paper tells a less optimistic story.
  During our formalization project, we repeatedly encountered two problems that a formally checked proof alone does not settle: (1) whether the formal statements actually express the mathematics we humans expect, and (2) how that meaning survives as the repositories and their dependencies evolve.
  We argue that there is no magic pill that turns a large agent-produced library into mathematics a community can trust. Even after the proofs are checked by the Lean kernel, someone must judge whether the definitions and statements express the intended mathematics and whether future updates preserve that meaning. This work, even when done, can only be trusted. We must therefore return to the usual trust model: relying on the mathematical judgment of maintainers and expert communities.
  But semantic review is expensive, making it tempting to keep producing code and take successful kernel checks as sufficient grounds for trust. This shortcut accumulates semantic debt: definitions and results with unchecked mathematical interpretations are reused as foundations for further work. A library may keep compiling and produce the desired formal theorem while users lose track of which mathematical claims they can responsibly rely on.
  Reliable reuse is also a prerequisite for Terence Tao's vision of formal mathematics as something people can navigate, explain, learn from, and build with. This paper takes our work connecting the VOA and CFSG repositories for the Moonshine Conjecture as a case study of the challenges of reliable reuse as formal developments scale.

</details>

### [27] [A preference for local interaction between layers emerges during training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27258)
*Jiheon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究深度神经网络（deep neural network）中逐层训练共享误差下的层间响应传播机制，发现训练早期层间响应弱且无稳定距离次序，随训练推进响应增强并形成"局部性（locality）"：相邻层对彼此变化的响应强于远层，且这一现象在几乎所有网络中成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络各层共享同一误差，直觉上任何一层的变化应波及所有层并引发相似反应。然而，由于训练同时连接所有层对，层间响应是否真如直觉般无差别、抑或会随训练演化出结构，尚不清楚，这是本文的核心研究动机。

Method: 作者在网络训练的多个阶段复制网络并"推动"（push）其中一层，测量该扰动（perturbation）传到其余各层的程度，以此量化层间响应。实验使用 512 个五层网络，在模加法（modular addition）任务上分别用 AdamW 与随机梯度朗之万动力学（stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics）两种优化器训练。

Result: 训练早期，层间响应较弱，且其按距离排列的次序不稳定；随训练进行，响应强度增大，除极少数例外，相邻层对彼此变化的响应均强于距离较远的层，即在几乎所有网络中都出现了局部性。

Conclusion: 局部性结构是在训练过程中自发形成的，尽管训练在形式上把每一层都与其它所有层联系起来；这表明共享误差并不必然导致各层无差别响应，训练动态会在层间塑造出距离依赖的响应格局。

Abstract: A deep neural network is a stack of layers, and all layers are trained to lower one shared error. A change in one layer can therefore reach every other layer. Because the error is shared by all layers, one would expect the other layers to respond alike, whether they are next to the changed layer or far from it. We find that early in training the response is weak and its order by distance is not stable. As training proceeds, the response grows, and neighboring layers come to respond to changes in each other more strongly than layers further apart, in almost every network. This locality forms during training, although training links every layer to every other. We show this by pushing one layer of a copy of the network at many stages of training and measuring how much of the push reaches each of the other layers. The experiments use 512 five-layer networks trained on modular addition with each of two optimizers, AdamW and stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics.

</details>


### [28] [Potential emergence in LLM agents: a statistical physics perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27618)
*Zhuo-Yang Song,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出以“智能体状态（agent states）”作为中间变量，从统计物理（statistical physics）视角量化与预测 LLM 智能体的后训练（post-training）行为，并在 Qwen3.5-9B 的两项任务上验证了全球势（global potential）结构的强化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究多从人类偏好（human preferences）或语料统计（corpus statistics）角度理解 LLM 智能体行为，缺少可测量、可预测的中间表征；本文希望以状态变量为桥梁刻画后训练如何组织智能体行为。

Method: 从统计物理视角研究支配智能体推理状态转移的全球势，引入配分函数（partition function）建模理想 KL 正则化（KL-regularised）转移分布，并追踪势结构、平均奖励（mean reward）与状态熵（state entropy）。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-9B 的安全测试选择（safety-test selection）与物理推理审计（physics-reasoning auditing）两项任务中，观察到后训练过程中势结构如框架所预测地增强，且奖励与状态熵的联合变化提供了一致性证据。

Conclusion: 状态可为后训练如何组织智能体行为提供定量描述。

Abstract: There has been significant recent interest in understanding the behaviour of LLM agents from the perspective of human preferences or corpus statistics. In this paper we propose agent states as intermediate variables through which post-training can be measured and predicted. From a statistical physics perspective, we study the emergence of a global potential which governs the transition of state in LLM agent reasoning. We introduce a partition function to model the ideal KL-regularised transition distribution and track potential structure, mean reward and state entropy. We demonstrate our approach in two Qwen3.5-9B tasks: safety-test selection and physics-reasoning auditing. We observe that the potential structure strengthens during post-training as predicted from our framework, while joint changes in reward and state entropy provide consistency evidence. States thus provide a quantitative description of how post-training organises agent behaviour.

</details>

### [29] [On a Supersymmetric Generalization of the Matytsin Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26997)
*Evgeny Porubov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作推导了超群（supergroup）$U(n|m)$ 上 Itzykson-Zuber 积分的超对称（supersymmetric）类比在 $n,m\to\infty$ 且 $n/m$ 固定时的领头渐近行为，并将其刻画为两个独立哈密顿系统（Hamiltonian systems）的经典作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 Matytsin 关于 $U(N)$ 情形的渐近结果，本文旨在将其推广到超群 $U(n|m)$ 的超对称类比，以刻画玻色与费米自由度共存时的大维极限行为。

Method: 在 $n,m\to\infty$ 且比值 $n/m$ 保持有限的渐近极限下，对超对称 Itzykson-Zuber 积分进行领头阶渐近分析（leading asymptotics）。

Result: 该积分的领头渐近行为由两个独立哈密顿系统的经典作用量（classical action）描述，分别对应玻色扇区（bosonic sector）与费米扇区（fermionic sector）。

Conclusion: 结果构成 Matytsin 的 $U(N)$ 结论向 $U(n|m)$ 超群情形的推广，表明大维极限下超对称积分可分解为两个相互独立的哈密顿结构。

Abstract: We derive the leading asymptotics of the supersymmetric analogue of the Itzykson-Zuber integral for the supergroup $U(n|m)$ in the limit $n,m\to\infty$ when $n/m$ is kept finite. This generalizes the Matytsin result for $U(N)$. We show that it is described by the classical action of two independent Hamiltonian systems corresponding to the bosonic and fermionic sectors.

</details>


### [30] [Reset Quantum Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27417)
*Thomas Argoud,Alain Joye*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出重置信道（Reset Quantum Channels, RQCs），作用于由任意数量子空间直和构成的希尔伯特空间；其不变态求解可化为规模由重置态数目决定的随机特征值问题，并讨论了原性与谱，给出连续时间极限下的 Lindblad 生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对一类兼具子空间内酉演化与子空间间重置转移的量子信道，需要刻画其不变态、唯一性、原性（primitivity）与谱结构，并连接开放量子系统的连续时间描述。

Method: 在子空间直和结构下定义 RQCs：子空间内为酉演化（unitary evolution），子空间间沿给定态进行测量-制备式重置（measure-and-prepare resets）；将不变态确定问题转化为随机特征值问题，并在重置态与酉演化对易时显式计算谱。

Result: 不变态问题的规模由重置态数目而非希尔伯特空间维数决定；给出不变态唯一性讨论及原性与谱的刻画。应用包括小系统耦合多个热库（thermal reservoirs）、随机酉演化下不变态的平均，以及适当连续时间极限得到分块对角算子代数上的重置型 Lindblad 生成元。

Conclusion: RQCs 为子空间直和结构上的重置型动力学提供统一框架，将不变态、谱与连续时间极限联系起来，并可用于多热库耦合与随机酉演化等场景。

Abstract: We introduce Reset Quantum Channels (RQCs), a class of quantum channels acting on a Hilbert space structured as a direct sum of an arbitrary number of subspaces. Within each subspace, the state evolves unitarily; across subspaces, it is transferred by measure-and-prepare resets along given states. In this general setting, we show that the determination of their invariant states reduces to a stochastic eigenvalue problem, whose size is set by the number of reset states rather than by the dimension of the Hilbert space, and we discuss the uniqueness of these states. We further address the primitivity and the spectrum of these channels, which we compute explicitly when the reset states commute with the unitary evolutions. Applications include a small system coupled to several thermal reservoirs. We also consider random unitary evolutions and analyze the averages of the invariant states in this setup. Finally, we show that a suitable continuous-time limit yields Lindblad generators of reset type on the algebra of block-diagonal operators.

</details>


### [31] [Scattering by two parallel semi--infinite arrays of sound--soft scatterers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27481)
*Matthew Nethercote*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将两条平行、相同的半无限点散射体阵列（semi-infinite arrays of point scatterers）的声波散射问题建模为带满2×2矩阵核的耦合离散Wiener–Hopf方程（discrete Wiener–Hopf equations），利用对称性将其解耦为两个独立标量问题并给出精确解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已用Wiener–Hopf技术求解单条半无限阵列问题，但两条平行阵列之间的互作用引入矩阵耦合核，缺乏解析处理；本文旨在给出该耦合阵列问题的精确解析解，并展示对称性对离散矩阵Wiener–Hopf问题的简化作用。

Method: 将散射系数写成一对耦合离散Wiener–Hopf方程，核为2×2满矩阵，由分别描述阵内相互作用与阵列间相互作用的两个标量核函数（lattice sum表示的格和）构成；引入散射系数的对称与反对称分量（symmetric and antisymmetric components），把矩阵系统解耦为核分别为两标量核之和与差的独立标量Wiener–Hopf方程，再沿用已有的半无限阵列求解方法处理，并讨论两标量核的实际计算技术。

Result: 得到耦合半无限阵列问题的精确解析解（exact analytical solution），并表明对称性可将离散矩阵Wiener–Hopf问题约化为两个标量问题；文中还给出了标量核的格和表示与实用评估方法，并与先前工作进行了批判性比较。

Conclusion: 对称性（对称/反对称分解）是处理此类离散矩阵Wiener–Hopf耦合问题的有效手段，所得公式为两条平行半无限散射体阵列的声散射提供了精确解析描述，并可与既有单阵列结果相衔接。

Abstract: This article considers the scattering of an acoustic wave by two identical semi--infinite arrays of point scatterers which are positioned parallel to one another. The resulting system of scattering coefficients is formulated as a pair of coupled discrete Wiener--Hopf equations with a full $2\times2$ matrix kernel composed of two scalar kernel functions that represent interaction within the selected array and between the two arrays. Exploiting the symmetry of the two arrays, we introduce symmetric and antisymmetric components of the scattering coefficients, which decouple the matrix Wiener--Hopf system into two independent scalar problems with kernels given by the sum and difference of the two original kernel functions. The scalar Wiener--Hopf equations are then solved using the Wiener--Hopf technique in a very similar way to what has previously been used to solve the semi--infinite array problem. The original definitions of the two scalar kernels are represented by a lattice sum and practical evaluation techniques are discussed. Comparisons with previous work are also critically discussed. This condition defines at once a vector space which is studied via barycentric coordinates and elementary exterior algebra, thereby yielding families of equilibrium equations for systems with any consistent number $n$ of particles and dimension $d$. Yet despite years of study, a theoretical description of the rolling motion of a solid magnetic sphere still eludes us. One of the reasons is that coupling magnetic fields with mechanical degrees of freedom creates a highly non-linear motion with non-holonomic constraints, which makes modeling these systems analytically or by simulations non-trivial. The Lagrange-d'Alembertian approach to holonomic systems remains one of the most versatile methods to prove central theorems and solve problems in analytical mechanics and electrodynamics. While non-holonomic systems are not accessible by the original theory, recent developments generalize the principle to certain dissipative systems. Here, we present a successful application of these developments to a simple mechanical system comprising a magnetic solid sphere on an incline within an external magnetic field. Using numerical simulations, we characterized how the system depends on its initial parameters. Furthermore, our findings are in line with recent experiments, demonstrating that even very weak magnetic fields can cause unexpected motion of ferromagnetic objects. The simplicity of this example also makes it a promising candidate for undergraduate physics education where the treatment of non-holonomic variational principles is still widely uncommon.

Abstract: Lieb-Robinson bounds (LRBs) have become a standard tool for perturbative results in quantum many-body physics, including gap stability and many-body adiabatic theory. Here, we use LRBs to locally approximate weakly interacting many-body dynamics with one-body dynamics on large, perturbative time scales. This allows us to use one-body transport results to establish the expected many-body transport behavior on finite time scales in the following two illustrative settings: (i) We prove quantum diffusion, as quantified by a certain operator spreading diagnostic, in weakly interacting and weakly disordered Fermi systems in dimensions $D\geq2$ on large finite time scales based on the non-interacting diffusion result of Black, Drogin, and Hernández [arXiv:2506.06468]. (ii) We show for Fermi-Hubbard models with initially low spin-down density, that all local multi-time spin-up correlation functions are approximated by their free counterparts. In particular, we obtain an OTOC (out-of-time-ordered correlator) counterpart to the patterns of short-time entanglement related to lattice path counts observed numerically by Bippus et al. [Phys. Rev. B 113, 035152 (2026)].

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Dobrushin 比较定理是证明平衡态唯一性、关联衰减与局域期望稳定性的有力工具，但其量子类比（尤其针对含配对、空间非均匀与长程相互作用的格点费米子）尚待建立。

Method: 证明格点费米子（lattice fermions）的量子 Dobrushin 比较定理，并将其应用于在二次型哈密顿量（quadratic Hamiltonian）上添加弱多体相互作用（many-body interactions）的费米子系统，允许配对（pairing）、空间非均匀性与长程相互作用。

Result: 若二次型哈密顿量有能隙（gapped），则在足够小的耦合强度下，任意正温度的热平衡（KMS）态唯一、关联在温度上一致衰减，局域期望以逆温度的指数速度收敛到唯一有能隙基态的值；同一区域内成立强 LPPL，且对有限体积含任意偶边界项的 Gibbs 态同样成立。无能隙时，在依赖温度的耦合强度阈值下唯一性、关联衰减与强 LPPL 在每个正温度成立；高温下对一般相互作用成立。

Conclusion: 该比较定理为量子格点费米子提供了统一的稳定性与唯一性判据，其结论在有能隙时对温度一致成立，并可延伸至无能隙与高温情形。

Abstract: We prove a quantum analogue of Dobrushin's comparison theorem for lattice fermions. As in the classical case, it is a tool for proving uniqueness of equilibrium states, decay of correlations, and stability of local expectations under distant perturbations. We apply it to fermions with weak many-body interactions added to a quadratic Hamiltonian, allowing pairing, spatial inhomogeneity and long-range interactions. If the quadratic Hamiltonian is gapped, there is a coupling strength below which the thermal equilibrium (KMS) state is unique at every positive temperature and its correlations decay uniformly in the temperature. Its local expectations converge to those of the unique gapped ground state exponentially fast in the inverse temperature. In the same regime, local perturbations perturb locally in a strong sense (strong LPPL): every equilibrium state of a system with a distant defect is locally close to the equilibrium state without the defect, however strong or extended the defect is. The same holds for finite-volume Gibbs states with arbitrary even boundary terms. Without a gap, uniqueness, decay of correlations and strong LPPL hold at each positive temperature for interactions below a coupling strength that depends on the temperature. At high temperature, they hold for general interactions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 弱耦合区存在尖锐阈值：低于阈值时凝聚体在环内均匀分布，具有无涡旋主体与宏观环流（macroscopic circulation）的巨涡旋剖面；高于阈值时旋转对称性破缺（rotational symmetry breaking）。中间耦合区 Ω^{-1/2}≪a_Ω≪1 中凝聚体局域于单位圆上一点，径向与角向宽度分别为 O(Ω^{-1/2}) 与 (a_Ω√Ω)^{-1}，重标后剖面分解为径向谐振子基态与角向亮孤子的乘积。在 a_Ω=a∈(0,a*) 时，径向与角向宽度均为 O(Ω^{-1/2})，极限剖面不再可分解，而是真正的二维局域态。

Conclusion: 结果表明转速趋于无穷时吸引型 BEC 基态存在由巨涡旋到角向局域亮孤子的相变，且极限形态由耦合强度 a_Ω 相对 Ω 的尺度决定，呈现变分对称性破缺、可分解乘积结构以及不可分解二维局域态三类截然不同的极限轮廓。

Abstract: We study the ground state of a Bose--Einstein condensate (BEC) with attractive interaction of strength $ a_Ω$, confined by a two-dimensional anharmonic trap rotating at angular velocity $ Ω$. We prove the occurrence of a phase transition, as $ Ω\to\infty $, from an annular giant vortex state to an angularly localized bright soliton. More precisely, we distinguish three regimes according to the relative sizes of the radial and angular concentration scales. In the weak coupling regime $ a_Ω= \mathcal{O}(Ω^{-1/2}) $, the limiting angular profile is governed by a variational problem on the unit circle showing rotational symmetry breaking: below a given sharp threshold, the condensate remains uniformly distributed in the annulus and has a giant vortex profile with a vortex-free bulk and macroscopic circulation; above it, rotational symmetry is broken. In the intermediate coupling regime $ Ω^{-1/2} \ll a_Ω\ll 1 $, the condensate localizes at a point on the unit circle with radial and angular widths of order $ Ω^{-1/2} $ and $ a_Ω\sqrtΩ)^{-1} $, respectively. After rescaling, the limiting profile factorizes into the ground state of the one-dimensional harmonic oscillator in the radial variable and a one-dimensional bright soliton in the angular variable. In the regime $ a_Ω= a \in (0,a^*) $, corresponding to stable attraction below the collapse threshold $ a^* $ both the radial and angular widths are of order $ Ω^{-1/2} $; consequently, the limiting profile is no longer a product but rather a genuinely two-dimensional localized state.

</details>

Abstract: This paper investigates measure synchronization (MS) in a two-component ultracold atomic system confined in a double-well potential, which realizes a quantum many-body bosonic Josephson junction. Through spectral-statistics analysis, we identify the quantum chaotic parameter regime and confirm that the system achieves MS within this regime with weak dependence on the initial energy imbalance. We further show that chaotic dynamics not only significantly shortens the time scale for establishing synchronization but is also accompanied by entanglement entropy approaching the random-state prediction. These results reveal close connections among quantum chaos, MS, and quantum entanglement in conservative quantum many-body systems and may motivate further studies on the underlying mechanisms of chaos-assisted synchronization.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索持续电流是否能自发诱导超固相（supersolid），并厘清自旋不平衡费米气体中超流与晶序共存的机制，同时区别于传统 FFLO 态。

Conclusion: 该调制可自由滑移，支持无能隙的平移哥德斯通模（gapless translational Goldstone mode），表明持续电流可自发产生超固相。

Abstract: We show that a supersolid can be spontaneously generated by a persistent current in a spin-imbalanced unitary Fermi gas in a ring. As polarization increases, stable superflow turns dissipative via vortex emission before superfluid and crystalline order coexist. Pairing and spin densities are modulated with an even number of nodes, where the pairing phase jumps by $π$ and excess majority spins accumulate. Even at fixed polarization, the modulation period changes with the winding number, unlike in FFLO states. The modulation slides freely, supporting a gapless translational Goldstone mode.

</details>


### [39] [Bayesian Quantum Thermometry of Ultracold Gases with Non-Equilibrium Single-Atom Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27584)
*Julian Feß,Luca Granert,Sabrina Burgardt,Silvia Hiebel,Alexander Guthmann,Mohammad Mehboudi,Martí Perarnau-Llobet,Artur Widera*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作将非平衡自旋基单原子探针置于超冷原子浴中，通过贝叶斯（Bayesian）迭代估计实现温度测量，并用信息增益优化测量设置，使自适应协议较未优化协议减少2.5倍测量次数。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用温度依赖的自旋交换碰撞（spin-exchange collisions）将浴温映射到探针原子的内态自旋布居，测量布居后迭代更新贝叶斯温度估计；并将不同测量设置下的信息增益与自旋动力学联系，识别温度相关布居轨迹的交点作为测温模糊性来源，进而以期望信息增益优化测量设置。

Abstract: Precise thermometry is essential for experiments with ultracold quantum gases, but conventional techniques such as time-of-flight thermometry are destructive and lose sensitivity at low atomic densities. Quantum probes provide an alternative route by encoding temperature information in accessible internal degrees of freedom. Here, we implement Bayesian thermometry with non-equilibrium spin-based single-atom probes immersed in an ultracold atomic bath. Temperature-dependent spin-exchange collisions map the bath temperature onto the internal spin populations of the probe atoms. Measurements of these populations are then used to iteratively update a Bayesian temperature estimate. We connect the information gained at different measurement settings to the underlying spin dynamics, identifying intersections of temperature-dependent population trajectories as a source of thermometric ambiguity. Both optimized protocols accelerate convergence, with the adaptive protocol reducing the average number of measurements required to reach a prescribed precision by a factor of 2.5 compared with the unoptimized protocol. These excitations, termed rotons, often reflect an underlying tendency of the liquid to crystallize into a competing symmetry-broken state, such as the solid state of helium or the Wigner crystal of electrons in a magnetic field. More than $50$ years ago, it was predicted that rotons should manifest as emergent density oscillations at the fluid's boundary, but this effect has not been observed. It follows the balloon from its dry inflated state to either swollen equilibrium or burst. The formulation recovers the analytical free-swelling stretch and the delayed-burst regime, and its converged states agree with equilibrium energy minimisation. Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文证明在 $d\\geq 4$ 且 $\\beta>\\beta_c(d)$ 时，Dobrushin 边界条件下的 Ising 测度在原点处具有不依赖 $n$ 的正下界，从而推出 Dobrushin 态非平移不变、不存在粗化转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Ising 模型中粗化转变（roughening transition）是否在 $d\\geq 4$ 时发生，即 Dobrushin 边界条件下的界面是否保持定域、对应态是否破坏平移不变性。

Method: 在区域 $\\Lambda_n=([-n,n]^{d-1}\\times[-n,n-1])\\cap\\mathbb{Z}^d$ 上构造 Dobrushin 边界条件（高度 $z\\geq 0$ 全为 +、$z<0$ 全为 -），考察逆温度 $\\beta$ 下的 Ising 测度 $\\mu^{\\text{Dob}}_{\\Lambda_n,\\beta}$，并估计原点自旋 $\\sigma_0$ 的期望。

Result: 对每个 $d\\geq 4$ 与 $\\beta>\\beta_c(d)$，证明 $\\mu^{\\text{Dob}}_{\\Lambda_n,\\beta}(\\sigma_0)$ 存在与 $n$ 无关的正下界。

Conclusion: 由此任何 Dobrushin 态（即 $\\mu^{\\text{Dob}}_{\\Lambda_n,\\beta}$ 的子列极限）在这些 $(d,\\beta)$ 下均非平移不变（translation invariant），故 $d\\geq 4$ 时不存在粗化转变。

Abstract: Consider the Ising measure $μ_{Λ_n,β}^{\text{Dob}}$ on $Λ_n:=([-n,n]^{d-1}\times[-n,n-1]) \cap \mathbb{Z}^d$ at inverse temperature $β$ with all plus boundary condition at heights $z \geq 0$ and all minus boundary condition at heights $z<0$. Let $β_c(d)$ be the critical inverse temperature. For every $d \geq 4$ and $β>β_c(d)$, we prove that $μ_{Λ_n,β}^{\text{Dob}}(σ_0)$ has a positive lower bound independent of $n$. As a consequence, any Dobrushin state (i.e., a subsequential limit of $μ_{Λ_n,β}^{\text{Dob}}$) is not translation invariant for those $(d,β)$, and thus there is no roughening transition when $d \geq 4$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We highlight strange similarities between the Anderson model with Cauchy potential with energy-disorder parameters $(E,λ)$ and the $H^{2|2}$ supersymmetric sigma field with initial non local conductances proportional to $W_{x,y}=λ^2\left \vert \left (-Δ-E+ iλ)\right)_{x,y}^{-1}\right\vert^2$ where $Δ$ is the discrete Laplace operator. More precisely, looking at the Brownian interpolation of both, we show that they follow very similar equations. Motivated by that we investigate the different phases of the $H^{2|2}$-model on $\mathbb{Z}^d$ with conductances $rW$ for some $r>0$ and show that whatever $r>0$ the $H^{2|2}$-model displays localized and delocalized phases which are coherent with what is expected or proved for the Cauchy potential with energy-disorder parameters $(E,λ)$. Finally, we propose a conjecture that compares key quantities of the two models and would imply existence of delocalized regime for the Anderson model with Cauchy potential in dimension $d\ge 3$.

</details>

Conclusion: 该一步式模块化方法因基于自组装而独立于器件制造，为将仿生纳米结构（biomimetic nanostructures）集成到稳健、高性能免疫检测中提供通用路径，推动生物传感超越终点测量（end-point measurements），迈向快速、连续的实时监测（real-time monitoring）。

Abstract: Sample-driven cross-reactivity remains a fundamental bottleneck for sensitive and selective biosensing in complex biological environments, particularly in microfluidic systems where high surface-to-volumes ratios amplify non-specific interactions. Here, we address this challenge with a one-step, biomimetic surface functionalization strategy based on engineered lipid-based nanocarriers that self-assemble within minutes into immunoassay-ready, antifouling interfaces that couple pegylated-lipid passivation with biorthogonal, site-specific probe conjugation. This approach enables rapid, tuneable, and reproducible surface functionalization, which suppresses sample-specific cross-reactivity even in undiluted serum, and supports direct, label-free digital biosensing assays directly in native biological environments. When integrated into an optofluidic platform combining digital in-line holography and microfluidics, this approach allows time-resolved quantification of tetraspanin-positive extracellular vesicle secretion dynamics from two-dimensional and three-dimensional lung cancer cell models using 50 μL of sample within 10 min, without isolation or purification. Because the presented surface functionalization proceeds via self-assembly rather than covalent surface modification, this one-step modular-approach is intrinsically decoupled from device fabrication and applies to both glass and PDMS, coating the entire microfluidic channel, thereby establishing a general route for integrating biomimetic nanostructures into robust, high-performance immunoassays, advancing biosensing beyond end-point measurements, and towards rapid and continuous real-time monitoring.

</details>

### [46] [Computing the Wave: Bridging Gravitational Wave and High-Energy-Physics software (with modular ROOT extensions)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27026)

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该工作将高能物理（High-Energy Physics, HEP）与引力波（Gravitational Wave, GW）天文学共用的软件需求整合为CERN ROOT框架的模块化扩展库RIOPlus，涵盖数据格式解析、联邦数据访问、信号处理与张量运算等可选动态加载组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HEP与GW天文学虽科学目标不同，却面临高度相似的计算挑战，并可通过共享软件相互促进；此前的ACAT 2024原型工作需进一步整合与固化。

Result: 报告各扩展的设计，说明其面向瞬态噪声（glitch）检测与分类的应用设想，并更新KFR与FFTW的快速傅里叶变换（FFT）性能基准对比。

Conclusion: 展示了这些扩展所支撑的以ROOT为中心的端到端工作流及其现状，并展望向列式RNTuple存储的发展方向。

Abstract: High-Energy Physics (HEP) and Gravitational Wave (GW) astronomy pursue different scientific goals, yet they face strikingly similar computing challenges and can enrich one another through shared software. Building on the prototype presented at ACAT 2024, we report on the consolidation of that work into a single, modular extension of the CERN ROOT data-analysis framework, freely distributed under RIOPlus. The library is now organised as a set of optional, dynamically loadable extensions covering configuration and file-format parsing (including the Gravitational Wave Frame (GWF) format and HDF5), remote and federated data access (OSDF/Pelican, S3, Kafka) with token-based credentials, an enhanced dynamic-display manager, an advanced signal-processing layer built on the KFR library, and a complex-capable tensor layer backed by LibTorch with ONNX interoperability. We summarise the design of each extension, outline the intended application to transient-noise (glitch) detection and classification, and update the FFT performance benchmark comparing KFR with FFTW. We close with the end-to-end ROOT-centred workflow these extensions enable, its current status, and an outlook toward column-wise RNTuple storage.

</details>

Main category: nlin.PS

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究对 MTM 中非零波背景的谱稳定性分类及代数衰减孤立波的存在性缺乏精确刻画，本文旨在补足这一空白。

Method: 在实验室坐标（laboratory coordinates）下分析非零波背景的谱稳定性（spectral stability），区分调制不稳定性（modulational instability）、边带不稳定性（side-band instability）与全带不稳定性（full-band instability），并构造代数暗孤子（algebraic dark soliton）解。

Conclusion: MTM 中代数暗孤子的存在性与背景稳定性类型紧密相关，且孤子数目被精确确定为两个，为后续相关可积模型研究提供依据。

Abstract: We address the existence of algebraically decaying solitary waves on the nonzero wave background in the massive Thirring model (MTM) in laboratory coordinates. We find the precise condition of the spectral stability and instability of the nonzero wave background. In the case of instability, we distinguish modulational, side-band, and full-band instabilities and show that the rogue wave exists if and only if the nonzero wave background is modulationally unstable. For the other unstable cases and in the case of stability, we show that there exist exactly two algebraic dark solitons which correspond to the end points in the admissible range of the wave speed for the dark soliton propagation.

</details>

Central to LISA data analysis is time-delay interferometry (TDI), which reduces otherwise overwhelming laser frequency noise. In previous work, we explored coronagraphic TDI, a sky-dependent null-stream channel which cancels the GW signal of a point-like source when pointed at its sky position. Here, we address three challenges which arise when analyzing realistic LISA data: (i) spacecraft motion; (ii) signal superposition; (iii) instrumental noise. This is confirmed by the analysis of a simulation containing a massive black hole binary and 49 verification GBs. Finally, we introduce a statistical test based on the analytic noise model, which provides well-calibrated confidence regions for the sky position of the source. Applying it to GBs in the presence of instrumental noise, we find that the geometric response alone only weakly constrains the sky position: even at signal-to-noise ratios of order 10^4, 90% confidence regions span ~10^3 deg^2. Abstract: Simultaneous global-fit inference of the numerous sources observed by the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) depends on accurate modeling of its stochastic components: instrumental noise, the Galactic foreground, and astrophysical or stochastic gravitational wave backgrounds (SGWB). Realistic features such as non-Gaussian transients and data gaps challenge traditional likelihood-based pipelines, creating an opportunity for simulation-based inference (SBI) to augment traditional analyses. We find flow matching slightly better calibrated than diffusion in simpler regimes and observe modest gains from physics-aware template inputs.   <summary>Details</summary>
