Conclusion: 当 $x$ 紧致时，伽利略推进（Galilean boost）仍将解映射为解，但不存在与之对应的全局 Noether 荷（global Noether charge），表明对称性存在并不意味着相应守恒荷在紧化域上良定义。

Abstract: Conserved quantities of nonlinear dispersive wave equations are typically derived on the whole plane, whereas numerical simulations evaluate them on a torus. The outcome is a three-valued classification: intrinsic, chart-dependent, or defective. Only the two first moments, whose characteristics carry an undifferentiated coordinate weight, are affected. A moment can become defective only if its weighted direction is compact and the mass flux crosses the seam of the chosen fundamental domain; for the Zakharov--Kuznetsov equation this happens exactly for the longitudinal moment when $x$ is compact. The status of each moment depends only on the boundary condition along its weighted direction. When $x$ is compact, the Galilean boost still maps solutions to solutions, but no global Noether charge corresponds to it.

Motivation: 在轴对称各向异性（axisymmetric anisotropic）系统中，偏振图像的旋转对称性与材料对称性并不等价，其来源与丧失机制缺乏统一框架，因此需要明确何种约束决定图像的方位角阶次。

Method: 基于协变性（covariance）论证场被限制在方位角阶次 m=0,±2；再引入逐点幺正性（pointwise unitarity）与衍射（diffraction）效应，并结合液晶 toron 的全息琼斯场及行进波模型（marching-wave model）与局域模型的对比分析。

Result: 协变性仅保证二重（twofold）而非四重对称，逐点幺正性额外带来四重（fourfold）对称，而衍射可破坏之；传播使二重—四重平衡发生移动，行进波模型定性重现了局域模型遗漏的二重形貌。

Conclusion: 偏振成像中对称性随传播而改变，可归纳为层级结构（symmetry hierarchy），据此可解释同一指向矢（director）下不同成像结果。

Abstract: A polarization image can gain or lose rotational symmetry with no change in material symmetry. In axisymmetric anisotropic systems, covariance restricts the field to azimuthal orders $m=0,\pm2$, guaranteeing twofold but not fourfold symmetry; pointwise unitarity adds fourfold symmetry, which diffraction can destroy. Holographic Jones fields of liquid-crystal torons show propagation shifts the twofold--fourfold balance; for the same director, a marching-wave model qualitatively reproduces the twofold morphology missed by a local model. This establishes a symmetry hierarchy for polarization imaging.

</details>

### [65] [Topological phases protected by half-chiral symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26693)
*Chen-Shen Lee,Itamar Kimchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文提出由“半手征对称性（half-chiral symmetry）”保护的新拓扑相：手征对称性仅在单一子晶格上成立，并为此建立了新的拓扑保护框架与实空间体拓扑不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手征（子晶格）对称性（chiral/sublattice symmetry）要求同一子晶格内 A-A、B-B 跃迁被禁止，从而保护拓扑相；但在子晶格失衡（如 graphene-hBN）或局域轨道（如 zincblende）体系中该条件可能仅在单个子晶格上满足，缺乏相应的拓扑保护理论框架。

Method: 将半手征对称性刻画为子晶格投影哈密顿量上的代数约束（如 $PHP=0$），而非群论描述的对称算符；据此发展新的理论框架，构造实空间体拓扑不变量并分析对应的拓扑边界模式；在二维中构建半手征 Kekule 蜂窝模型（half-chiral Kekule honeycomb model）。

Result: 所发展的框架给出了实空间体拓扑不变量，并预言钉扎于零能的拓扑边界模式（topological boundary modes）；在半手征 Kekule 蜂窝模型中，其非常规高阶拓扑（higher-order topology）保护单个角上的单一零能模式。

Conclusion: 半手征对称性构成一类不同于群论对称性的代数约束型保护机制，可支撑新的拓扑相与高阶拓扑现象，为子晶格失衡或局域轨道体系的拓扑分类提供了新框架。

Abstract: Chiral (or sublattice) symmetry describes bipartite systems such as the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain where same-sublattice A-A and B-B hoppings are forbidden, enabling the protection of topological phases. Here we identify new topological phases protected by half-chiral symmetry wherein chiral symmetry is preserved on only one sublattice, as may occur with sublattice imbalance (e.g. graphene-hBN) or localized orbitals (e.g. zincblende). Unlike conventional symmetry operators described by group theory, half-chiral symmetry is an algebraic constraint on the sublattice-projected Hamiltonian such as $PHP=0$, requiring a new framework for topological protection. We develop such a framework, identifying real-space bulk topological invariants and corresponding topological boundary modes that stick to zero energy. In two dimensions we construct a half-chiral Kekule honeycomb model and show that its unusual higher-order topology protects a single zero-energy mode at a single corner.

</details>


### [66] [Free-Space Excitation of Frozen Waves and Topological Edge States in Graphene at a Time Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26714)
*Shivaksh Rawat,Samyobrata Mukherjee,Jeffery W. Allen,Monica S. Allen,Gennady Shvets*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文利用自洽耦合电磁仿真的石墨烯电子输运模型，研究超快光激发所形成的时间界面（temporal interface）如何激发等离激元模式，发现除动态传播的等离激元外还会激发由横向漂移电子维持的静态空间变化电磁模式，并提出一种将自由空间光直接耦合到石墨烯磁反转边界拓扑边缘态的新技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变二维材料（如超快光激发下的石墨烯）被视为构建电磁波时间界面的有前景平台，但光激发产生的热电子与非热电子群体热化过程如何影响等离激元激发尚需研究，且拓扑边缘态通常依赖近场激发手段。

Method: 采用与电磁仿真自洽耦合（self-consistently coupled）的石墨烯电子输运模型（electron-transport model），模拟超快光激发所形成的时间界面，考察等离激元模式的激发过程。

Result: 除动态传播的等离激元模式（dynamic, propagating plasmonic modes）外，时间界面还在相互作用区域内激发由横向漂移电子（streaming transverse electrons）维持的静态、空间变化的电磁模式；同时实现了自由空间光向石墨烯磁反转边界（magnetic-inversion boundary）上高度局域拓扑边缘态（topological edge states）的直接耦合。

Conclusion: 时间界面可激发传播等离激元与静态空间变化电磁模式两类响应；所提出的直接耦合自由空间光至拓扑边缘态的技术免除了近场激发（near-field excitation）的需求，为时变二维材料中的电磁调控与拓扑光子学应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: Time-varying two-dimensional materials, such as graphene under ultrafast photoexcitation, offer a promising platform for engineering temporal interfaces for electromagnetic waves. Rapidly promoting valence electrons to the conduction band generates a high-energy, non-thermal hot-electron population that subsequently thermalizes with the pre-existing thermal electron population. We study the excitation of plasmonic modes in graphene by the time interface created by ultra-fast photoexcitation using a graphene electron-transport model that is self-consistently coupled to electromagnetic simulations. We find that in addition to dynamic, propagating plasmonic modes, the time interfaces result in the excitation of static, spatially varying electromagnetic modes in the interaction region sustained by streaming transverse electrons. We also demonstrate a novel technique for coupling free-space light directly to highly localized topological edge states at the magnetic-inversion boundary on graphene, eliminating the need for near-field excitation techniques.

</details>


### [67] [Gap-label bounds and optical reconstruction of Landau-subband quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27321)
*Özgür Kelekçi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文利用嵌套子丛的第二基本形式（second fundamental forms）分解量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor），推导出一个在被占据陈数（Chern number）为零时仍非零的磁能隙标记（magnetic gap-label）下界，并给出从圆偏振吸收重构量子权重与二色性的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有磁能隙标记下界在被占据陈数（Chern number）为零时退化为零，无法对内部量子权重（internal quantum weight）提供有效约束，因此需要构造在该情形下依然非平庸的下界。

Method: 借助嵌套子丛（nested subbundles）的第二基本形式（second fundamental forms）分解量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor），分离均匀母曲率（uniform parent curvature）施加的约束；并利用精确的圆偶极关系（circular-dipole relations），从圆偏振吸收重构低频导向中心权重（guiding-centre weight）与有符号二色性（signed dichroism）。

Result: 超过下界的过剩量子权重等于较弱圆跃迁权重（weaker circular transition weight）的两倍；对带周期标量势的非相互作用抛物带，朗道能级混合（Landau-level mixing）保持积分相对曲率不变且已占据直接能隙保持打开，但未修正的亚回旋吸收（sub-cyclotron absorption）未必满足投影下界；重构可去除一阶轨道极化贡献，并在弱耦合下以二次方式界定被忽略的远端跃迁权重。

Conclusion: 对孤立最低朗道能级（lowest-Landau-level）衍生簇的绝缘填充，得到重构量子权重的有限窗口下界，并界定了重构二色性与积分相对曲率之间的偏差。

Abstract: We derive a magnetic gap-label lower bound on the internal quantum weight of Landau subbands that remains nonzero even when the occupied Chern number vanishes. Using the second fundamental forms of nested subbundles to decompose the quantum geometric tensor, we isolate the constraint imposed by the uniform parent curvature and show that the excess quantum weight above the bound equals twice the weaker circular transition weight. For a noninteracting parabolic band with a periodic scalar potential, Landau-level mixing preserves the integrated relative curvature while the occupied direct gap remains open. Nevertheless, uncorrected sub-cyclotron absorption need not satisfy the projected bound. Using exact circular-dipole relations, we reconstruct the low-frequency guiding-centre weight and signed dichroism from circularly polarized absorption. This reconstruction removes the first-order orbit-polarization contribution. For an insulating filling of an isolated lowest-Landau-level-derived cluster, a potential-gradient estimate bounds the omitted remote-transition weight quadratically at weak coupling. This yields a finite-window lower bound on reconstructed quantum weight and bounds the deviation of reconstructed dichroism from the integrated relative curvature.

</details>


### [68] [Parametric excitation of nano-scale spin waves with controlled phase by an open-resonator-type transducer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26932)
*Roman Verba,Vladyslav Borynskyi,Vasil Tiberkevich,Andrei Slavin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究提出以开放谐振器形式的压控磁各向异性（VCMA）换能器实现相位受控的参量自旋波激发，使激发波仅存在两种允许相位状态，并降低参量激发阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参量激发虽能突破换能器有限宽度对波长的限制，但所激发的传播波相位任意，难以用于信号处理；研究旨在解决相位不可控问题。

Method: 采用具有边界部分反射控制的开放谐振器（open resonator）结构的VCMA换能器，并结合微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations）分析激发行为。

Result: 激发自旋波仅存在两种允许相位状态，由参量泵浦（parametric pumping）相位与换能器边界反射共同控制；边界反射同时降低了参量激发阈值；微磁学模拟显示可用静态电压调控磁振子（magnons）的相位分布。

Conclusion: 边界部分反射的开放谐振器VCMA换能器实现了相位可控的参量自旋波激发，有望用于信号处理及概率计算（probabilistic computing）等领域。

Abstract: Parametric excitation is one of the most effective methods for the excitation of nano-scale spin waves (SW) (or magnons) in magnetic films and nano-waveguides, as this method removes restrictions on the excited wave wavelength imposed by a finite width of the excitation transducer. Especially efficient parametric excitation of SW is achieved if the SW excitation is based on the effect of voltage-controlled magnetic anisotropy (VCMA). However, in a traditional parametric excitation process, the excited propagating waves have an arbitrary phase, which prevents the use of such waves in signal processing applications. Here, we demonstrate that the use of an VCMA transducer in the form of an open resonator with controlled partial reflections of waves at the boundaries allows one to excite SW having only two allowed phase states, controlled by the phase of the parametric pumping and reflections at the transducer boundaries. These reflections, also, lead to a reduced value of the parametric excitation threshold. In addition, micromagnetic simulations reveal the possibility to tune the phase distribution of the excited magnons using static voltage, which could be useful for applications in probabilistic computing.

</details>


### [69] [Pure Orbitronics: Hall Responses and Torques by Orbital Exchange in Spinless Transition-Metal Dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27391)
*Mahmoud Zeer,Dongwook Go,Yuriy Mokrousov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文以单层H相MoS₂为原型，引入仅作用于轨道空间、破坏时间反演的类塞曼轨道交换场，证明其可在无自旋磁性条件下产生显著本征反常霍尔电导，并对轨道霍尔响应与电流诱导轨道力矩进行系统刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道交换场（orbital exchange field）作为一种通过轨道自由度调控电子与磁现象的新机制受到关注，其直接耦合轨道角动量，可源于纯轨道相互作用或经由手性激发（如声子、磁振子）动态产生，但由此引发的输运与力矩响应尚缺乏研究。

Method: 采用单层H相MoS₂作为过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenide）的代表体系，引入打破轨道空间时间反演对称性、而自旋自由度保持不活跃的类塞曼轨道交换场（Zeeman-like orbital exchange field），计算其输运与力矩响应。

Result: 该微扰在无自旋磁性与自旋—轨道耦合驱动机制的情况下即可诱导可观的本征反常霍尔电导（intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity）；轨道霍尔响应（orbital Hall response）在弱场区保持稳健，并在强交换场下因能带显著重构而发生实质性改变；同时电流可在轨道磁化上产生可观的电流诱导轨道力矩（current-induced orbital torque）。

Conclusion: 轨道交换场可成为通向纯轨道物理驱动的霍尔输运与非平衡力矩现象的有效途径，为轨道电子学（orbitronics）及轨道工程化量子功能提供新视角。

Abstract: Recent studies have identified orbital exchange fields as a novel mechanism for controlling electronic and magnetic phenomena through the orbital degree of freedom. In contrast to conventional spin exchange fields, orbital exchange fields couple directly to orbital angular momentum and can emerge from purely orbital interactions or dynamically through couplings to chiral excitations such as phonons and magnons. Motivated by these developments, we investigate the transport and torque responses generated by orbital exchange fields in two-dimensional materials. Using monolayer H-phase MoS$_2$ as a prototypical transition-metal dichalcogenide, we introduce a Zeeman-like orbital exchange field that breaks time-reversal symmetry in orbital space while leaving the spin degree of freedom inactive. We demonstrate that this perturbation induces a sizable intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity even in the absence of spin magnetism and spin--orbit coupling driven mechanisms. Simultaneously, the orbital Hall response remains robust in the weak-field regime and undergoes substantial reconstruction at larger exchange strengths due to pronounced band modifications. In addition, we show that currents can generate sizable current-induced orbital torques on the orbital magnetization. Our results establish orbital exchange fields as an effective route toward Hall transport and nonequilibrium torque phenomena driven purely by orbital physics, opening new perspectives for orbitronics and orbitally engineered quantum functionalities.

</details>


### [70] [A Negative-U Double Quantum Dot in a LaAlO3/SrTiO3 Membrane on Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27428)
*Giulia Meucci,Thies Jansen,Pinelopi O. Konstantinopoulou,Dags Olšteins,Damon J. Carrad,Carina Babu Maliakkal,Jordi Arbiol,Nicolas Bergeal,Manuel Bibes,Roberta Citro,Emiliano Di Gennaro,Alexei Kalaboukhov,Nicola Manca,Carmine A. Perroni,Marco Salluzzo,Paweł Wójcik,Felix Trier,Nini Pryds,Fabio Miletto Granozio,Alessia Sambri,Thomas S. Jespersen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作将独立式LaAlO3/SrTiO3（LAO/STO）薄膜转移至硅衬底，利用双栅极局域静电调控，在界面电子系统中实现吸引型在位相互作用（U < 0）下的氧化物双量子点（DQD），并通过输运谱与两格点Hubbard模型模拟加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立式氧化物薄膜（freestanding oxide membranes）为将氧化物功能集成到硅技术中提供了新途径，核心问题是局域栅极能否在混合介观量子器件（hybrid mesoscopic quantum devices）所需尺度上调控转移薄膜的关联电子态。

Method: 在转移至硅的LAO/STO薄膜上制备双栅极收缩结构（dual-gated constriction），于低温下进行输运测量（charge stability diagram与谱学分析），并用吸引型两格点Hubbard模型（attractive two-site Hubbard model）对输运谱进行模拟以提取DQD参数。

Result: 低温柔性输运显示特征性蜂窝状电荷稳定性图（honeycomb charge stability diagram）；谱学表明低磁场下存在配对电子基态，超过配对场后奇占据态可被访问。模拟重现了实验输运谱，并提取出点内与点间相互作用、点间隧穿耦合及自旋轨道辅助隧穿耦合等DQD参数。

Conclusion: 研究确立氧化物薄膜可作为双量子点的宿主材料，并将耦合量子点体系拓展至吸引型在位相互作用（U < 0）区域，为氧化物基介观量子器件提供依据。

Abstract: The development of freestanding oxide membranes creates new opportunities to integrate oxide functionalities with silicon technology. A central question is whether local gates can control the correlated electronic states of transferred membranes at the scale required for hybrid mesoscopic quantum devices. Here, we demonstrate local electrostatic control of freestanding LaAlO3/SrTiO3 (LAO/STO) membranes transferred onto silicon. In a dual-gated constriction in the interfacial electron system, we observe an oxide-based double quantum dot (DQD) in the regime of attractive on-site interactions (U < 0). Low-temperature transport reveals the characteristic honeycomb charge stability diagram, and spectroscopy identifies a paired-electron ground state at low magnetic fields, with odd-occupancy states becoming accessible above the pairing field. The observed transport spectra are reproduced by simulations within an attractive two site Hubbard model, and we extract the relevant DQD parameters, including intradot and interdot interactions, interdot tunnel coupling, and spin-orbit-assisted tunnel coupling. These findings establish oxide membranes as hosts for DQDs and extend coupled quantum dot systems into the regime of attractive on-site interactions.

</details>


### [71] [Minimal chiral $p$-wave superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27555)
*Fabian Hassler,Michael Wimmer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出由量子点方阵与Andreev点耦合构成的最小手性p波超导体方案，利用隧穿势垒中的Rashba自旋轨道耦合提供p波缠绕、Zeeman场使量子点无自旋，在甜点处实现受限边缘态与近零劈裂的Majorana零模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性p波超导体（chiral p-wave superconductor）兼具涡旋束缚Majorana零模（Majorana zero mode）与手性Majorana边缘模，是最具吸引力的拓扑相之一，但迄今尚无材料被无歧义地确认为此类超导体，亟需可实现的候选平台。

Method: 采用将Kitaev链（Kitaev chain）自下而上推广至二维的思路，构建由量子点（quantum dot）方阵经Andreev点耦合的最小模型；隧穿势垒中的Rashba自旋轨道耦合（Rashba spin-orbit coupling）提供p波缠绕，Zeeman场使量子点有效无自旋。

Result: 在甜点（sweet spot）条件下，边缘态被限制在最外排，因而在仅3×3与5×5的阵列中即出现手性热导（chiral thermal conductance）以及劈裂低于百分之一的涡旋束缚Majorana零模。

Conclusion: 该方案规模需求小、结构极简，且可基于现有InAs与Ge量子点阵列实现，为手性p波超导与Majorana物理提供了实验可行的平台。

Abstract: Chiral $p$-wave superconductors are among the most intriguing topological phases: their vortices bind Majorana zero modes and their edges carry chiral Majorana modes, yet no material has been unambiguously identified as one. Here, we carry the bottom-up approach of Kitaev chains to two dimensions, proposing a minimal chiral $p$-wave superconductor from a square array of quantum dots coupled via Andreev dots. Rashba spin-orbit coupling in the tunnel barriers provides the $p$-wave winding, and a Zeeman field renders the dots spinless. At a sweet spot, the edge states are confined to the outermost row, so that a chiral thermal conductance and a vortex-bound Majorana zero mode with sub-percent splitting emerge already in $3\times3$ and $5\times5$ arrays. Our setup is realizable with existing InAs and Ge quantum-dot arrays.

</details>


### [72] [Modeling the contact area and intersurface forces between Pb nanoparticles and suspended graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27654)
*Mykola Prodanov,Anton Samilyk,Oleksii Khomenko,Antonina Biesiedina*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过大规模原子尺度模拟（atomistic simulations）研究零外载下物理吸附（physisorption）于悬浮石墨烯（suspended graphene）上的Pb纳米颗粒的接触力学，发现凝固态纳米颗粒的法向界面力分布（normal intersurface force distributions）在横向尺寸约10 nm以上具有尺寸无关性，并据此给出相对接触面积（relative contact area）的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解Pb纳米颗粒在悬浮石墨烯上自组装与固化过程中接触面积动力学与界面力分布随尺寸的标度行为，并为相对接触面积的定量估计建立解析近似模型。

Method: 采用大规模原子尺度模拟，考察零外载下纳米颗粒相对接触面积的动力学、自液相自组装（self-assembly）期间作用于颗粒的界面原子力量值分布，以及固化后颗粒这些量的尺寸标度；并对法向力分布的排斥与吸引部分进行函数形式拟合。

Result: 当线性横向尺寸大于约10 nm（表面积-体积比低于约1 nm^-1）时，力分布函数形式与尺寸无关；排斥尾部一阶近似为指数衰减（衰减指数约-5 nN^-1），截断幂律（cutoff power law）拟合更精确；吸引部分多数尺寸下最佳拟合为指数修正高斯（exponentially modified Gaussian）。时间平均相对接触面积在数nm直径颗粒中波动显著，较大颗粒收敛至约0.44，且由拟合导出的解析表达式与分子动力学（MD）时间平均值在统计误差内一致。

Conclusion: 该模型是发展Pb纳米颗粒/悬浮石墨烯体系相对接触面积定量解析近似的第一步。

Abstract: We perform large-scale atomistic simulations to explore the contact mechanics of Pb nanoparticles (NPs) physisorbed on a suspended graphene sheet at zero external load. The study considers the kinetics of the NPs' relative contact area and the distributions of intersurface atomic force magnitudes acting on the NP during self-assembly from the liquid phase, and the size scaling of these quantities for the solidified NPs. The functional forms of both the repulsive and attractive parts of the normal intersurface force distributions for solidified NPs are size-independent for nanoislands with a linear lateral size larger than about 10 nm (or a surface area-to-volume ratio less than about 1 nm$^{-1}$). To first order, the repulsive tail decays exponentially, while the cutoff power law fits more precisely, especially for the high-probability repulsive forces. In both cases, the decay exponent is around -5 nN$^{-1}$. The exponentially modified Gaussian is the best fit for the attractive part for most NP sizes. The time-averaged relative contact area calculated directly in the simulations shows considerable fluctuations for NPs a few nm in diameter, while converging to approximately 0.44 for larger NPs. Based on fitting the normal-force distributions, we obtain analytical expressions for the relative contact area. Ensemble-averaged relative contact areas for NPs larger than around 10 nm, calculated using the analytical expressions, match the time-averaged MD values within statistical error. Our model is the first step towards developing analytical approximations for quantitative estimates of the relative contact area of Pb NPs adsorbed on suspended graphene.

</details>


### [73] [General Transport Signatures of Odd-Parity Magnets: Charge--Spin Selection Rules and Temporal--Spectral Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27763)
*Junxi Yu,Pu Zhang,Yichen Liu,Yongpan Li,Cheng-Cheng Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出，具有幺正自旋翻转反演对称性 U=[C2⊥‖P] 的奇宇称磁体（odd-parity magnets）中，"时间—频谱的电荷—自旋分离"可作为跨全部自旋劈裂类别的普适输运判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶宇称交错磁体（altermagnets）已通过晶体自旋霍尔效应（crystal spin Hall effect）实现电荷与自旋电流的空间分离，而奇宇称磁体的对应输运特征尚未确立，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于幺正自旋翻转反演对称性 U=[C2⊥‖P] 的对称性分析，推导电荷与自旋响应的奇偶阶选择定则，并对全部线性与二阶输运机制进行验证；在最小奇宇称模型、Floquet 工程共线 MnPS3 与平衡态非共线 VCl2 体系中演示谐波分离。

Result: 电荷响应仅允许奇阶、z 极化自旋响应仅允许偶阶；半周期变号的交流驱动下，电荷与自旋电流具相反半周期宇称，频谱分别只含奇次与偶次谐波，从而实现无直流电荷对应的直流自旋整流（dc spin rectification）。此外，反幺正 Tτ 虽强制奇宇称能带，却反转贝里曲率（Berry curvature）与贝里曲率偶极响应的电荷—自旋选择，可作为区分两类保护对称性的输运指纹。

Conclusion: 电荷—自旋的时间与频谱分离是奇宇称磁体的普适输运特征，且不依赖微观机制与自旋守恒假设，为区分保护对称性及设计自旋整流器件提供了依据。

Abstract: While even-parity altermagnets are known for spatially separating charge and spin currents via the crystal spin Hall effect, the defining transport counterpart for odd-parity magnets remains to be established. Here, we establish temporal and spectral charge--spin separation as a general transport signature across all spin-splitting classes of collinear and noncollinear odd-parity magnets with unitary spin-flip inversion $\mathcal U=[C_2^\perp\Vert P]$. This symmetry restricts charge responses to odd orders and $z$-polarized spin responses to even orders, independent of microscopic mechanism and without requiring spin conservation. For ac drives that reverse sign after half a period, charge and spin currents exhibit opposite half-period parities, and their spectra contain only odd charge and even spin harmonics. This allows dc spin rectification without a dc charge counterpart, even in longitudinal transport and beyond weak fields. We show that although antiunitary $\mathcal Tτ$ enforces odd-parity bands, it reverses the charge--spin selection of Berry-curvature and Berry-curvature-dipole responses, providing a direct transport fingerprint distinguishing the two protecting symmetries. We verify the selection rules for all linear and second-order transport mechanisms and demonstrate harmonic separation in a minimal odd-parity model, representative Floquet-engineered collinear MnPS$_3$, and the equilibrium noncollinear VCl$_2$ family.

</details>


### [74] [Thermo-electric Response Bound on Fluctuations in Coherent Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27884)
*Gianmichele Blasi,Rosa López,Mykhailo Moskalets,Sungguen Ryu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在 Floquet-Nambu 散射形式下，借助 Cramér-Rao 型不等式，建立了相干电子输运中电流噪声的可达下界，该下界以对热库温度与化学势的响应表示，并适用于静态或周期驱动下的正常端及混合超导体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干电子输运中的电流噪声通常缺乏可由实验直接获取的普适下界；既有热力学不确定性关系（thermodynamic uncertainty relations）需依赖熵产生或动力学活度，且在某些情形约束不够强。本文旨在给出不依赖这些不可及量的可达噪声下界。

Method: 在 Floquet-Nambu 散射形式（Floquet-Nambu scattering formalism）框架下，采用 Cramér-Rao 型不等式（Cramér-Rao-type inequality）推导下界，覆盖正常端中电荷流与热流的任意线性组合，并涵盖静态与周期驱动、以及混合超导体系。

Result: 该下界在实验相关区间内是紧的（tight），典型情形包括静态或振荡电压下的量子点接触（quantum point contacts）与正常-超导结（normal-superconductor junctions），且往往比热力学不确定性关系给出更强约束。

Conclusion: 该界可由实验可测的响应系数（response coefficients）直接评估，无需推断熵产生（entropy production）或动力学活度，从而为纳米尺度热学与量子器件的精度提供有用约束。

Abstract: We establish an accessible lower bound on current noise in coherent electron transport, expressed in terms of responses to reservoir temperatures and chemical potentials. Derived within the Floquet-Nambu scattering formalism using a Cramér-Rao-type inequality, it holds for arbitrary linear combinations of charge and heat currents in normal terminals, including those of hybrid superconducting systems, under static or periodic driving. The bound is tight in experimentally relevant regimes, notably in quantum point contacts with static or oscillating voltages and normal-superconductor junctions, often providing stronger constraints than thermodynamic uncertainty relations. It can be evaluated via experimentally accessible response coefficients, without inferring entropy production or dynamical activity, and provides useful constraints on the precision of nanoscale thermal and quantum devices.

</details>


### [75] [Structure and relaxation of long-lived odd-parity modes in two-dimensional Fermi liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27980)
*Stan Boeve,Johannes Hofmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文解决了二维费米液体奇数宇称费米面形变弛豫率理论预测（~m^4 ln m）与精确对角化结果不符的问题，指出热修饰（thermal dressing）抑制共线散射，残余弛豫由大角度散射主导，须重求和 ln m/ln(T_F/T) 修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维费米液体中奇数宇称费米面形变以 ~T^4 异常缓慢弛豫，但主导对数预测 ~m^4 ln m 超出精确对角化结果且未观测到 ln m 标度，需澄清这一差异的物理起源。

Method: 对一般规则各向同性势，在 T/T_F 领头阶（含 ln(T_F/T) 项之外的非对数项）计算二维费米液体碰撞积分（collision integral），并以精确对角化（exact diagonalization）结果对常数相互作用情形进行基准检验。

Result: 奇数模式局域温度调制（即热修饰）抑制共线散射（collinear scattering），残余弛豫由大角度散射设定；大角度过程给出 ~ln m/ln(T_F/T) 量级修正，需重求和（resummation）。与精确对角化在 m^2 T/T_F ≲ 0.4 范围内一致，并给出自旋极化偶极气体（spin-polarized dipolar gas）最慢奇数模式弛豫率的闭式结果。

Conclusion: 热修饰效应解释了原 ln m 标度预测失效的原因，大角度散射的对数修正不可忽略且须重求和；所得结果在较大参数范围内与精确对角化吻合，并为偶极气体提供解析弛豫率表达式。

Abstract: In two-dimensional Fermi liquids, odd-parity deformations of the Fermi surface relax anomalously slowly at a rate $\sim T^4$, where $T$ is the temperature. However, the leading-logarithmic prediction for the magnitude of this rate $\sim m^4 \ln m$, where $m$ is an angular harmonic index, exceeds exact-diagonalization results and the $\ln m$ scaling is not observed. We resolve this discrepancy by evaluating the 2D Fermi-liquid collision integral for general regular isotropic potentials to leading order in $T/T_F$, including the nonlogarithmic term beyond the $\ln(T_F/T)$ term. We find that the local temperature modulation of the odd mode, which we call the thermal dressing, suppresses relaxation by collinear scattering, with residual relaxation set by large-angle scattering. Such large-angle processes give corrections of order $\ln m/\ln(T_F/T)$ that are not small and must be resummed. We benchmark our results against exact-diagonalization calculations for constant interactions and find agreement over a range $m^2 T/T_F \lesssim 0.4$, and we provide closed-form results for the slowest odd-mode relaxation rate of a spin-polarized dipolar gas.

</details>


### [76] [Thermopower overshoot from collinear scattering in two-dimensional Fermi liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27981)
*Johannes Hofmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 电子输运通常以弛豫时间（relaxation time）描述，但本文指出二维费米液体（two-dimensional Fermi liquid）的热电势（thermopower）实际探测的是费米面形变的局域温升结构，即“热缀饰”（thermal dressing）。该结构使热电势随波矢先超过流体力学极限值 $-s/(ne)$，再在弹道极限（ballistic limit）降至其一半，且在低温下呈 $1/\ln(T_F/T)$ 的对数温度依赖，构成一种由共线散射（collinear scattering）驱动的独立“热成像输运”（thermographic transport）区制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上用弛豫时间刻画电子输运，但该量只衡量费米面形变衰减的快慢，未必能反映形变本身的内部结构信息。论文旨在表明热电势可作为探测量，揭示弛豫时间框架之外所忽略的形变空间结构及其输运后果。

Method: 以二维费米液体为对象，从理论上分析费米面形变的局域温度变化（热缀饰）结构，研究热电势随波矢的演化及其在流体力学与弹道极限之间的行为，并考察共线散射对费米面能量再分布的作用及其随角向谐波阶数 $m$ 的变化。

Result: 随波矢增大，热电势先超过流体力学值 $-s/(ne)$ 出现过冲（overshoot），随后在弹道极限降至该值的一半。低温下，该过冲量不遵循费米液体预期的线性温度（linear-in-temperature）标度，而在最优波矢处仅按 $1/\ln(T_F/T)$ 对数式下降。过冲源于共线散射引起的能量再分布，其强度随形变的角向谐波阶数 $m$ 增大。

Conclusion: 与电荷电导（charge conductivity）中的层析（tomographic）效应不同，该热电势过冲不依赖准粒子寿命（quasiparticle lifetime）的奇偶效应，因而定义一个独立的热成像输运区制。该过冲在现实无序（disorder）下依然存在，并预期可在高迁移率样品的热电偶（thermocouple）中观测到。

Abstract: Electron transport is typically described in terms of relaxation times, which measure how quickly Fermi-surface deformations decay. We show here that the thermopower of a two-dimensional Fermi liquid instead probes the structure of these deformations in the form of their local temperature change, which we call the thermal dressing. This structure leads to an overshoot of the thermopower over its hydrodynamic value $- s/(ne)$ with increasing wave vector, before it falls to half that value in the ballistic limit. At low temperatures, the excess thermopower does not have the linear-in-temperature scaling expected of a Fermi liquid, but decreases only logarithmically as $1/\ln(T_F/T)$ at optimal wave vector. The overshoot is caused by collinear scattering, which redistributes energy over the Fermi surface and induces the thermal dressing, the strength of which grows with the angular harmonic index $m$ of the deformation. In contrast to tomographic effects in the charge conductivity, this overshoot does not rely on an odd-even effect in quasiparticle lifetimes and defines a distinct thermographic transport regime. The thermopower overshoot survives realistic disorder and should be detectable in a thermocouple with high-mobility samples.

</details>

### [77] [Calabi-Yau at 50: mathematical discovery in the age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26974)
*Yang-Hui He*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 以丘成桐证明卡拉比猜想（Calabi conjecture）五十周年为切入点，回顾卡拉比-丘流形（Calabi-Yau manifolds）数据集如何自1980年代起联结数学家与物理学家，并在2017年后引入机器学习与人工智能，梳理近十年“AI辅助数学（AI-assisted mathematics）”的发展历程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借卡拉比猜想证明五十周年之机，追溯卡拉比-丘流形数据集所促成的数学、物理与人工智能跨学科交汇，并对该领域的发展作出历史性评述。

Method: 采用历史叙述（historical account）的方法，以卡拉比-丘流形数据集为线索，按时间顺序梳理1980年代以来数学与物理的互动，以及2017年后机器学习与AI的介入。

Result: 摘要未给出具体研究结果，仅概述了“AI辅助数学”领域近十年的成长，并指出2026年或为自欧几里得（Euclid）以来数学史上最具戏剧性的一年。

Conclusion: 卡拉比-丘流形数据集构成数学、物理与AI长期交叉的纽带；作者认为该趋势在2026年达到高峰，并将其定位为数学史上的重要节点。

Abstract: Using the golden jubilee of Yau's proof of the Calabi conjecture as a spring-board, we discuss how the dataset of Calabi-Yau manifolds brought together mathematicians and physicists since the 1980s, and then further brought in the world of machine-learning and AI since 2017. We give a historical account on the growth of the field of ``AI-assisted mathematics'' in the last decade, and comment on what culminated in 2026 as perhaps the most dramatic year in the history of mathematics since Euclid.

</details>


### [78] [Projectivized ASEP-Bethe system and counting solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27706)
*Shinsuke Iwao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于射影簇（projective varieties）上标准相交理论（intersection theory）的更简方法，用于计数 ASEP-Bethe 拟设方程（ASEP-Bethe ansatz equations）的解的个数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的预印本（Brattain、Do、Saenz）虽已给出计数这类解的代数几何（algebro-geometric）方法，但其依赖深奥工具，需要高等代数几何与拓扑知识；本文旨在提供更简单、更易掌握的替代方案。

Method: 采用代数几何中的常规思路“将问题射影化并计数次数（projectivize the problem and count degrees）”：构造 ASEP-Bethe 方程的射影化版本，将解集实现为某射影簇的子概形（subscheme），并随跃迁速率参数 p 变化构造单参数解集族 S。

Result: 对含 n 个格点、m 个粒子的 ASEP，当 p 为一般值时，纤维（fiber）S|_p 含二项式系数 C(n,m) 个带重数的点，与 ASEP-Bethe 拟设方程的可容许 Bethe 根（admissible Bethe roots）的预期数目一致。

Conclusion: 该射影化加相交计数的框架以较低的技术门槛复现了既有计数结果，说明标准相交理论足以处理 ASEP-Bethe 方程的解计数问题。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of counting solutions to the ASEP-Bethe ansatz equations. In an earlier preprint, Brattain, Do, and Saenz presented an algebro-geometric method for counting the number of such solutions. As their method relies on sophisticated machinery, it requires advanced knowledge of algebraic geometry and topology.
  In this paper, we provide an alternative and simpler method for counting solutions, based on a standard intersection theory on projective varieties. We take a conventional approach in algebraic geometry: ``Projectivize the problem and count degrees.'' We introduce a projectivized version of the ASEP-Bethe equations, whose solution set is realized as a subscheme of a certain projective variety. Varying the hopping-rate parameter $p$, we give a one-parameter family $S$ of the solution sets. We show that, for the ASEP with $n$ sites and $m$ particles, the fiber $S|_p$ with generic $p$ contains $\binom{n}{m}$ points with multiplicity, which equals the expected number of admissible Bethe roots to the ASEP-Bethe ansatz equation.

</details>
