### [79] [A unified closed-form theory for elastic waves in polycrystals of any crystal symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26674)
*Ming Huang,Stanislav I. Rokhlin,Michael J. S. Lowe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文在一阶平滑近似（first-order smoothing approximation）框架下，针对等轴、无织构、任意晶体对称性的多晶，采用指数相关函数推导出质量算符（mass operator）的闭式解析表达，从而得到全频率范围内显式的色散方程（dispersion equation），并揭示相干场的根结构与分区规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有描述随机多晶中相干弹性波的质量算符仅为数值求值的积分形式，且该积分形式在距实轴超过一个反相关半径处失效，无法刻画深埋复波数平面中的强衰减根（heavily attenuated roots），因此需要闭式解析表达。

Method: 对等轴、无织构、任意晶体对称性且具指数相关函数（exponential correlation function）的多晶，推导质量算符的闭式（closed form），得到全频率显式色散方程；其形式紧凑，可退化包含Born近似与远场近似（far-field approximation）作为特例。

Result: 闭式在复波数平面深处仍成立；可复现Stanke与Kino对立方晶粒的色散方程并推广至任意对称性。结果揭示每支极化存在三个根，分别来自入射波与两个散射通道（无/有模式转换）；并给出瑞利区、随机区与几何区（Rayleigh、stochastic、geometric regimes）下速度、衰减与分区的闭式极限。

Conclusion: 闭式极限解释了长波下仅观测到单一相干波、短波下可观测到多个相干波的原因；该结果为多晶微结构的进一步理论与定量表征提供基础。

Abstract: The coherent elastic wave in random polycrystals is described by the first-order smoothing approximation, whose mass operator remains an integral evaluated only numerically. Here we derive its closed form for equiaxed, untextured polycrystals of any crystal symmetry with an exponential correlation function, giving an explicit dispersion equation at all frequencies. Unlike the integral form, which fails beyond one inverse correlation radius from the real axis, the closed form holds deep in the complex wavenumber plane, where heavily attenuated roots lie. It is compact, reproduces the dispersion equations of Stanke and Kino for cubic grains, extends them to any symmetry and contains the Born and far-field approximations as special cases. It reveals the structure of the roots and their partitions of the coherent field: three roots per polarization, one from the incident wave and one from each of the two scattering channels, without and with mode conversion. It also yields closed-form limits of their velocity, attenuation and partition in the Rayleigh, stochastic and geometric regimes, which show why a single coherent wave has been observed at long wavelengths, whereas more than one can be observed at short wavelengths. These results underpin further theory and quantitative characterization of polycrystalline microstructure.

</details>


### [80] [When the magnetic force theorem fails: dressed exchange in the induced-moment magnets FePt, FePd, CoPt, and CoPd](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26697)
*Vladislav Borisov,Maryna Pankratova,Rohit Pathak,Pamela Costa Carvalho,Erna K. Delczeg-Czirjak,Sagar Sarkar,Anders Bergman,Qichen Xu,Nezhat Pournaghavi,Manuel Pereiro,Hermann Dürr,Mikhail I. Katsnelson,Olle Eriksson,Anna Delin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文指出，磁力定理（magnetic-force-theorem, MFT）交换常数对含高自旋磁化率原子（如Pd、Pt）的巡游磁体描述不完整，会遗漏由交换增强（dressed）磁化率主导的诱导矩能量学；对FePt甚至给出错误的基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用做法是先用MFT求交换常数，再构建有效自旋哈密顿量（effective spin Hamiltonian）以模拟天斯基明（skyrmion）等精细自旋织构；但对含Pd/Pt等大自旋磁化率原子的体系，该流程可能遗漏关键能量项，需量化其误差并追溯来源。

Method: 以完全自洽的自旋螺旋密度泛函（spin-spiral density-functional）计算为基准（每个自旋构型下电子结构及诱导Pd/Pt矩均弛豫），对L1$_0$有序合金FePt、FePd、CoPt、CoPd进行比较；并对比仅在诱导Pt矩处理上不同的一族模型，包括Mryasov与Polesya的构型依赖模型。

Result: 缺失能量学源于诱导矩由交换增强（dressed）磁化率支配，而冻结势（frozen-potential）MFT仅捕获裸（bare）部分——在FePt中约为Pt介导相互作用的约三分之一；并给出一般性界（bound）证明任何在位（on-site, intra-atomic）修正都无法补足缺失强度。该效应对FePt决定磁基态（层间序完全依赖Pt介导相互作用），并使其他合金居里温度（Curie temperature）移动数百开尔文。

Conclusion: 对含高自旋磁化率元素的巡游磁体，仅基于冻结势MFT交换常数的有效自旋模型不足以描述能量学与磁序，需要纳入交换增强磁化率效应；文中给出四种化合物的有序温度、磁振子谱（magnon spectra）与自旋波刚度（spin wave stiffness）。

Abstract: Effective spin Hamiltonians for itinerant magnets are commonly parameterized by magnetic-force-theorem (MFT) exchange constants, which are subsequently used to compute the behavior of complex and delicate spin textures, for example skyrmions. We show that for systems containing atoms with large spin susceptibility, such as Pd or Pt, this approach misses important parts of the energetics. For FePt, it even predicts the wrong ground state. Benchmarking against fully self-consistent spin-spiral density-functional calculations - in which the electronic structure, including the induced Pd/Pt moments, relaxes for every spin configuration - we quantify the missing energetics for the ordered L1$_0$ alloys FePt, FePd, CoPt, and CoPd. By comparing a family of models that differ only in their treatment of the induced Pt moment, including the configuration-dependent models of Mryasov and Polesya, we identify the origin: the energetics of the induced moments are governed by the exchange-enhanced (dressed) susceptibility, of which the frozen-potential MFT captures only the bare part - about one third of the Pt-mediated interaction in FePt. We derive a general bound showing that no on-site (intra-atomic) refinement can supply the missing strength. The dressing decides the magnetic ground state in FePt, where the interlayer ordering rests entirely on the Pt-mediated interactions, and shifts the Curie temperatures of the other alloys by several hundred kelvin. Computed ordering temperatures, magnon spectra and spin wave stiffness for all four compounds are provided.

</details>


### [81] [Atomic layer etching of niobium via alternating O$_2$ and H$_2$/SF$_6$ plasma exposures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26735)
*Lillian B. Hughes Wyatt,Azmain A. Hossain,Yifei Yan,Austin J. Minnich*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作报道了一种基于O₂等离子体与H₂/SF₆等离子体顺序暴露的铌（Nb）等离子体增强原子层刻蚀（plasma-enhanced atomic layer etching, ALE）工艺，实现5–7 Å/cycle的刻蚀速率并兼具表面平坦化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铌（niobium, Nb）薄膜是超导探测器与超导电子器件的关键材料，其表面质量、表面粗糙度与几何尺寸精度影响多项性能指标，因此需要具备埃级精度且能改善表面形貌的刻蚀手段。

Method: 采用等离子体增强原子层刻蚀（plasma-enhanced ALE），以O₂等离子体与H₂/SF₆等离子体的顺序循环暴露（sequential exposures）构成自限制刻蚀循环。

Result: 工艺实现每循环5–7 Å的刻蚀量（etch per cycle），并表现出表面平滑（surface smoothing）特性；经ALE处理的薄膜其超导转变温度（superconducting transition temperature）的下降可由膜厚减薄预期解释而得以保持。

Conclusion: 该ALE工艺有望应用于对表面质量与精确厚度控制要求较高的超导电子器件（superconducting electronics）。

Abstract: Niobium thin films are a key component of superconducting detectors and electronics, and film surface quality, surface roughness, and geometrical dimensional accuracy affect many performance metrics. Atomic layer etching (ALE) is of interest for modifying film surfaces owing to its Angstrom-scale etching precision and surface smoothing characteristics. Here, we report a plasma-enhanced ALE process for Nb based on sequential O$_2$ plasma and H$_2$/SF$_6$ plasma exposures. The process achieves an etch per cycle of 5-7 Angstrom/cycle and exhibits surface smoothing. The superconducting transition temperature of the ALE-treated films is maintained up to the expected decrease associated with reduced film thickness. Our ALE process could find applications in superconducting electronics for which surface quality and precise thickness control are of high importance.

</details>


### [82] [Prediction of lattice thermal conductivity of half-Heusler compounds by machine learning and transfer-function calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26788)
*Shivam Randive,Ojaswini Randive,Heet Lungaria,Himanshu Pandey*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究量化并校正了第一性原理计算与实验测量之间半赫斯勒（half-Heusler）化合物晶格热导率（$\kappa_L$）的系统性偏差，提出含温度依赖的简单传递函数（transfer function）方法，预测精度达到独立实验室间的一致性水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量筛选（high-throughput screening）依赖第一性原理计算或基于其训练的机器学习模型给出的 $\kappa_L$，但计算描述理想单晶，而测量在存在晶界与缺陷散射的多晶（polycrystalline）样品上进行，二者差距在单一化合物中已被注意到，却未在整个材料类别上被量化，导致筛选所用数值带有未知的系统性偏差（systematic bias）。

Method: 构建严格区分计算值与测量值的文献语料库（literature corpus），聚焦18价电子的立方半赫斯勒化合物；分析偏差随键合家族（bonding family）与温度的变化趋势，并引入具有该温度依赖性的简单传递函数，按键合家族重新拟合其幅度；再结合由晶体结构预测计算值的机器学习模型，在未参与任何拟合的化学体系上测试。

Result: 计算值在多数键合家族中高估实测 $\kappa_L$，且超出量随温度升高而减小；传递函数可消除中位偏移（median offset），按家族重新拟合幅度可进一步降低中位误差，但逐化合物的改善并不一致。在留出化学体系上，该方法预测实测 $\kappa_L$ 的准确度与独立实验室之间的一致程度相当，并给出尚未测量化合物的可检验预测。

Conclusion: 计算与实测 $\kappa_L$ 之间的偏差在该材料类别中可被系统量化与校正，校正后的模型可支撑更可靠的筛选，并为未测化合物提供可验证的预测；数据尚无法唯一确定偏差成因（如缺陷散射叠加于晶格振动本征散射），机制的单一归因仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: High-throughput searches for half-Heusler thermoelectrics increasingly rely on lattice thermal conductivity ($κ_L$) from first-principles calculations, or from machine-learning models trained on them. These calculations describe an ideal single crystal, whereas measurements are made on polycrystalline samples, in which grain boundaries and defects scatter heat as well. The resulting gap between calculated and measured $κ_L$ has been noted for individual compounds but not, to our knowledge, quantified across a materials class, so the values used for screening carry an unknown, systematic bias. Here we quantify and correct this bias for cubic half-Heusler compounds with 18 valence electrons. Using a curated literature corpus that keeps calculated and measured values apart, we show that calculations overestimate measured $κ_L$ in most bonding families, and that the excess shrinks with temperature. Scattering by defects, added to the intrinsic scattering between lattice vibrations, would give such a trend, but the data do not single out one cause. A simple transfer function with this temperature dependence removes the median offset, and refitting its magnitude within a bonding family lowers the median error further, though not consistently compound by compound. Combined with a machine-learning model that predicts the calculated value from crystal structure, and tested on chemistries withheld from all fitting, the method predicts measured $κ_L$ in this class about as closely as independent laboratories agree with one another, and provides testable predictions for compounds not yet measured.

</details>


### [83] [Tuning the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction in CoFeB thin film heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26808)
*Rabindra R Oliya,Christopher Keck,Lia Compton,Jing Guo,Kristen S Buchanan,Robert Streubel,Shireen Adenwalla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过在Pt/CoFeB/Ta/Pt超薄磁性异质结中改变Ta亚单层厚度（0.1–0.4 nm）系统调控DMI，并用三种独立方法测量其大小与符号，三者在实验误差内高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMI对手性自旋织构的稳定至关重要，但不同测量技术在名义相同的样品上给出的DMI大小与手性不一致，表明DMI对界面性质与测量方法均高度敏感，缺乏可靠的定量一致性。

Method: 在Pt/CoFeB/Ta/Pt异质结中改变Ta亚单层厚度（0.1–0.4 nm）实现DMI的系统调控，并采用平行条纹畴模型（parallel stripe domain model）、边缘形核场分析（edge nucleation field analysis）与布里渊光散射（Brillouin light scattering）三种独立方法测量DMI的大小与符号。

Result: 三种独立方法在实验误差范围内对DMI的测量结果高度一致；DMI的大小与符号可通过Ta亚单层厚度实现可控调节。

Conclusion: 可控调控与可靠定量DMI为畴壁、斯格明子（skyrmions）等手性磁织构的工程化及下一代自旋电子器件提供可行路径；由于各技术的DMI计算依赖不同参数，需精确测量磁参数并注重细节。

Abstract: The Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya Interaction (DMI) is essential for stabilizing chiral spin textures in spintronics, yet achieving consistent quantitative measurements across different techniques remains a significant challenge. Heterostructures consisting of magnetic thin films sandwiched between non-magnetic heavy metals have been shown to possess substantial DMI; however, these vary in both magnitude and handedness for supposedly identical samples, indicating the sensitivity of DMI to both interfacial properties and measurement techniques. Here, systematic tuning of the DMI in ultra-thin magnetic heterostructures of Pt/CoFeB/Ta/Pt is achieved by varying the thickness of a tantalum sub-monolayer with thicknesses ranging from $0.1\,\mathrm{nm}$ to $0.4\,\mathrm{nm}$. The magnitude and sign of the DMI are measured using three independent methods: the parallel stripe domain model, edge nucleation field analysis, and Brillouin light scattering. The three independent approaches reveal excellent agreement within the experimental error. This ability to controllably tune and reliably quantify DMI provides a viable route toward engineering chiral magnetic textures, including domain walls and skyrmions, for next-generation spintronic devices. The necessity for precise measurements of magnetic parameters and careful attention to detail are discussed because calculations of the DMI for each technique depend on different parameters.

</details>


### [84] [Artificial Spin-Glass of Antiferromagnetic Co3O4 Nanocubes Using Spin-Polarized Oxygen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26860)
*Gurleen K. Uppal,Alberto Acosta,Rachel Nickel,Christoph Klewe,Yanna Chen,Chengjun Sun,Charles A. Roberts,Johan van Lierop*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过立方体形纳米颗粒优先暴露Co₃O₄的(100)晶面，可诱导离子非常规重排，使反铁磁纳米立方体产生类自旋玻璃磁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）纳米材料中通过晶面调控获得磁性阻挫（magnetic frustration）与类自旋玻璃（spin-glass-like）行为的可能性，并为自旋电子学（spintronics）与物理储备池计算（physical reservoir computing）等新兴技术提供材料平台。

Method: 采用立方体形纳米颗粒优先暴露(100)表面终端；并通过尺寸控制（size control）优先极化Co-O键，从而调控八面体位点上Co²⁺与异常高自旋Co³⁺的组合。

Result: 磁性阻挫源于O 2p与Co 3d轨道间电荷转移产生的极化Co-O键，与高度各向异性Co²⁺及八面体位点异常高自旋（high spin）Co³⁺之间的竞争；由此在反铁磁纳米立方体中产生类自旋玻璃磁性。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了对反铁磁纳米立方体中阻挫磁性的调控，提供人工自旋玻璃（artificial spin glass）平台，用于探索非常规纳米磁性并支撑自旋电子学与物理储备池计算等应用。

Abstract: Preferentially exposing (100) surface terminations of Co$_{3}$O$_{4}$ using cube-shaped nanoparticles yields an unconventional rearrangement of ions that generates spin-glass-like magnetism from the antiferromagnetic nanocubes. The necessary magnetic frustration arises out of competition between polarized Co-O bonds from charge transfer between O 2p and Co 3d orbitals, and highly anisotropic Co$^{2+}$ ions combined with anomalous high spin Co$^{3+}$ ions in octahedral sites. Size control permits tuning this novel combination of ions via preferentially polarizing Co-O bonds. The presence of control over frustrated magnetism among antiferromagnetic nanocubes yields an artificial spin glass platform for exploring unconventional nanomagnetism and engendering new opportunities for emerging technologies like spintronics and physical reservoir computing.

</details>


### [85] [First-principles-based Prediction of Phase Fields: Part II. Solid Solution Tunability in Refractory Complex Concentrated Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26866)
*Pravan Omprakash,Nicholas Crnkovich,Nathan Curtis,John Cavin,Adrien Couet,Rohan Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作采用快速热力学相场（phase-field）计算，在Cr–Hf–Mo–Nb–Ta–Ti–V–W–Zr构成的难熔复杂浓缩合金（RCCAs）体系中系统绘制固溶体稳定性拓扑图，并提出“可混溶区域百分比（percentage miscible region）”指标以区分稳定单相与可调多相体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有RCCA研究多集中于等原子比附近的孤立成分点，缺乏对周围成分空间中固溶体稳定性如何变化的系统认识，难以支撑面向单相固溶或可控多相组织的定向设计。

Method: 以快速热力学相场计算对九元素RCCAs进行成分空间扫描，并用109个实验报道的四元至六元合金成分（涵盖多种制备工艺）进行基准验证，分类准确率达80%；随后引入“可混溶区域百分比”作为量化指标，并对三元、四元、五元体系进行分析。

Result: 模型总体分类准确率为80%；高阶体系的互溶性与组成它的二元子系统存在强继承关系；元素层面上Ti、Ta、Nb、Mo倾向稳定固溶体，Cr与Zr促进有序析出与相分离，Hf、W、V则可在两类行为之间调节。

Conclusion: 研究建立了面向RCCA成分空间的设计框架，可用于定向获取稳健的单相固溶体或可调多相显微组织，并强调二元交互作用选择在合金设计中的关键作用。

Abstract: Refractory complex concentrated alloys (RCCAs) offer a high-dimensional composition space for designing either robust single-phase solid solutions or controlled multiphase microstructures. However, most RCCA studies have examined isolated compositions, often near the equimolar point, rather than determining how solid-solution stability varies across the surrounding composition space. Here, we use rapid thermodynamic phase-field calculations to map the topology of solid-solution stability in RCCAs composed of Cr, Hf, Mo, Nb, Ta, Ti, V, W, and Zr. Benchmarking the model against 109 experimentally reported quaternary-to-senary compositions produced through diverse processing routes yields an overall classification accuracy of 80%. We then introduce percentage miscible region, a metric that separates robust solid-solution-forming systems from those exhibiting compositionally tunable microstructures. Analysis of ternary, quaternary, and quinary RCCAs reveals that higher-order miscibility is strongly inherited from constituent binary subsystems, indicating the importance of choosing suitable binary interactions when designing RCCAs. Element-specific trends further show that Ti, Ta, Nb, and Mo generally stabilize solid solutions, whereas Cr and Zr promote ordered precipitation and phase segregation. Hf, W, and V, meanwhile, enable tuning between these regimes. These results establish a composition-space framework for the targeted design of RCCAs with robust solid-solution stability or tunable multiphase microstructures.

</details>


### [86] [Interfacial Transition Waves and Domain Wall Self-Pinning in Bi-stable Mechanical Metamaterial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26877)
*Michael J. Frazier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出双稳态机械超材料（bi-stable mechanical metamaterials）在矢量序参量（vector order parameter）情形下的第四类转变波原型，并报道局限于构型边界（configurational boundaries）的两种界面转变波及其速度调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有转变波（transition waves）研究仅覆盖三类原型（多稳态标量/矢量序参量、双稳态标量序参量），而“双稳态+矢量序参量”这一第四原型明显缺失，补全该四元体系是本文动机。

Method: 采用数值方法（numerically）考察具有矢量序参量的双稳态机械超材料中独特的转变动力学，并分析离散性效应（discreteness effects）的调控途径。

Result: 报告了两种界面转变波（interfacial transition waves），其传播被限制在构型边界（如畴壁 domain walls）的狭窄区域内；波速可能依赖于其极化方向（polarization）；并揭示可调节离散性效应强弱的途径，使离散性成为控制波速（含畴壁钉扎/脱钉 pinning/de-pinning）的可调内禀机制。

Conclusion: 结果可支撑可重构机械电路（reconfigurable mechanical circuits）与复杂畴图案化（complex domain patterning）等应用，并推动对具有非常规转变过程的超材料开展进一步研究。

Abstract: The understanding of transition waves in mechanical metamaterials currently derives from architectures fitting one of three archetypes as distinguished by the stability and order parameter: multi-stable with scalar or vector order parameter, and bi-stable with scalar order parameter. However, a fourth archetype is conspicuously absent: bi-stable with vector order parameter. Completing the tetrad, in this article, we numerically examine the transformation dynamics unique to bi-stable mechanical metamaterials with vector order parameter. We report two types of interfacial transition waves, whose propagation is restricted to the narrow region of configurational boundaries (e.g., domain walls). We show that the wave speed may depend on its polarization. In addition, we reveal an approach to regulate the severity of discreteness effects, which elevates discreteness as an intrinsic, tunable mechanism for controlling the wave speed -- including pinning/de-pinning of the domain wall -- without altering the metamaterial architecture. The results support applications in, e.g., reconfigurable mechanical circuits and complex domain patterning, and motivate further investigation of metamaterials with unconventional transition processes.

</details>


### [87] [Transition-Metal Substitutions in MOF-5: A Hybrid-DFT Study of Oxygen Electrocatalysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27037)
*Pedro H. Souza,Walter Orellana*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用杂化密度泛函理论（hybrid density functional theory）研究 MOF-5 中 Zn 位点被 3d 过渡金属取代后的稳定性、电子性质与氧电催化活性，发现 Mn 取代可提供接近贵金属基准的双功能 ORR/OER 活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过过渡金属掺杂调控 MOF-5 的电子结构，探索其在氧还原与析氧反应（ORR/OER）电催化中的潜力，并建立掺杂剂电子结构与催化性能之间的关联以指导 MOF 基电催化剂设计。

Method: 基于杂化密度泛函理论计算，系统考察 M = Sc 至 Cu 九种过渡金属取代 Zn 位点（M<sub>Zn</sub>）的 MOF-5 [Zn<sub>4</sub>O(BDC)<sub>3</sub>]；ORR 与 OER 活性通过计算氢电极模型（computational hydrogen electrode model）评估。

Result: 多数取代在 MOF-5 带隙（band gap）内引入 3d 态并诱导局域磁矩（localized magnetic moments）；部分占据的 3d 态促进与含氧中间体的轨道杂化（orbital hybridization）与电荷转移。Mn<sub>Zn</sub> 与 Fe<sub>Zn</sub> 的 ORR 过电位（overpotential）均为 0.57 V，Mn<sub>Zn</sub> 与 Co<sub>Zn</sub> 的 OER 过电位分别为 0.42 V 与 0.51 V，接近贵金属基准电催化剂水平。

Conclusion: Mn 取代是实现 MOF-5 双功能（bifunctional）ORR/OER 活性的有前景策略；研究确立了掺杂剂电子结构与催化性能之间的关联，可为 MOF 基电催化剂的设计提供指导。

Abstract: Hybrid density functional theory calculations were used to investigate the stability, electronic properties, and catalytic activity of transition-metal impurities substituting Zn sites (M\(_{\rm Zn}\)) in the MOF-5 metal-organic framework [Zn\(_4\)O(BDC)\(_3\), BDC = 1,4-benzenedicarboxylate], with M = Sc, Ti, V, Cr, Mn, Fe, Co, Ni, and Cu. Most substitutions introduce \(3d\) states within the MOF-5 band gap and induce localized magnetic moments. Partially occupied \(3d\) states promote orbital hybridization and charge transfer with oxygenated intermediates, activating the dopant sites toward oxygen electrocatalysis. The oxygen reduction and evolution reaction (ORR and OER) activities were evaluated using the computational hydrogen electrode model. Mn\(_{\rm Zn}\) and Fe\(_{\rm Zn}\) exhibit calculated ORR overpotentials of 0.57~V, while Mn\(_{\rm Zn}\) and Co\(_{\rm Zn}\) yield OER overpotentials of 0.42 and 0.51~V, respectively, approaching those of benchmark precious-metal electrocatalysts. These findings identify Mn substitution as a promising strategy for achieving bifunctional ORR/OER activity in MOF-5 and establish a link between dopant electronic structure and catalytic performance to guide the design of MOF-based electrocatalysts.

</details>


### [88] [Growth and anisotropic directional dependent transport properties of Altermagnetic CrSb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27072)
*Resham Babu Regmi,Hari Bhandari,Sk Jamaluddin,Abhijeet Nayak,Ashok Chaudhary,Mohamed El Gazzah,Alex T. Sheardy,Maksym Zhukovskyi,Allen G. Oliver,Nirmal J. Ghimire*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了Sb自助熔剂（Sb self-flux）、Bi助熔剂（Bi flux）与化学气相输运（chemical vapor transport, CVT）三种方法生长的CrSb单晶的结构、磁性与各向异性输运性质，表明不同生长路线下各向异性磁输运具有可重复性，但载流子散射对生长条件敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CrSb是一种具有大动量依赖自旋劈裂（momentum-dependent spin splitting）且奈尔温度远高于室温的金属性交错磁体（altermagnet），其电子与磁输运性质已受广泛关注，但不同生长方法制备晶体的系统性比较仍然有限。

Method: 采用Sb自助熔剂、Bi助熔剂和化学气相输运（CVT）三种方法生长CrSb单晶，并对其结构、磁性及各向异性输运性质（磁化、电阻率、磁阻与霍尔测量，最高至9 T）进行对比研究。

Result: 三种方法所得晶体形貌各异；磁化测量显示反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）响应总体一致，Bi助熔剂晶体存在额外低温贡献；所有样品呈金属性电阻率与正的非饱和磁阻（magnetoresistance）；Sb助熔剂与CVT晶体的剩余电阻率比（residual resistivity ratio）及磁阻相近，而Bi助熔剂晶体剩余电阻率升高、磁阻显著降低，与更强的载流子散射（carrier scattering）一致；霍尔测量显示明显的场—电流构型依赖性，包括非线性场依赖及不同晶向下的相反霍尔极性。

Conclusion: 结果确立了三者生长路线间各向异性磁输运的可重复性，同时凸显载流子散射对晶体生长条件的敏感性，为面向取向依赖输运与自旋电子学（spintronics）研究的CrSb单晶选择提供了比较框架。

Abstract: CrSb is a metallic altermagnet exhibiting large momentum-dependent spin splitting and antifer- romagnetic ordering well above room temperature. Although its electronic and magnetotransport properties have attracted considerable attention, a systematic comparison of crystals prepared us- ing different growth methods remains limited. Here we investigate the structural, magnetic, and anisotropic transport properties of CrSb single crystals grown by Sb(self)-flux, Bi flux, and chem- ical vapor transport (CVT). Each of these growth reveals distinct crystal morphologies. Magne- tization measurements exhibit broadly consistent antiferromagnetic responses, with an additional low-temperature contribution observed in Bi-flux-grown crystals. All samples display metallic resis- tivity and positive, nonsaturating magnetoresistance up to 9 T. Sb-flux- and CVT-grown crystals exhibit comparable residual resistivity ratios and magnetoresistance, whereas Bi-flux-grown crys- tals show enhanced residual resistivity and a substantially reduced magnetoresistance, consistent with stronger carrier scattering. Hall measurements reveal a pronounced dependence on the field- current configuration, including nonlinear field dependence and opposite Hall polarities for different crystallographic orientations. These results establish the reproducibility of anisotropic magneto- transport across distinct growth routes while highlighting the sensitivity of carrier scattering to crystal-growth conditions. Our findings provide a comparative framework for selecting CrSb single crystals for orientation-dependent transport and spintronic investigations.

</details>


### [89] [Axial Thermal Transport in Transition Metal Chalcogenide Nanowires from Machine-Learning Molecular Dynamics Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27103)
*Wenwu Jiang,Hekai Bu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合多组分神经演化势（neuroevolution potential, NEP）与均匀非平衡分子动力学（homogeneous nonequilibrium molecular dynamics, HNEMD），系统量化六种M6X6纳米线的轴向热导率，揭示其随元素组成变化的非单调或单调趋势源于金属—硫族振动匹配与声子软化之间的竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属硫族化合物M6X6纳米线是有前景的一维（one-dimensional, 1D）构筑单元，但元素组成如何调控其本征轴向热输运尚不清晰，亟需原子尺度上的定量认识以指导纳米线束与阵列中的导热单元选择。

Method: 基于第一性原理参考数据构建多组分神经演化势（NEP），并采用均匀非平衡分子动力学（HNEMD）模拟计算六种MX6纳米线的热导率；辅以频率分辨热导率、声子平均自由程（mean-free path）、振动态密度（vibrational density of states）与谱能量密度（spectral energy density）分析，并提出包含原子质量及金属—硫族相对质量差的现象学模型。

Result: Mo6X6系列热导率随组成呈非单调变化，而W6X6系列随硫族原子质量增加单调下降；轴向热传导由低频声子主导。振动谱分析表明，两种趋势的差异源于金属—硫族振动匹配（vibrational matching）与较重硫族原子诱导的声子软化（phonon softening）之间的竞争，现象学模型可重现上述趋势。

Conclusion: 研究将元素组成、声子特征与M6X6纳米线的轴向热导率相联系，为纳米线束与阵列中导热单元的选取提供了原子尺度依据。

Abstract: Transition metal chalcogenide M6X6 nanowires (M = Mo or W; X = S, Se, or Te) are promising one-dimensional (1D) building blocks for nanowire bundles and arrays, but how elemental composition controls their intrinsic axial heat transport remains unclear. Here we develop a multicomponent neuroevolution potential (NEP) from first-principles reference data and use homogeneous nonequilibrium molecular dynamics (HNEMD) simulations to quantify the thermal conductivity of six MX6 nanowires. The Mo6X6 series show a nonmonotonic composition dependence, whereas the thermal conductivity of the W6X6 series decreases monotonically with increasing chalcogen mass. Frequency-resolved thermal conductivity and phonon mean-free path analyses show that axial heat conduction is dominated by low-frequency phonons. Vibrational density of states and spectral energy density analyses further reveal that the contrasting trends arise from a competition between metal-chalcogen vibrational matching and phonon softening induced by heavier chalcogen atoms. A phenomenological model incorporating the combined atomic mass and the relative mass difference between the metal and chalcogen atoms reproduces these trends. These results link elemental composition, phonon characteristics and axial thermal conductivity in M6X6 nanowires, providing an atomistic basis for selecting heat-conducting units in nanowire bundles and arrays.

</details>


### [90] [Power Law Transients in Perovskite Photoluminescence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27163)
*Anas M. M.,Pradeep R. Nair*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文指出文献中多种钙钛矿薄膜瞬态光致发光（PL）衰减实为随时间的幂律（power law）变化而非多指数衰减，并将幂律指数与缺陷态分布（trap distributions）相关联，进而修正了既有理论对幂律来源的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对钙钛矿薄膜载流子动力学与复合的研究多依赖瞬态光致发光测量及单/多指数（single/multi-exponentials）拟合，而既有理论方法难以解释文献中出现的幂律型PL瞬态之来源与含义，需厘清这一矛盾。

Method: 基于文献已报道的多个实验PL瞬态曲线进行分析，识别其幂律时间依赖特征，并将幂律指数与缺陷态分布进行关联，据此重新审视既有复合理论。

Result: 分析表明PL瞬态可呈幂律而非多指数形式，且幂律指数与缺陷态分布相关；值得注意的是，即使在单分子复合（monomolecular recombination）主导时PL瞬态也可表现为t^{-2}，而既有理论仅将该特征归因于双分子复合（bimolecular recombination）主导。

Conclusion: 研究界定了微分寿命（differential lifetime）等概念的适用边界，并强调需发展预测性建模（predictive modeling）并与深入的数据分析相结合，以优化钙钛矿光电器件性能。

Abstract: Carrier dynamics and recombination in perovskite thin films are often explored through transient photoluminescence (PL) measurements and subsequent analysis based on single/multi-exponentials. In contrast, here we show that several experimental PL transients reported in literature vary as power laws with time and are not multi-exponentials. Puzzlingly, the existing theoretical methods are ill-equipped to explain the origin and implications of such power law trends. Our analysis resolves this puzzle and correlates the power law exponents with the trap distributions. Curiously, we show that PL transients could vary as $t^{-2}$ even in the presence of dominant monomolecular recombination while the existing theory associates this unique trend exclusively with dominant bimolecular recombination. These findings identify the limits of applicability of concepts such as differential lifetime and highlight the need for predictive modeling synergistic with insightful data analysis towards performance optimization of perovskite optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [91] [Quantum effects on the Peierls stress for screw dislocations in α-Fe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27176)
*Jiaqu Sun,Yang Li,Boyang Gu,David McDowell,Youping Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究采用声子（phonon）描述温度并结合并发原子-连续体（Concurrent Atomistic-continuum, CAC）方法，对α-Fe中螺位错（screw dislocation）在0.7 K下的动力学进行多尺度（multiscale）研究，成功再现了实验测量的Peierls应力并量化了量子效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子尺度方法计算的Peierls应力与实验测量值之间存在显著差异，尽管过去数十年已有大量研究，对该不匹配现象的理解仍然有限。

Method: 采用温度（temperature）的声子描述，以并发原子-连续体（CAC）方法为模拟工具，开展位错线长度0.1至2.1微米的大尺度模拟，考察声子-位错相互作用。

Result: 模拟保持了声子模式的Bose-Einstein分布，并再现了扭结对（kink pair）的形核与扩展；成功复现了实验测得的α-Fe Peierls应力，并量化了量子效应对Peierls应力的影响。

Conclusion: 研究提供了α-Fe中声子-位错相互作用的定量与机理认识，并探讨了声子-位错相互作用对位错线长度与试样尺寸的依赖性。

Abstract: The large discrepancy between the Peierls stress calculated using atomistic methods and that measured in experimental studies is well known. Despite significant research efforts over the past decades, our understanding of this mismatch remains limited. This work presents a multiscale study on the kinetics of screw dislocations in α-Fe at 0.7K, employing the phonon description of temperature and using the Concurrent Atomistic-continuum (CAC) method as the simulation tool. Our simulations are shown to preserve the Bose-Einstein distribution of phonon modes and reproduce the nucleation and propagation of kink pairs. Large-scale CAC simulations with dislocation line lengths ranging from 0.1 to 2.1 microns, a typical range of lengths for screw dislocation lines, are performed, providing a quantitative and mechanistic understanding of the phonon-dislocation in α-Fe. The experimentally measured Peierls stress in α-Fe has been successfully reproduced, and the quantum effects on Peierls stress are quantified. Dependencies of phonon-dislocation interactions on dislocation line length and specimen size are explored and discussed.

</details>


### [92] [Generating grain boundary structures at a specified energy with a symmetry-constrained diffusion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27188)
*Kazuya Miyamoto,Ibuki Okuda,Izumi Takahara,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作基于 MatterGen 构建对称约束扩散模型（symmetry-constrained diffusion model），强制周期性晶胞内两个晶界（grain boundary, GB）通过对称操作等价，从而将 GB 能量作为条件实现晶界结构的逆向设计（inverse design）。在 Al 的 Σ5(210)[001] 对称倾斜晶界上，350 个生成结构弛豫后均保持几何等价的晶界对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶界（grain boundary）决定多晶材料众多性质，若能将晶界结构本身作为设计变量即可实现逆向设计；但 MatterGen 等晶体生成模型作用于含两个晶界的周期性晶胞（periodic cell），其能量仅在两个晶界等价时才对应单一晶界，而现有模型并不强制这一等价性，导致晶界性质无法成为良定义的设计目标。

Method: 在 MatterGen 基础上提出对称约束扩散模型：两个晶界由对称操作相联系，仅生成靠近一侧晶界的原子，另一侧原子则通过在每个去噪步骤（denoising step）施加的对称投影（symmetry projection）重建。将该方法应用于 Al 的 Σ5(210)[001] 对称倾斜晶界，并以机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potential, MLIP）给出的晶界能量作为生成条件。

Result: 全部 350 个生成结构在弛豫（relaxation）后均保持几何等价的晶界对。给定目标晶界能量时，模型可生成对应能量的结构：对于三个位于训练范围内且邻近训练结构较多的目标，44%–70% 的生成结构落在目标值 ±0.1 J/m² 内；能量更高时跟踪能力减弱，超出训练范围则失效。91 个接近目标的结构中有 90 个按所采用的几何判据不匹配任何训练结构，表明模型能提出与训练结构不同的近目标能量构型。

Conclusion: 将晶界对等价性内建于生成过程，使晶界性质成为良定义的设计目标，从而实现晶界结构的逆向设计；该能力有望帮助识别实验中观察到但未知的亚稳晶界（metastable grain boundary）结构。

Abstract: Grain boundaries (GBs) govern many properties of polycrystalline materials, and inverse design would make the GB structure itself a design variable. Crystal generative models such as MatterGen operate on periodic cells, which contain two GBs. The cell energy defines a single GB only when the two are equivalent, but existing models do not enforce this equivalence. Building on MatterGen, we developed a symmetry-constrained diffusion model in which the two GBs are related by a symmetry operation: atoms near one GB are generated, and those near the other are reconstructed through a symmetry projection applied at every denoising step. We applied it to the $Σ5(210)[001]$ symmetric tilt GB in Al, conditioning generation on the GB energy from a machine-learning interatomic potential (MLIP). All 350 generated structures kept a geometrically equivalent GB pair after relaxation. Given a target GB energy, the model generated structures at that energy: for the three in-range targets with many nearby training structures, 44% to 70% fell within $\pm 0.1$ J/m$^2$ of the target. Tracking weakened at higher energies and failed outside the training range. Of the 91 near-target structures, 90 matched no training structure under the adopted geometric criterion, indicating that the model can propose configurations near the target energy that are distinct from the training structures, a capability that could help identify unknown metastable GB structures observed in experiments. Building GB-pair equivalence into generation thus makes GB properties well-defined design targets, enabling inverse design of GB structures.

</details>


### [93] [Tailoring Phonon Transport in Bi-based Quaternary Half-Heusler alloys for Thermoelectric Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27234)
*Supriya Ghosal,Sayan Paul,Swapan K. Pati*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用第一性原理计算与非谐晶格动力学，研究四种四元半赫斯勒（quaternary half-Heusler, QHH）化合物的热电性能；其中p型ZrHfRh2Bi2与Zr2RhIrBi2在1000 K下ZT分别达2.28与2.18，晶粒细化至30 nm后提升至2.51与2.58。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四元半赫斯勒（QHH）作为常规半赫斯勒（half-Heusler, HH）化合物的替位无序衍生物，被认为有望调控声子输运（phonon transport）并提升热电性能（thermoelectric performance），但其具体热电潜力尚需系统评估。

Method: 基于第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与非谐晶格动力学（anharmonic lattice dynamics），对ZrHfRh2Bi2、ZrHfIr2Bi2、Zr2RhIrBi2和Hf2RhIrBi2四种QHH的电子结构与热电输运性质进行计算分析。

Result: ZrHfRh2Bi2与Zr2RhIrBi2呈半导体电子结构，其余两种为半金属（semimetallic）；四元替位使平均声子群速度（phonon group velocity）较母体化合物降低约17–22%，而非谐散射率（anharmonic scattering rates）基本相当。p型ZrHfRh2Bi2与Zr2RhIrBi2在1000 K下最大ZT分别为2.28和2.18，p型ZrHfRh2Bi2的最大热电能量转换效率（power conversion efficiency）为16.3%；晶粒尺寸降至30 nm时，两者ZT分别升至2.51和2.58。

Conclusion: 替位无序（substitutional disorder）与晶界工程（grain boundary engineering）相结合，是提升半赫斯勒化合物热电性能的有效策略。

Abstract: Quaternary half-Heuslers (QHHs) are substitutionally disordered derivatives of conventional halfHeusler (HH) compounds, which offer a potential route to modify phonon transport and enhance thermoelectric performance. Here, we investigate the thermoelectric properties of the quaternary half-Heuslers ZrHfRh2Bi2, ZrHfIr2Bi2, Zr2RhIrBi2, and Hf2RhIrBi2 using first-principles calculations and anharmonic lattice dynamics. Among four compounds, ZrHfRh2Bi2 and Zr2RhIrBi2 exhibit semiconducting electronic structure and others are semimetallic in nature. Both the semiconducting compounds show favorable electronic transport properties. However, quaternary substitution substantially modifies the phonon dispersion, lowering the average phonon group velocity by about 17-22% relative to the parent compounds while leaving the anharmonic scattering rates comparable. The combination of high power factor and low kl results in high thermoelectric performance, with maximum ZT values of 2.28 and 2.18 at 1000 K for p-type ZrHfRh2Bi2 and Zr2RhIrBi2, respectively, with maximum thermoelectric power conversion efficiency 16.3% for p-type ZrHfRh2Bi2. Further reducing the grain size enhances the thermoelectric performance, with maximum ZT values of 2.51 and 2.58 at 1000 K for p-type ZrHfRh2Bi2 and Zr2RhIrBi2, respectively, at a grain size of 30 nm. Our results demonstrate that combining substitutional disorder with grain boundary engineering, provides an effective strategy to enhance the thermoelectric performance of HH compounds.

</details>


### [94] [Frustration-Stabilized Hexamerons and Their Topological Magnons in Janus MXene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27384)
*C. B. Subba,Z. Pachuau,Samy Brahimi,Samir Lounis,D. P. Rai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文预测Janus MXene材料MnHf2C2FI在Mn层形成受挫120°奈尔态，并在其上支持由六个meron/antimeron组成的亚稳态复合拓扑织构"hexameron"，其磁振子谱与热霍尔输运具有可观测指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角晶格受挫磁体（frustrated magnets）在反演对称性破缺时可承载局域拓扑织构，但缺乏同时具备受挫性、对称性破缺与自旋轨道耦合的二维材料平台。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与原子尺度自旋模拟（atomistic spin simulations），并采用线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory）分析激发谱。

Result: 磁性局限于Mn层，强反铁磁近邻交换与显著Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用稳定120°奈尔基态；识别出亚稳态hexameron（三个子晶格各含一对涡旋-反涡旋，拓扑电荷补偿），其为局域能量极小，非共面核束缚位于体能隙以下的离散带内磁振子模；周期性阵列中磁振子能带具非零陈数（Chern number），贝里曲率（Berry curvature）导致磁振子热霍尔响应，其符号取决于所实现的两种时间反演伙伴之一。

Conclusion: Janus MXenes构成二维平台，其中受挫性、反演对称性破缺与自旋轨道耦合共同产生具独特谱学与输运指纹的亚稳态反铁磁孤子。

Abstract: Frustrated magnets on triangular lattices favour noncollinear order, and when inversion symmetry is broken they can host localized topological textures on top of that order. Here we show that the Janus MXene MnHf2C2FI is such a system. Combining first-principles calculations with atomistic spin simulations, we find that magnetism is confined to the Mn layer, where a strong antiferromagnetic nearest-neighbor exchange together with a sizable Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction stabilizes a frustrated 120 Neel ground state. On top of this ground state we identify a metastable hexameron: a composite of six merons and antimerons, one vortex-antivortex pair on each of the three sublattices, whose sublattice-resolved topological charges compensate. Linear spin-wave theory confirms that the hexameron is a local energy minimum, and its noncoplanar cores act as potential wells that bind discrete in-gap magnon modes below the bulk continuum. In a periodic hexameron array the magnon bands carry nonzero Chern numbers, and the resulting Berry curvature produces a magnon thermal Hall response whose sign depends on which of the two time-reversal partners of the hexameron is realized. Our results establish Janus MXenes as a two-dimensional platform in which frustration, broken inversion symmetry, and spin-orbit coupling combine to produce metastable antiferromagnetic solitons with distinct spectroscopic and transport fingerprints.

</details>


### [95] [Decoupling Exciton Energetics from Wavefunction Geometry in Imide-Based Defect Double Antiperovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27440)
*Dhriti Mandal,Saswata Bhattacharya*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用自旋轨道耦合准粒子计算、模型Bethe–Salpeter方程（mBSE）与晶格极化解析模型，研究酰亚胺基缺陷双反钙钛矿AE₅AsPn(NH)₂的激子束缚能（140–210 meV）、波函数几何及极化子输运，并揭示激子能量学与波函数几何对化学/结构修饰的部分解耦响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 酰亚胺基缺陷双反钙钛矿（defect double antiperovskites）的激发态性质尚不清楚，尤其化学取代与四方—单斜结构畸变如何分别影响激子能量学、激子波函数几何与载流子输运缺乏系统认识。

Method: 采用自旋轨道耦合（spin–orbit-coupled）准粒子计算、模型Bethe–Salpeter方程（model Bethe–Salpeter equation, mBSE）以及晶格极化（lattice polarization）效应的解析描述，对五个四方（P4/mmm）与单斜（Cm）结构模型进行计算，并辅以Fröhlich极化子（Fröhlich-polaron）计算。

Result: 激子束缚能为140–210 meV；化学取代同时改变束缚能与激子空间尺度的氢原子模型（hydrogenic）估计，而P4/mmm（D₄ₕ）到Cm（Cₛ）的对称性降低对束缚能影响较小，却显著重塑实空间与动量空间的激子分布；晶格极化作为附加屏蔽通道使束缚能降低约9–16%；极化子质量增强适中，电子极化子迁移率估计为39–99 cm² V⁻¹ s⁻¹，两相差异不大。

Conclusion: 激子能量学与波函数几何存在部分解耦（partial decoupling），激子几何对结构畸变具有与对称性相关的敏感性，且该变化伴随局域配位与电子色散的改变而非对称性单独作用；激子能量学、激子波函数几何与载流子输运对化学和结构修饰呈现不同响应。

Abstract: The excited-state properties of imide-based defect double antiperovskites AE$_5$AsPn(NH)$_2$ (AE = Ca, Sr; Pn = Sb, Bi) are investigated using spin--orbit-coupled quasiparticle calculations, the model Bethe--Salpeter equation (mBSE), and analytical descriptions of lattice-polarization effects. Across five tetragonal ($P4/mmm$) and monoclinic ($Cm$) structural models, the calculated exciton binding energies range from 140 to 210 meV. Chemical substitution modifies both the binding energies and the hydrogenic estimates of exciton spatial extent. By contrast, the structural distortion from tetragonal $P4/mmm$ ($D_{4h}$) to monoclinic $Cm$ ($C_s$), which removes inversion and fourfold rotational symmetry, produces comparatively modest changes in binding energy but appreciably reshapes the real- and momentum-space exciton distributions. This contrast reveals a partial decoupling between exciton energetics and wavefunction geometry, and identifies a symmetry-associated sensitivity of exciton geometry to structural distortion. The changes are understood to occur together with modifications of local coordination and electronic dispersion, rather than as an isolated effect of symmetry alone. Lattice polarization provides an additional screening channel, reducing the estimated exciton binding energies by approximately 9--16\%. Complementary Fröhlich-polaron calculations yield moderate mass enhancement and model-estimated electron-polaron mobilities of 39--99 cm$^2$ V$^{-1}$ s$^{-1}$, with comparatively modest differences between the two polymorphs. These results highlight the distinct responses of exciton energetics, exciton wavefunction geometry, and carrier transport to chemical and structural modifications.

</details>


### [96] [Designing Perovskites for Chemical Looping Hydrogen Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27580)
*Ivana Staničić,Alfred Juhlin Onbeck,Anders Hellman,Tobias Mattisson,Magnus Rydén*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种结合Wasserstein自编码器（WAE）与深度集成代理模型（deep ensemble surrogate model）的自主主动学习（active learning）框架，用于在钙钛矿（perovskite）庞大的阳离子取代空间中高效筛选高化学链制氢（CLHP）产能材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿因组成灵活、氧化还原性质可调而成为化学链制氢的有前景材料，但阳离子取代带来的巨大组成空间使高性能材料的高通量筛选计算成本高昂，需要更高效的搜索策略。

Method: 采用自主主动学习框架，将Wasserstein自编码器（WAE）与深度集成代理模型相结合，在七次独立运行、每次50个循环中迭代搜索高制氢容量（HPC）的钙钛矿组成，并进一步以多目标排序纳入原材料价格、供应韧性及潜在环境健康影响进行候选筛选。

Result: 七次运行中，所获得组成的平均HPC从初始集的0.41 mmol H₂ g⁻¹提升至2.05 mmol H₂ g⁻¹，最优组成达2.65–2.99 mmol H₂ g⁻¹；所有运行均富集B位Sn，多与Ti组合，A位常见Mg、Ce、K、Y。最终筛选出20个多样化候选材料（2.22–2.84 mmol H₂ g⁻¹）。

Conclusion: 该框架将材料发现与实际部署约束相连接，并具备迁移至其他化学链过程的潜力。

Abstract: Perovskites are promising materials for chemical looping hydrogen production (CLHP) due to their compositional flexibility and tunable redox properties. However, cation substitution opens a vast compositional space, making screening for new high-performing materials computationally demanding. Here, an autonomous active-learning framework combining a Wasserstein autoencoder (WAE) with a deep ensemble surrogate model was used to search for perovskites with a high hydrogen production capacity (HPC). In seven independent runs of 50 cycles, the mean HPC of the acquired compositions rose from 0.41 mmol H$_2$ g$^{-1}$ in the starting set to 2.05 mmol H$_2$ g$^{-1}$, and the best compositions reached 2.65-2.99 mmol H$_2$ g$^{-1}$. All runs enriched Sn on the B-site, mostly combined with Ti and with Mg, Ce, K and Y on the A-site. Twenty diverse candidates (2.22-2.84 mmol H$_2$ g$^{-1}$) were then selected by a multi-objective ranking that also accounts for raw-material price, supply resilience and potential environmental and health impacts. The approach links materials discovery with practical deployment constraints, and the methodology is transferable to other chemical looping processes.

</details>


### [97] [Edge-dislocation interactions with sub 3 nm vacancy and interstitial clusters in FeCr alloys: Mechanisms, Obstacle strength and Fate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27583)
*Layatmika Sahoo,Suchandrima Das*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究用分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟300 K下位错穿过Fe-3Cr/5Cr/10Cr中1–3 nm空位与混合Fe-Cr间隙团簇，给出缺陷特异性障碍强度（obstacle strength）与位错扫过后团簇的存留/吸收结局，指出空位团簇是更强的障碍，且以往拟合TEM可见环得到的α值偏高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fe-Cr合金在透射电子显微镜（transmission electron microscopy, TEM）观察不到缺陷的辐照剂量下即发生硬化，说明存在亚分辨尺寸缺陷；其障碍强度未知，硬化模型因而忽略这些缺陷，仅用可见缺陷拟合实测硬化。

Method: 采用分子动力学（molecular dynamics）在300 K下驱动刃型位错（edge dislocation）穿过Fe-3Cr、Fe-5Cr、Fe-10Cr中经弛豫的1–3 nm空位团簇与混合Fe-Cr间隙团簇，并配合原子追踪（atom tracking）分析位错钉扎（pinning）时Cr的偏聚来源。

Result: 空位团簇弛豫为片状（platelet），强度随尺寸增至2.9 nm时约350 MPa，遵循强障碍标度（strong-obstacle scaling）且与Cr含量无关，且总能存留；间隙团簇弛豫为1/2⟨111⟩、混合或⟨100⟩位错环，多数弱于同尺寸片状缺陷、强度随局部Cr排列变化达五倍且多被吸收，仅Fe-5Cr与Fe-10Cr中的⟨100⟩环常存留。计算的弥散障碍强度（dispersed-barrier strength, α≈0.02–0.25）为上界，低于以往拟合TEM可见环所得的α值。

Conclusion: 小空位团簇一旦形成即为比同尺寸间隙团簇更有效的障碍；障碍强度与存留是两种不同性质，存留的环并不更强；以往基于TEM可见环拟合的α值因忽略TEM不可见缺陷而被高估。这些缺陷特异性强度与归宿为低剂量硬化（low-dose hardening）模型提供物理基础。

Abstract: Fe-Cr alloys harden at irradiation doses where transmission electron microscopy (TEM) shows no defects, implicating defects too small to be resolved. Their obstacle strength is unknown, so hardening models ignore them and fit measured hardening to visible defects alone. Here, molecular dynamics drives an edge dislocation through relaxed 1-3 nm vacancy and mixed Fe-Cr interstitial clusters in Fe-3Cr, Fe-5Cr and Fe-10Cr at 300 K. Vacancy clusters relax into platelets whose strength rises with size to about 350 MPa at 2.9 nm, following strong-obstacle scaling independently of Cr content, and always survive dislocation passage. Interstitial clusters relax into $\frac{1}{2}\langle 111 \rangle$, mixed or $\langle 100 \rangle$ loops. They are mostly weaker than platelets of the same size, their strength varies up to fivefold with the local Cr arrangement, and most are absorbed. Only $\langle 100 \rangle$ loops in Fe-5Cr and Fe-10Cr often survive. During pinning, Cr collects at the junction between these loops and the dislocation; atom tracking for one loop shows that more than half comes from the loop itself. Surviving loops are not stronger than absorbed ones: strength and survival are thus distinct. Computed dispersed-barrier strengths ($α\approx$ 0.02-0.25) are upper bounds that lie below the $α$ values obtained in earlier studies by fitting hardening to TEM-visible loops. These earlier values are therefore inflated by TEM-invisible defects. Wherever small vacancy clusters form, they are more effective obstacles than interstitial clusters of the same size. These defect-specific strengths and fates provide a physical basis for models of low-dose hardening.

</details>


### [98] [Atomistic theory of the $d$-wave phonon angular momentum splitter effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27747)
*Daniel A. Bustamante Lopez,Dominik M. Juraschek*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出反铁磁方格晶格中声子角动量分束效应（phonon angular momentum splitter effect）的原子尺度理论，类比d波交错磁体（altermagnet）中的自旋分束效应，并区分出霍尔型与d_xy波型两类横向响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子可承载固体中的玻色型角动量输运，但其动量空间纹理如何在输运中体现尚不清楚，尤其缺乏对反铁磁体系中声子角动量横向响应的微观理论描述。

Method: 构建棋盘格调制弹性耦合（checkerboard-modulated elastic couplings）的反铁磁方格晶格原子尺度模型，计算温度梯度（temperature gradient）驱动下的声子角动量输运与积累，并将横向响应分解为霍尔（Hall）贡献与d_xy波分束贡献。

Result: 分束贡献需同时具备反铁磁序与棋盘格弹性调制，并呈现特征的d_xy角度依赖；霍尔贡献近似各向同性。两者叠加可实现边缘声子角动量积累的方向性调控。

Conclusion: 声子角动量分束效应构成交错磁体自旋分束的玻色型类比，为固体中角动量输运的定向操控提供理论依据。

Abstract: Phonons offer a bosonic route to angular-momentum transport in solids, but how their momentum-space textures manifest in transport remains unclear. Here we develop an atomistic theory of the phonon angular momentum splitter effect in an antiferromagnetic square lattice with checkerboard-modulated elastic couplings, analogous to the spin-splitter effect in $d$-wave altermagnets. We calculate the transport and accumulation of phonon angular momentum driven by a temperature gradient and find that the transverse response separates into Hall and $d_{xy}$-wave splitter contributions. The splitter contribution requires both the antiferromagnetic order and the checkerboard elastic modulation and exhibits a characteristic $d_{xy}$ angular dependence, whereas the Hall contribution is nearly isotropic. Their combination enables directional control of the edge phonon angular momentum accumulation.

</details>


### [99] [Shift current as a stacking spectroscopy of hexagonal boron nitride multilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27785)
*Sanber Vizcaya,Juan M. Florez,Eric Suárez Morell*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride, hBN）多层堆叠为对象，通过第一性原理（first principles）计算极化与位移电流（shift current）张量，检验“光电流可追踪极化”这一假设，结论是该对应关系仅在面外方向的反转符号上稳健，其余细节需谨慎解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华材料（van der Waals materials）中极性单层的滑动可调控面外极化（out-of-plane polarization），而体光伏效应（bulk photovoltaic effect）被期望作为探测极化的天然光学手段，其前提是光电流随极化变化；该前提尚缺乏系统验证。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，在双层、三层与四层六方氮化硼的全部堆叠构型（stacking landscape）中，计算极化强度与位移电流张量，并比较二者沿堆叠路径的对应关系及光谱响应。

Result: 两者仅共享一条稳健规律：垂直（面外）响应随极化反转，面内响应不随极化反转。此外对应关系存在明显偏差——相同极化的堆叠可具有符号相反的垂直单色光电流；在恒定极化平台上光谱发生重组，甚至在某些光子能量处变号；整条路径中最强的垂直峰出现在极化过零处。

Conclusion: 差异源于结构层面：极化统计的是极性界面数目，而位移电流读取的是这些界面的位置。据此，宽带照明（broadband illumination）可用于识别畴的符号，单色光电流分布图可读取堆叠配准（stacking registry），即体光伏效应在滑动铁电体（sliding ferroelectric）中可作为“堆叠光谱学（stacking spectroscopy）”。

Abstract: Out-of-plane polarization can be tuned by sliding polar monolayers in van der Waals materials; the bulk photovoltaic effect is emerging as a natural optical probe, provided the photocurrent tracks the polarization. We test that assumption by computing the polarization and the shift-current tensor from first principles across the stacking landscape of bi-, tri-, and tetralayer hexagonal boron nitride. The two quantities share one robust property: the vertical response reverses with the polarization while the in-plane one does not. Beyond this rule, the correspondence must be read with caution: stackings with identical polarization carry vertical monochromatic photocurrents of opposite sign; the spectra reorganize and even reverse sign at some photon energies along a constant-polarization plateau; and the strongest vertical peak of the entire pathway sits where the polarization crosses zero. The reason is structural: the polarization counts polar interfaces, while the shift current reads their positions. Broadband illumination reads the domain sign; monochromatic photocurrent maps can read the stacking registry. In a sliding ferroelectric, the bulk photovoltaic effect can operate as a stacking spectroscopy.

</details>


### [100] [Voltage-Controlled Dielectric Cavity Magnonics in Epitaxial Fe Films on SrTiO3 Single Crystal Substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27837)
*Stijn Reniers,Ekaterina Gorokh,Lukas Nulens,Andries Boelen,Christian Haffner,Clement Merckling,Alejandro V. Silhanek,Kristiaan Temst,Joris Van de Vondel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种将本征介质微波腔（intrinsic dielectric microwave cavity）与铁磁介质（ferromagnetic medium）结合的材料异质结构，在低温下实现耦合速率达0.1–1 GHz的磁子-光子相互作用（magnon-photon interaction），并可通过电压有效调控杂化模式谱（hybrid mode spectrum）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔磁子学（cavity magnonics）依赖自旋集体激发与腔模式的杂化，可实现自旋电子器件与微波电路、光信息载体的快速高效耦合；本文旨在构建一种兼具可调性与低功耗的杂化材料平台。

Method: 将本征介质微波腔与铁磁介质集成为材料异质结构，形成磁子-光子耦合体系，并在低温（cryogenic temperatures）下实施电压控制以调控杂化模式谱。

Result: 磁子-光子耦合速率处于0.1–1 GHz量级；低温下通过电压实现对杂化模式谱的有效调控，获得高度可调的光-磁子材料（photo-magnonic material）。

Conclusion: 该可调谐光-磁子材料有望推动高性能、低功耗的低温射频应用，包括混合量子技术（hybrid quantum technology）、超导电子学（superconducting electronics）以及用于非常规计算与信号处理的非线性微波器件。

Abstract: Cavity magnonics relies on the hybridization between collective spin excitations and well-defined photonic or mechanical cavity modes, enabling fast and efficient coupling of spintronic devices to microwave circuits and optical information carriers. In this work, we present a hybrid material heterostructure that combines an intrinsic dielectric microwave cavity with a ferromagnetic medium, establishing magnon-photon interaction with coupling rates in the order of 0.1-1 GHz. Demonstrating effective voltage-control of the hybrid mode spectrum at cryogenic temperatures, we achieve a highly tunable photo-magnonic material, that could provide significant advancements for high-performance and power-efficient cryogenic RF-applications, including hybrid quantum technology, superconducting electronics and nonlinear microwave devices for unconventional computation and signal processing.

</details>


### [101] [Prediction and characterisation of cation topology of pentanary high-entropy alloy (MoWNbTaV)S$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27842)
*Shubham Agarwal,Tahir Wahab,Jesper Byggmästar,Antonio Cammarata,Tomas Polcar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合高斯近似势（Gaussian approximation potential, GAP）与迭代学习，解决了五元(MoWNbTaV)S₂单层中金属排布采样与温度效应难题：振动熵重塑稳定性排序，拉曼/红外谱提供实验指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵过渡金属硫族化合物（high-entropy transition-metal dichalcogenides）性能可由组分调控，但其合成条件下金属排布方式及实验识别手段两个基本问题尚未解决。密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）无法采样庞大的排布空间，且常用的基态能量筛选忽略了合成温度的影响。

Method: 针对五元(MoWNbTaV)S₂单层，利用基于DFT数据迭代学习训练的高斯近似势（Gaussian approximation potential, GAP），并进行吉布斯自由能（Gibbs free energy）计算与纳秒级分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟。

Result: 振动熵（vibrational entropy）重塑了稳定性排序；五种选定构型在高达1300 K的纳秒分子动力学中保持三方棱柱（trigonal-prismatic）骨架。拉曼与红外谱兼有母体单层特征和缺陷激活模式（disorder-activated modes），构成实验识别指纹。预测晶格参数遵循Vegard定律，与溅射合金薄膜的X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）结果在0.8%以内吻合。

Conclusion: XRD一致仅能确认固溶体形成，而区分金属排布需依赖振动指纹。该势函数及其开发流程可支持进一步的原子尺度模拟，并为筛选相关二维高熵材料提供可迁移方法。

Abstract: High-entropy transition-metal dichalcogenides offer properties tuned by composition, yet two basic questions remain open: which arrangement of the metals forms under synthesis conditions, and how it can be recognised in experiment. Density functional theory (DFT) cannot sample the vast space of possible arrangements, and the ground-state energies usually employed to screen them do not take into account the temperatures at which these materials are made. This work solves both issues for the pentanary (MoWNbTaV)S\textsubscript{2} monolayer with a Gaussian approximation potential trained on DFT data by iterative learning. Gibbs free energy calculations reveal that vibrational entropy reshapes the stability ranking, while five selected configurations retain their trigonal-prismatic framework during nanosecond molecular dynamics up to \qty{1300}{\kelvin}. Raman and infrared spectra combine parent-monolayer signatures with disorder-activated modes, providing fingerprints for experimental identification. %
The predicted lattice parameter follows Vegard's law and agrees within \qty{0.8}{\percent} with X{-}ray diffraction on a sputtered alloy film, which identifies the solid solution but not the arrangement of its metals: distinguishing between arrangements requires the vibrational fingerprints. The potential and its development workflow enable further atomistic simulations and offer a transferable approach to screening related two-dimensional high-entropy materials.

</details>


### [102] [Linear Response Theory of Nitrogen K-Edge X-ray Absorption of TCNQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27869)
*Alberto Boccuni,Vita Ilakovac,Stéphane Carniato,Jacques Kontak-Desmarais*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作在绝热含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）的广义Kohn-Sham框架下，采用杂化交换关联泛函，推导出频率相关的单电子约化密度矩阵线性响应的Dyson型方程，并在Crystal程序中实现，用以解释TCNQ分子氮K边NEXAFS谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有NEXAFS谱峰指认多基于基态态密度（DOS），可能不足；需发展包含杂化泛函精确交换（exact exchange）贡献的频率域线性响应方法以正确指认谱峰。

Method: 在广义Kohn-Sham（GKS）绝热TDDFT框架下，由广义Casida方程导出参考体系全频率相关单电子约化密度矩阵线性响应的Dyson型方程，并在Crystal代码中实现；对TCNQ的N K边NEXAFS按递增复杂度的构建模块逐级分解谱，分析π共轭、多个等价吸收中心及环结构对π∥与π⊥共振的影响，并与角分辨实验对比。

Result: 理论结果与角分辨实验一致；与仅基于基态态密度的早期指认不同，最低能共振主要由基态KS LUMO贡献主导；与独立粒子近似（independent-particle approximation）比较表明，核中精确交换贡献对谱峰正确指认至关重要。

Conclusion: 杂化泛函GKS-TDDFT的频率域线性响应实现可可靠指认NEXAFS谱；精确交换项不可忽略，基态DOS式指认需修正。

Abstract: The linear response of the density is computed in the frequency domain within the generalized Kohn--Sham framework of adiabatic time-dependent density functional theory, employing hybrid exchange-correlation functionals. It is shown that the generalized Casida equations imply a Dyson-like equation for the linear-response of the full frequency-dependent one-electron reduced density matrix of the reference GKS system. We implement this formalism in the \textsc{Crystal} code and apply it to the nitrogen K-edge near-edge X-ray absorption fine structure (NEXAFS) of 7,7,8,8-tetracyano-$p$-quinodimethane (TCNQ), the acceptor in the charge-transfer salt TTF-TCNQ. We interpret the spectrum through a step-by-step construction by building blocks of increasing complexity. This procedure separates the effects of $π$ conjugation, of multiple equivalent absorbing centers and of the ring on the in-plane ($π_\parallel$) and out-of-plane ($π_\perp$) resonances. The theoretical result is confirmed by a comparison with angle-resolved experiments. In contrast with earlier assignments based only on the ground-state density of states, we find that the lowest-energy resonance is dominated by the ground-state KS LUMO contribution. Indeed, comparison with the independent-particle approximation shows that the exact-exchange contribution to the kernel is essential for a correct assignment of the peaks.

</details>


### [103] [Group-III doping of the p-type oxide SnO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27938)
*Nicola Gutmann,Georg Hoffmann,Aidan Flynn Campbell,Jonas Laehnemann,Oliver Bierwagen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy）生长 Al、Ga、In 掺杂的 SnO 薄膜，Hall 测量表明三种 III 族掺杂剂均呈受主（acceptor）特性，其中 Al 的受主行为与既有理论预测的施主（donor）特性相矛盾，掺杂极限约在中等 10¹⁹ cm⁻³ 量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验 III 族元素（Al、Ga、In）在 SnO 中的掺杂行为与导电类型，尤其是澄清 Al 掺杂的理论预测（施主）与实验观测之间的分歧，并探究 SnO 缺陷化学（defect chemistry）的基本限制。

Method: 采用分子束外延生长 Al、Ga、In 掺杂 SnO 薄膜，通过 Hall 测量（Hall measurements）判定导电类型与载流子浓度；测试 10¹⁸–10²² cm⁻³ 的掺杂浓度范围；并额外生长一组在氧化气氛（oxidizing atmosphere）下的 Al 掺杂样品以稳定 Al³⁺ 氧化态。

Result: 三种掺杂剂均表现受主特性，与 Ga、In 的理论计算和以往实验一致，但与预测 Al 为施主的理论计算相矛盾。Al、Ga、In 的最大空穴浓度（hole concentration）分别为 2×10¹⁹、3×10¹⁹、1×10¹⁹ cm⁻³，掺杂效率（doping efficiency）分别为 3%、9%、1%；中等 10¹⁹ cm⁻³ 的掺杂极限与文献中其他掺杂剂的极限一致。

Conclusion: 鉴于 Al 意外的受主行为，作者假设样品中存在 Al¹⁺ 而非 Al³⁺；然而在氧化气氛下生长的 Al 掺杂 SnO 仍呈 p 型（p-type），故需以更复杂的缺陷化学来解释 Al 的受主特性。

Abstract: Al-, Ga-, and In-doped tin monoxide (SnO) films were grown by molecular beam epitaxy. Hall measurements reveal acceptor character of all investigated group-III dopants in SnO, which correspond to theoretical calculations and previous experiments for Ga and In, but contradicts theoretical calculations predicting donor character for Al in SnO. Doping concentrations of 10e18-1e22 cm-3 were tested and maximum hole concentrations of 2e19, 3e19, and 1e19 cm-3 with doping efficiencies of 3 percent, 9 percent, and 1 percent were found for Al, Ga, and In, respectively. The observed doping limit in the mid e19 cm-3 aligns with the limits observed in literature for different dopants, hinting to fundamental aspects of the SnO defect chemistry. Due to the unexpected Al acceptor behavior, we hypothesized Al1+ being present instead of Al3+. To stabilize the higher Al3+ oxidation state, another sample series of Al-doped SnO was grown in an oxidizing atmosphere. Despite the oxidizing atmosphere, these samples showed p-type character. A more complex defect chemistry is thus expected to explain the Al acceptor character.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Infection Spreading and Stochastic Extinction in Finite Spatially Inhomogeneous Susceptible-Infected-Susceptible and Susceptible-Infected-Recovered Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26792)
*Sara Shabani,Uwe C. Täuber*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 通过蒙特卡洛模拟研究两个子系统空间耦合如何改变非均匀二维方格子（square lattices）上SIS与SIR模型的随机灭绝（stochastic extinction）与传播行为，发现入侵前沿可重新激活脆弱区域并建立长寿命活跃态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限种群中人口统计涨落（demographic fluctuations）可使系统进入无病的吸收态（absorbing disease-free state），即便对应的无限系统支持持续活动；因此需理解空间耦合的两个子系统如何影响这一随机灭绝过程。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations），在非均匀二维方格子上考察SIS与SIR模型，并构建扩散耦合（diffusively coupled）、传播特性不同的SIS-SIS、SIR-SIS与SIR-SIR三种耦合构型；用灭绝时间、遍历时间（traversal times）与空间关联测量进行分析，并通过一段时间后解耦（decoupling）检验自治持续性。

Result: 孤立SIS系统即使在较小规模下，灭绝时间在疫情阈值附近亦呈近似幂律标度（power-law scaling）；入侵前沿可在初始感染消失后重新激活脆弱的SIS区域并建立长寿命活跃态，且可由持续的SIS源或单次瞬态SIR波诱发。空间关联在平行于子系统界面方向尤为显著；解耦实验表明自治持续需要超过首次界面到达的足够耦合时间，系统尺寸增大会延迟入侵但提高最终成功稳定的概率；两个SIR区域耦合时可引发宏观二次暴发，但永久免疫使两种疫情均局限于瞬态活动。

Conclusion: 传播活动通过产生扩展的关联感染种群（correlated infectious population），为重新扩散提供有利初始条件；耦合区域的持续性与入侵延迟及耦合时间相关，而免疫特性（SIR）限制了持续活跃态的形成。

Abstract: Demographic fluctuations can drive finite epidemic populations into an absorbing disease-free state even when the corresponding infinite system supports sustained activity. By means of Monte Carlo simulations, we investigate how spatial coupling of two distinct subsystems modifies stochastic extinction and epidemic spreading in susceptible-infectious-susceptible (SIS) and susceptible-infectious-recovered (SIR) models on inhomogeneous two-dimensional square lattices. For isolated SIS systems, extinction times exhibit approximate power-law scaling near the epidemic threshold even for small systems. Traversal times characterize the distinct propagation dynamics of SIS and SIR infection clusters. We consider diffusively coupled regions with different transmission characteristics in coupled SIS-SIS, SIR-SIS, and SIR-SIR configurations. Invading fronts can reactivate a vulnerable SIS region after its initial infection has disappeared and establish a long-lived active epidemic state. This stabilization can be induced by either a sustained SIS source or a single transient SIR epidemic wave. Spatial correlation measurements support a mechanism in which the invading activity generates an extended correlated infectious population, effectively providing favorable initial conditions for renewed epidemic spreading. Correlations develop particularly strongly parallel to the subsystem interfaces. Decoupling the regions after some elapsed period demonstrates that autonomous persistence requires sufficient coupling time beyond the first interface arrival for this correlated population to become established. Increasing system size delays invasion but enhances the probability of ultimate successful stabilization. In two coupled SIR regions, transmission can initiate a macroscopic secondary outbreak, while permanent immunity restricts both epidemics to transient activity.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [A posteriori estimates for inverse evolutionary problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26678)
*Sergey Repin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文针对不适定演化问题提出后验误差估计框架，以泛函型估计量化近似解与"邻近"适定问题精确解之间的距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hadamard意义下的不适定演化问题可能无解且近似极不稳定，适用于适定问题的后验误差估计概念失效，需重新表述。

Method: 采用泛函型后验估计（a posteriori estimates of the functional type），构造可计算量，估计近似解与同一微分方程对应的"邻近"适定初边值问题（well-posed initial boundary value problem）精确解之间的距离；该邻近问题的解精确满足部分条件，其余条件以精度ε近似满足。该方法用于反向抛物问题、非局部时间条件问题、Cauchy抛物问题及参数识别问题。

Result: 所得估计不仅控制近似解的精度，还为数值计算提供了统一途径。

Conclusion: 泛函型后验估计为不适定演化问题的误差分析与数值求解提供了统一框架。

Abstract: The paper is focused on a posteriori error analysis of evolutionary problems, which are ill-posed in the sense of Hadamard. They may have no solutions and respective approximations may be highly unstable. For these problems, the a posteriori error estimation concept applicable to well-posed problems is not valid and requires a significant reformulation. A posteriori estimates of the functional type provide the basis for addressing this issue. They generate computable quantities that estimate the distance between a function (approximate solution to the inverse problem) and exact solution of a "close" well-posed initial boundary value problem associated with the same differential equation. The solution of this close problem exactly satisfies one part of the imposed conditions and the others are satisfied approximately with the accuracy $\eps$. This idea is elaborated for several evolutionary problems generated by the reaction-convection-diffusion equation: backward parabolic problem, problem with non-local time conditions, Cauchy parabolic problem, and parameter(s) identification problem. The estimates derived not only control the accuracy of approximate solutions, but also suggest a unified approach to calculating them numerically.

</details>


### [106] [Inverse scattering for the one-dimensional discrete Schrödinger operator: a computational perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27049)
*Peter C. Gibson,Basant Lal Sharma*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文从计算视角重新审视紧支撑扰动下一维离散薛定谔算子的逆散射问题，提出以正交多项式表示散射数据的新框架，并给出由数字采样反射系数重建 Jacobi 矩阵及扰动最小支撑区间长度的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典逆散射理论多为解析性结果，难以直接适配反射系数被数字采样（digitally sampled）的实际计算场景；本文旨在建立适用于采样数据的计算框架，同时确定扰动的最小支撑区间长度。

Method: 以正交多项式（orthogonal polynomials）重新表示散射数据，并利用由直线上相邻正交多项式对构造的圆周上的多项式（polynomials on the circle）作为关键技术工具，构建逆散射计算算法。

Result: 获得了以 Jacobi 矩阵形式表示扰动的重建方法，并可计算支撑扰动的最小区间长度；算法通过数值算例（numerical examples）得到演示验证。

Conclusion: 正交多项式表示与圆周多项式为采样反射系数下的离散逆散射提供了可行的计算途径，数值实验支持该算法的有效性。

Abstract: We revisit inverse scattering for the one-dimensional discrete Schrödinger operator with compactly supported perturbation. We consider a computational perspective, adapted to the case of a digitally sampled reflection coefficient, to determine the perturbation in the form of a Jacobi matrix, including a computation of the length of the smallest interval supporting the perturbation. A new representation of scattering data in terms of orthogonal polynomials underpins the computational results. A key technical role is played by polynomials on the circle constructed from pairs of successive orthogonal polynomials on the line. The inverse algorithm is demonstrated on numerical examples.

</details>


### [107] [Convex Structure-Preserving EDMD: Passive Koopman Matrices as $Q$-Contractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27730)
*Matthias Ehrhardt,Achraf Zinihi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究端口-哈密顿（port-Hamiltonian, pH）系统的Koopman算子数据驱动逼近，提出基于lifted pH模型隐式中点离散的结构保持型EDMD。所提方法以半定规划（semidefinite program, SDP）求解，可精确保持能量平衡、无源性与线性Casimir不变量，并给出误差认证界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展动态模态分解（extended dynamic mode decomposition, EDMD）用有限观测集构造矩阵逼近Koopman算子，但其最小二乘矩阵对端口-哈密顿（pH）系统一般不具无源性（passivity）也不保持不变量，导致模型可能生成能量、丢失Casimir，甚至在噪声数据下发散。

Method: 作者证明lifted pH模型的隐式中点离散对任意步长精确保持能量平衡、无源性与全部线性Casimir；固定存储度量（storage metric）Q时，所得Koopman矩阵为(J−R)Q的Cayley变换（J反对称、R半正定），该集合与步长无关且为Q-压缩（Q-contraction）的闭包。约束集可由线性矩阵不等式（linear matrix inequality, LMI）表示，从而将端口约束（离散时间Kalman-Yakubovich-Popov条件）与Casimir约束（仿射约束）纳入EDMD，形成半定规划。

Result: 约束估计是未约束估计在数据加权范数下的投影（projection），当总体目标与pH相容时不增加估计误差；对每个Q-压缩，lifted预测误差的认证界至多随时间线性增长，而未约束模型在Q增益大于1时呈几何增长。阻尼摆与阻尼刚体基准显示约束模型不产生能量、Casimir保持至求解器精度（约10⁻⁸），而EDMD在噪声数据下可能发散或丢失Casimir；但当Q取物理能量时，约束模型精度低于EDMD，且可能出现显著结构偏差（structural bias）。

Conclusion: 结构保持EDMD可表述为半定规划，在理论上保证无源性、能量平衡与Casimir守恒并给出误差界，基准实验验证了其鲁棒的结构保持性；代价是在Q取物理能量时精度不及EDMD并存在结构偏差，体现精度与结构一致性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Extended dynamic mode decomposition (EDMD) approximates the Koopman operator using a matrix based on a finite set of observables. However, the least-squares matrix of a port-Hamiltonian (pH) system is generally neither passive nor invariant-preserving. We demonstrate that the implicit midpoint discretization of a lifted pH model precisely preserves energy balance, passivity, and all linear Casimirs for every step size. For a fixed storage metric $Q$, the resulting Koopman matrices are the Cayley transforms of $(J-R)Q$, where $J$ is skew-symmetric and $R$ is positive semidefinite. This set does not depend on the step size and is the closure of all Q-contractions. This set is LMI-representable; therefore, structure-preserving EDMD, including ports through a discrete-time Kalman-Yakubovich-Popov condition and Casimirs through affine constraints, is a semidefinite program. The constrained estimate is a projection of the unconstrained estimate in the data-weighted norm and cannot increase estimation error when the population target is pH-compatible. For every $Q$-contraction, a certified bound on the lifted prediction error grows linearly at most in time, whereas it grows geometrically for unconstrained models with Q-gain above one. Benchmarks on a damped pendulum and a damped rigid body demonstrate that constrained models never generate energy and that imposed Casimirs are maintained with solver accuracy (approximately $10^{-8}$). In contrast, EDMD may diverge or lose the Casimir with noisy data. The benchmarks also demonstrate that when $Q$ is the physical energy, the constrained models are less accurate than EDMD and can exhibit significant structural bias.

</details>

### [108] [Spectral and dynamical properties of quasiperiodically kicked staggered chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27455)
*Sanghoon Lee,Kyoung-Min Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究由Fibonacci序交变势脉冲驱动的一维晶格，通过周期性重复有限段的准能谱（quasienergy spectrum）推断非重复驱动下的行为，揭示时间序（temporal ordering）控制带间振荡与上带布居时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非周期（Fibonacci序）驱动缺乏平移周期性，其准能谱难以直接定义，因此需要探讨：周期性重复有限段所得到的准能谱，能对非重复驱动提供哪些信息。

Method: 对一维晶格施加Fibonacci序交变势脉冲串；计算周期性重复有限段时的准能谱与带间振荡，并与非重复驱动下的动力学进行对比；分析特殊动量处偶数步与奇数步脉冲计数之差对带间布居的精确决定作用。

Result: 弱脉冲下，单一时间傅里叶振幅（temporal Fourier amplitude）可同时预测准能隙与带间振荡频率；随段长增大能隙趋于零，但非重复驱动下的振荡频率趋于有限极限耦合。非重复驱动下，失配量（imbalance）的最大幅度随时间对数增长；弱脉冲下首次达到固定上带布居的时间随脉冲强度倒数指数增长，而在固定偶数段重复驱动下仅线性增长。

Conclusion: 时间序（temporal ordering）是控制带间振荡及上带布居所需时间的关键因素；周期重复段与非重复Fibonacci驱动在布居时间标度上呈现指数与线性的本质差异。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional lattice driven by Fibonacci-ordered kicks of a staggered potential. We ask what the quasienergy spectra of periodically repeated finite segments reveal about the nonrepeating drive. For weak kicks, one temporal Fourier amplitude predicts both the quasienergy gap and the frequency of interband oscillations under periodic repetition. As the segment grows, the gap tends to zero, but a finite limiting coupling sets the oscillation frequency under the nonrepeating drive. At special momenta, the difference between kick counts on even and odd steps determines the exact interband population at any kick strength. Under the nonrepeating drive, the maximum magnitude of this imbalance grows logarithmically with time. For weak kicks, the time to first reach a fixed upper-band population therefore grows exponentially in the inverse kick strength. Under repetition of a fixed even-length segment, it grows linearly in the inverse kick strength. Temporal ordering thus controls interband oscillations and the time needed to populate the upper band.

</details>


### [109] [From non-universal spectra at the RS-RSB transition to the universal $ω^4$ regime inside the glassy phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27564)
*Tommaso Pettinari,Edan Lerner,Gustavo Düring*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文利用平均场（mean-field）KHGPS 模型揭示，结构玻璃中普适的 ω⁴ 低频非声子态密度（nonphononic density of states）源自玻璃态（glassy phase）及复制对称破缺（replica-symmetry-broken, RSB）相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出，结构玻璃（structural glasses）中普适的低频非声子态密度 𝒟_G(ω)∼ω⁴ 虽被广泛观测，但其起源仍缺乏理解，本文旨在探究其物理来源。

Method: 采用描述相互作用非谐振子（interacting anharmonic oscillators）的平均场（mean-field）KHGPS 模型进行分析，并构建一个简化变体模型以刻画不同区域间的交叉行为（crossover）。

Result: 结果表明，普适的四次（quartic）谱深深植根于 RSB 玻璃相；而复制对称（replica-symmetric, RS）相边界处的无能隙态密度（gapless density of states）则非普适，敏感依赖于淬火无序（quenched disorder）的细节。此外，适当选择淬火无序可抑制伴随发散磁化率的传统类自旋玻璃（spin-glass-like）连续 RS-RSB 转变，从而在无外场条件下实现不连续转变。

Conclusion: 研究建立了普适 ω⁴ 谱的出现与玻璃相之间的直接联系，并为探索玻璃低温性质及由双稳局域势（bistable local potentials）产生的软局域激发（soft localized excitations）提供了简单框架。

Abstract: The universal low-frequency nonphononic density of states $\mathcal{D}_\mathrm{G}(ω)\simω^4$ is a robust feature of structural glasses, yet its origin remains poorly understood. Here we investigate this question using the mean-field KHGPS model of interacting anharmonic oscillators. We show that the universal quartic spectrum is deeply rooted in the glassy, replica-symmetry-broken (RSB) phase, whereas the gapless density of states found at the boundary of the replica-symmetric (RS) phase is non-universal and depends sensitively on the details of the quenched disorder. We formulate a simplified variant of the KHGPS model that allows us to characterize the crossover between these two regimes and provides a physical interpretation of the associated crossover frequency scale. We further show that the conventional, spin-glass-like, continuous RS-RSB transition, accompanied by diverging susceptibilities, can be suppressed by an appropriate choice of the quenched disorder, allowing for a discontinuous RS-RSB transition even in the absence of an external field. Our results establish a direct connection between the emergence of the universal $ω^4$ spectrum and the glassy phase, and provide a simple framework for exploring the low-temperature properties of glasses and the soft localized excitations that emerge from bistable local potentials.

</details>


### [110] [Cyclic perturbations encode memories in trained neural networks without loss of performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27647)
*Max Kerr Winter,Liesbeth M. C. Janssen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 对已训练的过参数化深度神经网络（overparameterized deep neural network）施加循环扰动，可在权重结构中编码扰动历史并随后读取，且不影响原任务性能；受循环剪切悬浮液（cyclically sheared suspensions）启发提出的含噪写入协议可长期保留多条记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序材料（disordered materials）具有记忆循环扰动历史的现象，而人工神经网络（artificial neural networks）在数学结构与动力学上与无序物理系统相似，其是否具备此类记忆此前未知。

Method: 对训练好的过参数化深度网络执行循环写入：每轮写入使所有权重发生位移，随后通过网络梯度下降（gradient descent）松弛；并借鉴循环剪切悬浮液，引入含噪写入（noisy-write）协议以在长时间尺度上保留所有编码记忆。

Result: 重复循环使网络进入对不超过写入幅度（write amplitude）的扰动可逆的状态，编码值可由不可逆权重运动的起始点读出；确定性写入下多条记忆中长时间仅最大者留存，含噪协议则可全部保留；两种写入方式均未可测量地影响网络在原学习任务未见数据上的性能。

Conclusion: 循环记忆（cyclic memory）是无序物理系统与深度神经网络共有的现象，网络可在不损害原有功能的前提下利用此前未被探索的记忆能力。

Abstract: Many disordered materials exhibit a remarkable form of memory: they retain information about the history of cyclic perturbations. Whether this phenomenon also occurs in artificial neural networks, whose mathematical structure and dynamics share many similarities with disordered physical systems, is unknown. Here we show that cyclically perturbing a trained, overparameterized deep neural network encodes information in the structure of the weights that can subsequently be retrieved, thus retaining memory of the perturbation history. During each write cycle, all weights are displaced, after which the network relaxes through gradient descent. Repeated cycles organise the neural network into a state that is reversible for perturbations up to the write amplitude, allowing the encoded value to be read from the onset of irreversible weight motion. Multiple memories can also be encoded, although under deterministic writing only the largest persists at long times. Inspired by cyclically sheared suspensions, we introduce a novel noisy-write protocol that preserves all encoded memories at long times. Remarkably, neither deterministic nor noisy memory writing measurably affects the network's performance on unseen data from its original learning task. Our results establish cyclic memory as a phenomenon shared by disordered physical systems and deep neural networks, and show that networks can exploit as yet unexplored memory capabilities without compromising their original function.

</details>


### [111] [Minkowski Correlation Functions (MCF): Long-range, robust shape descriptors for point patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27948)
*Dominik Pabst,Michael A. Klatt,Klaus Mecke*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 论文提出“环状闵可夫斯基相关函数（ring-based Minkowski correlation functions, MCFs）”，将每个点赋予球壳（ring），并用闵可夫斯基泛函（Minkowski functionals）量化由此形成的两相形态，从而获得兼具稳健性与敏感性的无序点模式（disordered point patterns）形状描述子，并在多尺度应用中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闵可夫斯基泛函形态学分析对点模式而言是“短视的（short-sighted）”，而相关函数（correlation functions）又不能提供稳健、可加的（additive）形状描述子；研究旨在结合两类标准技术的优势并克服其缺陷。

Method: 为每个点分配球壳，并通过闵可夫斯基泛函量化所得两相形态，定义环状闵可夫斯基相关函数；同时给出一个精确且高效的平面点模式算法，将既有的基于圆盘（disk-based）的形态学方法扩展到长程尺度。

Result: MCFs 可跨多个长度尺度捕捉结构信息，在传统圆盘式闵可夫斯基泛函已饱和的半径处仍保持敏感性，并隐式编码超越对相关函数（pair correlation）与结构因子（structure factor）的高阶相关。在活性胶体（Quincke rollers）、长叶松（longleaf pine）与哈勃超深场（Hubble Ultra Deep Field）的应用中，覆盖面积、边界长度与欧拉示性数（Euler characteristic）相对均匀泊松参考（homogeneous Poisson reference）呈现清晰且系统的偏离，且响应各异。

Conclusion: 该方法兼具计算时间短、抗噪性强、边缘处理简便的特点，并可解释为以外环半径（outer ring radius）为变量的低维函数，具有清晰的可解释性。

Abstract: A morphometric analysis of point patterns using Minkowski functionals is short-sighted, whereas correlation functions do not provide robust, i.e., additive shape descriptors. To combine the advantages of both standard techniques and to overcome their weaknesses, we introduce ring-based Minkowski correlation functions (MCFs) as robust and sensitive descriptors of disordered point patterns. By assigning to each point a spherical shell and quantifying the resulting two-phase morphology via Minkowski functionals, MCFs capture structural information across multiple length scales, remain sensitive at radii where conventional disk-based Minkowski functionals have saturated, and implicitly encode higher-order correlations beyond the pair correlation and structure factor. We present an exact and efficient algorithm for planar patterns extending the established disk-based morphological approach to long ranges. Applications across scales -- active colloids (Quincke rollers), longleaf pine trees, and the Hubble Ultra Deep Field -- demonstrate clear and systematic deviations from homogeneous Poisson references, with distinct responses of covered area, boundary length, and Euler characteristic. The approach combines short computation times, robustness to noise, and straightforward edge handling with clear interpretability as low-dimensional functions of the outer ring radius.

</details>
