### [112] [Feynman integrals, coactions and Lie-algebra representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26679)
*Emiel Claasen,Mehregan Doroudiani,Claude Duhr,Axel Kleinschmidt,Franca Lippert,Oliver Schlotterer,Yoann Sohnle*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文建立费曼积分（Feynman integrals）的规范微分方程（canonical differential equations）与取值于特定李代数（Lie algebras）的平坦联络（flat connections）矩阵表示之间的联系，并由此推出单值解（single-valued solutions）与余作用（coactions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于利用弦论（string theory）洞见与李代数相关周期（periods）的性质，为费曼积分的微分方程结构及其解提供代数化、表示论式的统一刻画。

Method: 方法上，将规范微分方程与平坦联络在李代数中的矩阵表示相对应，借助周期性质推导单值解与积分的余作用，并提出一种与李代数表示论相联系的新型微分方程形式，该形式可在受控方式下偏离 ε-分解（ε-factorisation）。

Result: 所得结果为费曼积分的单值解及余作用关系，以及一种可受控偏离 ε-分解的新微分方程表述；文中以相关示例说明该形式体系。

Conclusion: 结论是规范微分方程与李代数平坦联络表示之间存在结构性联系，为费曼积分的解与余作用提供新框架，并允许在控制下推广超出 ε-分解的标准形式。

Abstract: We outline a link between canonical differential equations of Feynman integrals and matrix representations of flat connections taking values in certain Lie algebras. Leveraging insights from string theory and properties of periods associated with these Lie algebras, we deduce single-valued solutions to the differential equations and coactions for the integrals. We introduce a novel form of the differential equations which connects with the representation theory of the Lie algebras and may depart from $\varepsilon$-factorisation in a controlled way. We illustrate the formalism through relevant examples.

</details>


### [113] [A matrix model of an M5--brane on a plane--wave background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26687)
*Carmine Montella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了平面波背景下 A₆ 矩阵模型的极大超对称形变，并通过 M5 膜约化独立导出该理论，分类其真空，确认其中一个为五膜巨引力子且涨落构成单个 su(2|4) 多重态，同时发现流量与膜尺寸平衡的新真空族并指出 BLG 理论内嵌于该模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对近期提出的平面波背景上 A₆ 矩阵模型，需要构建其极大超对称形变（maximally supersymmetric deformation），并厘清其真空结构与与其他膜理论（BMN、BLG）的关系。

Method: 一方面直接构造该极大超对称形变理论；另一方面在此背景下将光锥 M5 膜（light-cone M5-brane）于五维球面（five-sphere）上约化至最低谐波，包含手征二形式（chiral two-form）与费米子的动力学，从而独立导出同一理论；随后分类所有经典真空（classical vacua）。

Result: 证明其中一个真空为五膜巨引力子（fivebrane giant graviton），其涨落形成单个 su(2|4) 多重态（multiplet），重现 BMN 理论大 N 极限下球形横向五膜（spherical transverse fivebrane）的 ℓ=1 能级；动力学问量张量（dynamical tensor）还支持流量平衡膜尺寸的新真空族，其谱包含模糊膜（fuzzy membrane）谱；BLG 理论（BLG theory）同时出现在无质量理论的库仑分支（Coulomb branch）与有质量理论的巨引力子附近（两膜被极化为五膜）。

Conclusion: 该 A₆ 模型统一包含了巨引力子、模糊膜与 BLG 理论等结构：五膜巨引力子涨落对应 su(2|4) 单个多重态并与 BMN 结果衔接，新真空族拓展了谱结构，而 BLG 理论作为模型的一部分在两类情形下嵌入其中。

Abstract: We construct the maximally supersymmetric deformation of the recently proposed $\mathcal{A}_6$ matrix model on a plane--wave background. We derive the same theory independently, by reducing the light--cone M5--brane on a five--sphere in this background to its lowest harmonics, including the dynamics of its chiral two--form and of its fermions. We then classify all its classical vacua. We show that one of them is the fivebrane giant graviton, and that its fluctuations form a single $\mathfrak{su}(2|4)$ multiplet, which reproduces the $\ell=1$ level of the spherical transverse fivebrane identified in the large $N$ limit of BMN theory. Moreover, the dynamical tensor supports new families of vacua, where the flux balances the size of the brane, and whose spectrum contains that of a fuzzy membrane. Finally, we show that BLG theory sits inside the model, both on a Coulomb branch of the massless theory and around the giant graviton of the massive one, where the two membranes are polarized into the fivebrane.

</details>


### [114] [Onset of quantum chaos in a family of two-dimensional conformal field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26694)
*Felix M. Haehl,Moshe Rozali*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在二维共形场论（conformal field theory）的标度维度（scaling dimensions）中发现随机矩阵（random matrix）统计，并对存在隐藏对称性时的偏离作了定量刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非BPS原初算符（non-BPS primary operators）标度维度中是否出现随机矩阵统计，并检验量子混沌（quantum chaos）特征在含隐藏对称性（hidden symmetry）的超对称sigma模型中的表现。

Method: 考察K3靶空间、具有N=(4,4)超对称的sigma模型在Kummer orbifold点附近的行为；在自由费米子半径处，对Z_2^4不变扇区的零自旋谱，沿四维边缘形变（marginal deformation）方向（以单位四元数quaternion参数化）计算简并能级的一阶劈裂，即领头反常维度（anomalous dimension），并用多种谱诊断量检验其统计。

Result: 在所有精确对称性（含偶数能级处隐藏的Z_2）都被分辨的扇区中，沿一般方向的一阶非零位移服从高斯正交系综（Gaussian orthogonal ensemble, GOE）；而已知的单态形变（singlet deformation）即便分辨所有已知对称性后仍保留精确简并，其能隙比（gap ratio）仅随能量缓慢趋近GOE值，展开谱（unfolded spectrum）缺乏GOE的长程刚性（long-range rigidity）。

Conclusion: 标度维度中的随机矩阵统计可在特定超对称模型中涌现，但隐藏对称性与增强对称性会显著改变谱统计特征，因此需在完全分辨对称性后才能与GOE比对。

Abstract: We exhibit random matrix statistics in the scaling dimensions of non-BPS primary operators in a family of two-dimensional conformal field theories. Specifically, we consider the sigma model with K3 target space and $\mathcal N=(4,4)$ supersymmetry in a neighborhood of the Kummer orbifold point $\mathbb T^4/\mathbb Z_2$. At the free-fermion radius, the spin-zero spectrum of the $\mathbb Z_2^4$-invariant sector is exactly degenerate at integer energies. The exactly marginal blow-up deformations that preserve this symmetry form a four-dimensional space, whose directions we parametrize by unit quaternions. Along each deformation direction, we compute the first-order splitting of the degenerate levels, i.e., the leading anomalous dimensions as the coupling goes to zero. In sectors where all exact symmetries are resolved, including a hidden $\mathbb Z_2$ at the even levels, the non-zero first-order shifts along a generic direction follow the Gaussian orthogonal ensemble (GOE), as we verify by computing several spectral diagnostics. We compare quantitatively with the well-known singlet deformation, which has enhanced symmetry: even after all its known symmetries are resolved, its first-order spectrum has exact degeneracies, its gap ratio approaches the GOE value only slowly as the energy increases, and its unfolded spectrum lacks the long-range rigidity of the GOE.

</details>


### [115] [Energy and Charge Transmission at Conformal Interfaces: Relations and Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27002)
*Yuma Furuta,Yuya Kusuki,Toshiki Onagi,Yin Tang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文针对保持对角仿射代数（diagonal affine algebra）的 Wess–Zumino–Witten（WZW）模型共形界面，导出能量与电荷透射系数之间的精确关系，并据此给出界面无关的热电导比（广义 Wiedemann–Franz 定律）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性限制了能量与电荷通过共形界面（conformal interface）时能否独立传输，需要有定量的判据来刻画这种约束，并厘清不同能级（level）下完全能量透射是否可能。

Method: 考察 $G_{k_L}$ 与 $G_{k_R}$ 两个 WZW 模型之间保持对角仿射代数的界面，推导透射系数的精确关系 $c_{LR}=2(\dim G)k_{LR}/(k_L+k_R+2h^\vee)$，并结合出射能量正定性（positivity）以及陪集（coset）中心荷给出的界进行分析。

Result: 当能级不等时，该关系排除完全能量透射；引入额外体自由度（bulk degrees of freedom）后等式变为由陪集中心荷控制的界。在线性响应（linear response）下，该关系将热导与电荷导之比固定为两侧体材料比值（bulk ratios）的调和平均，且与界面无关，陪集部分则对固定电荷导下的热导给出上界。

Conclusion: 该与界面无关的比值构成 WZW 模型间共形界面的广义 Wiedemann–Franz 定律；电荷透射系数 $k_{LR}$ 同时支配平衡电荷涨落（equilibrium charge fluctuations），并确定边界条件改变算符（boundary-condition-changing operator）标度维数对旋转角（rotation angle）的领头二次依赖。

Abstract: Symmetry constrains how independently energy and charge can pass through a conformal interface. For interfaces between Wess--Zumino--Witten (WZW) models $G_{k_L}$ and $G_{k_R}$ preserving the diagonal affine algebra, we derive an exact relation between the energy and charge transmission coefficients, $c_{LR}=2(\dim G)k_{LR}/(k_L+k_R+2h^\vee)$. Together with positivity of outgoing energy, this relation excludes complete energy transmission at unequal levels. With additional bulk degrees of freedom, the equality is replaced by bounds controlled by the coset central charges. Complete transmission from a lower-level WZW model then imposes a lower bound on the coset central charge on the other side. In linear response, the relation fixes the ratio of thermal to charge conductance to the harmonic mean of the two bulk ratios, independently of the interface, while coset sectors bound the thermal conductance at fixed charge conductance. This interface-independent ratio is a generalized Wiedemann--Franz law for conformal interfaces between WZW models. The charge transmission coefficient $k_{LR}$ also governs equilibrium charge fluctuations and fixes the leading quadratic dependence of the boundary-condition-changing operator's scaling dimension on the rotation angle.

</details>


### [116] [Perturbative $S$-matrix Unitarity in Higher-Derivative Theory of Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27021)
*Yugo Abe,Takeo Inami,Keisuke Izumi,Shinsuke Kawai*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造含四费米相互作用（four-Fermi interactions）的高阶导数费米子理论（higher-derivative fermion theory），通过详细分析 S-矩阵（S-matrix）结构考察其是否满足树级幺正性判据（tree-unitarity criterion），并论证费米子鬼态（fermionic ghost states）必然导致负范数态，但该理论仍满足微扰 S-矩阵幺正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论中有一个猜想：树级散射振幅良好的高能行为意味着可重整化（renormalizability），即树级幺正性判据。作者希望检验高阶导数费米子理论是否满足该判据，并将此前关于高阶导数标量理论（higher-derivative scalar theories）的结论推广至费米子情形。

Method: 构建带四费米相互作用的高阶导数费米子理论，并详细考察其 S-矩阵结构。由于高阶导数动能项，旋量场（spinor field）的质量量纲（mass dimension）不同于常规费米子，使四费米相互作用可重整化，代价是引入鬼态。

Result: 与玻色子鬼态（bosonic ghosts）不同，费米子鬼态无论其能量本征值符号如何，都必然产生负范数态（negative-norm states）。同时，与常规四费米理论相比，高阶导数四费米理论满足微扰 S-矩阵幺正性（perturbative S-matrix unitarity）。

Conclusion: 结合树级幺正性判据，上述结果为高阶导数费米子理论的可重整化性提供了支持性证据，并将先前关于高阶导数标量理论的发现推广到费米子情形。

Abstract: We construct a higher-derivative fermion theory with four-Fermi interactions. In quantum field theory, it is conjectured that good high-energy behavior of tree-level scattering amplitudes implies renormalizability, a notion referred to as the tree-unitarity criterion. To investigate whether this criterion is satisfied in a higher-derivative fermion theory, we examine the structure of the $S$-matrix in detail. Owing to the higher-derivative kinetic term, the mass dimension of the spinor field differs from that of a conventional fermion, rendering four-Fermi interactions renormalizable at the cost of introducing ghost states. We show that, unlike bosonic ghosts, fermionic ghosts inevitably give rise to negative-norm states regardless of the sign of their energy eigenvalues. We further demonstrate that, in contrast to conventional four-Fermi theories, the higher-derivative four-Fermi theory satisfies perturbative $S$-matrix unitarity. In light of the tree-unitarity criterion, these results provide supporting evidence for the renormalizability of higher-derivative fermion theories and generalize our previous findings on higher-derivative scalar theories to the fermionic case.

</details>


### [117] [String breaking in a (1+1)-dimensional $e^+e^-$ collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27134)
*Ross Dempsey,Anna-Maria E. Glück,Silviu S. Pufu,Benjamin T. Søgaard*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文构建了利用张量网络（tensor networks）研究(1+1)维规范理论中孤子（soliton）的框架，并在θ=π、裸费米子质量超过临界值的大质量Schwinger模型中实时模拟e⁺e⁻碰撞，发现末态为弹性散射与弦断裂（string breaking）轨迹的叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在θ=π且裸费米子质量超过临界值的参数区间，理论存在两个简并真空（degenerate vacua），e⁺与e⁻表现为连接两个真空的孤子态；需要一种可行方法来寻找、刻画并实时模拟这类孤子及其碰撞过程。

Method: 采用张量网络方法，具体使用无限张量网络（infinite tensor network methods）定位并刻画孤子态，随后对孤子碰撞进行实时（real-time）模拟。

Result: 碰撞过程中形成高能通量弦（energetic flux string），最终态被证明是弹性（elastic）轨迹与弦断裂（string-breaking）轨迹的量子叠加。

Conclusion: 张量网络可为(1+1)维规范理论中的孤子物理及非微扰实时动力学（如e⁺e⁻碰撞与弦断裂）提供有效研究框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a framework for studying solitons in $(1+1)$-dimensional gauge theories using tensor networks, and we apply it to simulate $e^+e^-$ collisions in the massive Schwinger model. We work at $θ= π$ and at a bare fermion mass exceeding the critical value; in this regime, there are two degenerate vacua, and the $e^+$ and $e^-$ particles are realized as soliton states interpolating between them. We carefully explain how to find and characterize such soliton states using infinite tensor network methods, and then exhibit real-time simulations of their collisions. These collisions involve the formation of an energetic flux string, and we demonstrate that our final states are superpositions of elastic and string-breaking trajectories.

</details>


### [118] [Pairing and modular: Generalized electrons and quasiholes inside the $\boxtimes$ of topological orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27378)
*Yoshiki Fukusumi,Yuma Furuta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究物理学中的对产生机制（pair creation mechanism）与数学中的配对运算（$\boxtimes_{\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub}}}$）之间的关系，从模性质（modular properties）与代数性质出发，提出通过非阿贝尔任意子（nonabelian anyon）类比将两个共享公共非群状结构的拓扑有序系统耦合起来，并将配对结果识别为扩展对称性的拓扑场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于建立物理对产生机制与数学配对运算之间的对应，并为两个共享相同（非群状）结构的拓扑有序系统（topologically ordered systems）提供一种耦合方式。

Method: 以非阿贝尔任意子（nonabelian anyon）对产生机制的类比，构造 $\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub},\boxtimes}=\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub}}\boxtimes \mathbf{A'}_{\text{ub}}$，并将其实现为隐藏拓扑算子（hidden topological operator）。

Result: 将 $\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub},\boxtimes}$ 识别为一般拓扑序中电子算子的推广或涉及共形嵌入（conformal embeddings）的（半）整数自旋隐藏流；并将 $\mathbf{A}\boxtimes_{\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub}}}\mathbf{A'}$ 识别为 $\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub},\boxtimes}$-扩展对称拓扑场论（extended symmetry topological field theory）。

Conclusion: 作者猜想存在一系列尚未探索的底层模不变量（modular invariants），将共形嵌入与配对运算联系起来。

Abstract: We study the relationship between the pair creation mechanism in physics and the pairing ($\boxtimes_{\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub}}}$) in mathematics, focusing on the modular and algebraic properties. We provide a way to couple two topologically ordered systems, $\mathbf{A}$ and $\mathbf{A'}$ sharing the common (non-group-like) structure $\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub}}=\overline{\mathbf{A'}_{\text{ub}}}$, through the nonabelian anyon analog of the pair creation mechanism denoted as $\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub},\boxtimes}=\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub}}\boxtimes \mathbf{A'}_{\text{ub}}$. We identify $\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub},\boxtimes}$ as the generalized version of electron operators in general topological orders or hidden current with (half-)integer spin involving the conformal embeddings. We provide a way to construct $\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub},\boxtimes}$ as the hidden topological operator and identify $\mathbf{A}\boxtimes_{\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub}}}\mathbf{A'}$, a general class of topoloical orders, as the $\mathbf{A}_{\text{ub},\boxtimes}$-extended symmetry topological field theory. We also conjecture a series of underlying (unexplored) modular invariants, connecting the conformal embedding and the pairing.

</details>


### [119] [Neural variational Skyrmions as a topological benchmark: emergent symmetry, precision energies and KAN--MLP comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27411)
*Koji Hashimoto,Masaki Murata,Gakuto Ogiwara*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出以三维 Skyrme 模型（Skyrme model）作为神经变分方法（neural variational methods）的拓扑基准，在不施加对称性拟设（symmetry ansatz）的条件下高精度构造了重子数（baryon number）B=1–4 的三维 Skyrmion，并系统比较了 KAN 与 MLP 的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一个能严格检验神经变分方法拓扑保持能力与数值精度的基准问题，并为经典拓扑孤子（topological solitons）建立面向 AI 智能体（AI-agentic）的评测基础。

Method: 以三维 Skyrme 模型为测试平台，从无对称性信息的 B 个单位电荷团块（unit-charge lumps）出发做弛豫；通过 Derrick 位力关系（Derrick virial relation）、Faddeev–Bogomolny 界（Faddeev–Bogomolny bound）、独立求积（independent quadratures）与分辨率扫描进行验证；并在上千次训练中对比 Kolmogorov–Arnold 网络（KANs）与多层感知机（MLPs），含与不含有理映射拟设（rational-map ansatz）两种情形。

Result: 环形、四面体与立方 Skyrmion 自发出现；B=1–4 的变分能量略低于已发表参考值，拓扑荷（topological charge）误差最大仅 10^-6。默认为设置下 KAN 明显占优，但调优 MLP 激活函数与学习率后大部分差距消失：KAN 参数效率更高、固定 epoch 预算下更稳健，而调优后的 MLP 在固定实际耗时（wall-clock time）下表现不逊。

Conclusion: 连续梯度下降（gradient descent）无法平滑改变神经场的拓扑扇区（topological sector），这解释了神经变分求解器中观察到的拓扑冻结（topological freezing）；该工作为 AI 辅助孤子物理与相关智能体基准提供了研究基础。

Abstract: We propose the three-dimensional Skyrme model as a topological benchmark for neural variational methods and construct fully three-dimensional Skyrmions with baryon number $B=1,\ldots,4$ without imposing a symmetry ansatz, with high precision. For $B=2,3,4$, the relaxation of these Skyrmions starts from $B$ unit-charge lumps with random positions and orientations, which carry no symmetry information. The known toroidal, tetrahedral, and cubic Skyrmions nevertheless emerge spontaneously. The resulting variational energies for $B=1,\ldots,4$ lie slightly below the published reference values. The neural method highlights the high accuracy for retaining the topological charge, differing from $B$ by at most $10^{-6}$ in the highest-precision solutions. Our numerical method goes through physical constraint tests such as the Derrick virial relation and the Faddeev--Bogomolny bound, together with evaluation / verification through independent quadratures and resolution sweeps. We then compare Kolmogorov--Arnold networks (KANs) with multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) in more than a thousand training runs, both with and without a rational-map ansatz. With default settings, KANs appear markedly superior, but most of the gap disappears once the MLP activation function and learning rate are tuned. KANs remain more parameter-efficient and more robust at fixed epoch budget, while MLPs with the tuned activation function perform at least as well at fixed wall-clock time. We also argue that continuous gradient descent cannot smoothly change the topological sector of our neural field, clarifying the origin of topological freezing observed in our neural variational solvers. Our results on the neural methods applied to Skyrmions pave a research ground for AI-agentic benchmarks for classical topological solitons, toward AI-assisted soliton physics.

</details>


### [120] [Gauging background isometries of the ILST null string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27437)
*Ulf Lindström*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对 ILST 弦（ILST string）背景几何等距代数 $\mathfrak g$ 的子代数 $\mathfrak h$ 作一般化规范（gauging），讨论残余对称性、约束代数（algebra of constraints）的收缩，并以此为基础给出约化/商构造（quotient construction），示例为由 SO(d,2) 背景模型经 SO(d,1) 等距商得到 $AdS$ 背景下的 ILST 弦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 ILST 弦模型仅在平坦背景（flat background）下给出完整的 Carroll-Weyl 代数（Carroll-Weyl algebra）；需明确在弯曲背景上对等距子代数规范后，哪些对称性与约束结构得以保留，并为后续约化构造奠定基础。

Method: 对背景几何等距代数 $\mathfrak g$ 的子代数 $\mathfrak h$ 实施规范（gauging），考察 $\mathfrak g$ 中哪些等距在规范后仍为对称性；推导并讨论相应的约束代数（部分置于附录），指出规范对称一般不含矢量密度（vector density）$V$ 的变换。

Result: 相较于平坦背景，约束代数出现收缩（contraction），且不再表示完整的 Carroll-Weyl 代数；规范等距为约化（reduction）或商构造（quotient construction）提供前提，可在规范群轨道空间上得到新的零弦（null string）。

Conclusion: 规范等距的框架可用于商化构造：文中在群空间（group space）中由 SO(d,2) 背景模型经 SO(d,1) 等距商，导出 $AdS$ 背景下的 ILST 弦，验证了该约化路径的可行性。

Abstract: We present a general gauging of a subalgebra, $\mathfrak h$, of the isometries $\mathfrak g$ of the background geometry for the ILST string (e-Print: hep-th/9307108 [hep-th]). We discuss which of the isometries in $\mathfrak g$ survive the gauging as symmetries. The gauged symmetry does not in general involve transformations of the vector density V. There is thus a corresponding contraction of the algebra of constraints as compared to the flat background and it no longer represent the full Carroll-Weyl algebra. This algebra of constraints is derived and discussed, partly in an appendix. The gauging of isometries sets the stage for a reduction, or quotient construction, leading to a new null string on the space of orbits of the gauge group. We illustrate this by deriving the ILST string in $AdS$ background as a quotient of a model in SO(d,2) background by a SO(d,1) isometry, in group space

</details>


### [121] [The $a$-function in fractional dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27464)
*I. Jack,D. R. T. Jones*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究标量场论与超对称手征标量场论在一般（非整数）临界维度下的β函数，并在超对称情形完成至四阶耦合的完整计算，构造出与β函数通过正定度规梯度流相联系的a函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在探讨一般n次自相互作用场论在其（通常为非整数）临界维度上的β函数结构，并检验是否存在一个类似a函数（a-function）的单调量，其与β函数之间由带正定度规（positive-definite metric）的梯度流（gradient flow）相联系，从而为更高维度的a定理类问题提供证据。

Method: 研究对象分为标量场论（scalar field theory）与超对称手征标量场论（supersymmetric chiral scalar field theory），相互作用取一般n阶自相互作用，工作维度取各自的临界维度（critical dimensions），一般为非整数。超对称情形下将计算完整推进至耦合的四阶，标量情形仅做部分计算；随后检验β函数能否写成带正定度规的梯度流形式。

Result: 在超对称情形中，至耦合四阶证明了a函数（a-function）的存在，其与β函数（β-functions）之间满足带正定度规的梯度流关系；标量情形下的全部四阶结果均与该性质同样成立相一致，但未给出标量情形的完整证明。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在非整数临界维度的超对称手征标量理论中，耦合四阶内存在由正定度规梯度流刻画的a函数；标量场论的四阶结果与此图景相容，暗示该梯度流性质可能具有更普遍的适用性。

Abstract: We consider the $β$-functions for both scalar, and supersymmetric chiral scalar, field theories with a general $n$th-order self-interaction, in their critical dimensions, which are in general non-integer. We perform a full computation up to fourth order in the coupling in the supersymmetric case, and a partial one in the scalar case. We then demonstrate the existence of an $a$-function, related to the $β$-functions by a gradient flow with a positive-definite metric, through fourth order in the coupling in the supersymmetric case; and show that all our fourth-order results in the scalar case are consistent with a similar property holding there too.

</details>


### [122] [Polarized IKKT holography: bulk realization of BPS operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27482)
*Franz Ciceri,Aymeric Proust,Henning Samtleben*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究 IKKT 矩阵模型中最低 BPS 多重态（BPS multiplet）极化（polarization）的全息（holographic）印记，通过矩阵模型侧与一维最大规范超引力（maximal gauged supergravity）侧的对照，建立了该 BPS 算符结构的体（bulk）实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解 IKKT 矩阵模型中极化形变在全息对偶下的体现，特别是最低 BPS 多重态中极化如何被组织并映射到引力一侧。

Method: 方法上，矩阵模型侧借助算符混合（operator mixing）与破缺对称性 Ward 恒等式，将存活算符组织为 F(4) 超代数（superalgebra）的不可约非典型多重态（atypical multiplet）；体侧利用一维最大规范超引力确定具有 F(4) 对称性的解析半 BPS 背景，并精确求解该背景及其未形变对应物附近的线性化玻色与费米方程，提取独立渐近数据。

Result: 结果显示，这些渐近数据的超对称变换（supersymmetry transformations）可重现矩阵模型的多重态，从而在体侧实现了相应 BPS 算符结构。

Conclusion: 结论是 IKKT 矩阵模型的极化在全息体中得到实现，矩阵模型与超引力两侧的 BPS 多重态结构相互对应。

Abstract: We study the holographic imprint of polarization in the lowest BPS multiplet of the IKKT matrix model. On the matrix model side, the deformation requires operator mixing and broken-symmetry Ward identities to organize the surviving operators into an irreducible atypical multiplet of the $F(4)$ superalgebra. In the bulk, we use one-dimensional maximal gauged supergravity to identify the analytic half-BPS background with $F(4)$ symmetry whose boundary behavior matches the IKKT polarization. We solve the linearized bosonic and fermionic equations around this background and its undeformed counterpart exactly, and extract the independent asymptotic data. The supersymmetry transformations of these data reproduce the matrix model multiplets, thus establishing a bulk realization of this BPS operator structure.

</details>


### [123] [BMS Charges from Null Hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27488)
*Martina Adamo,Luca Ciambelli,Felipe Diaz,Michael Imseis,Leonardo Sanhueza,Simone Speziale*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明未来零无穷（future null infinity）处BMS群（BMS group）的引力动力学、相空间与Noether荷可作为有限距离零超曲面（null hypersurfaces）上对应量的极限导出；BMS荷的恢复需借助协变边界拉格朗日量选取所确定的角点改进（corner improvement）以消除额外的软（soft）贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立有限距离零超曲面与未来零无穷处BMS分析之间的系统对应，澄清BMS荷、辛势（symplectic potential）与Bondi质量损失等结果如何由有限距离构造取极限得到，并处理BMS生成元延拓到体（bulk）时并非与零超曲面相切所带来的困难。

Method: 以有限距离零超曲面上类似量取极限的方式导出BMS群的引力动力学、相空间与Noether荷；Bondi质量损失与角动量演化方程由Raychaudhuri约束与Damour约束的极限得到；将Ashtekar–Streubel（又称Wald–Zoupas）辛势识别为双参数族零边界辛势中对应Dirichlet（即正则）极化的特定成员；并处理BMS生成元非切向性及相关反常（anomalies），通过协变边界拉格朗日量的规定来确定角点改进。

Result: Bondi质量损失与角动量演化方程作为Raychaudhuri和Damour约束的极限出现；Ashtekar–Streubel（Wald–Zoupas）辛势对应Dirichlet（正则）极化，族中其他成员与之相差场空间恰当项（field-space exact terms），导致相同的辐射预辛结构（radiative pre-symplectic structure）但可能不同的BMS通量（fluxes）；在计入生成元非切向性与反常之后，重整化正则荷仍含额外软贡献，而协变边界拉格朗日量所确定的角点改进可将其去除并恢复标准BMS荷。

Conclusion: 未来零无穷处的BMS结构可视为有限距离零超曲面构造的极限，但BMS荷的定义对体延拓方式敏感；只有引入由协变边界拉格朗日量规定的角点改进，才能从重整化正则荷中得到标准BMS荷。

Abstract: We show how the gravitational dynamics, phase space and Noether charges of the BMS group at future null infinity $\mathscr{I}^+$ can be derived as limits of their analogues on null hypersurfaces at finite distance. The Bondi mass-loss and angular-momentum evolution equations arise as limits of the Raychaudhuri and Damour constraints. The Ashtekar--Streubel (aka Wald--Zoupas) symplectic potential arises as a specific member of a two-parameter family of null-boundary symplectic potentials, corresponding to the Dirichlet (aka canonical) polarization, while the other members differ by field-space exact terms, leading to the same radiative pre-symplectic structure but potentially different BMS fluxes. Deriving the BMS charges requires additional care, since BMS generators extended into the bulk are generally not tangent to the null hypersurfaces. After accounting for this and the associated anomalies, the renormalized canonical charges still contain an additional soft contribution. The prescription of a covariant boundary Lagrangian determines a corner improvement that removes this term and recovers the standard BMS charges.

</details>


### [124] [The Kerr Metric in Canonical Harmonic Coordinates to All Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27573)
*Amina Sadu,Pierre Vanhove*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文给出从 Boyer–Lindquist 坐标到 Kerr 度规正则调和坐标（canonical harmonic coordinates）的全阶闭式变换（对牛顿常数与自旋各阶成立），并证明该坐标图为 Cook–Scheel 坐标所依赖两个径向调和函数的实、时间反演协变组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多极矩后闵可夫斯基（MPM）方案与以 Kerr 多极矩为源的调和规范爱因斯坦递推需要确定的调和坐标图，而此前该图的选取与残余调和自由度（residual harmonic freedom）未被完全厘清。

Method: 通过对残余调和自由度的分类证明：旋转协变性、宇称、时间反演与对自旋的解析性仅允许在第三后闵可夫斯基阶（third post-Minkowskian order）空间分量、自旋阶 a^p 时角量子数 ℓ=p+2 处存在衰减齐次解；该扇区为纯规范模，等价于 MPM 规范矩。选取使该模为零的代表，即以同一条件约束递推关系。

Result: 给出精确 gothic 度规及闭式后闵可夫斯基系数至五阶（反正切函数首次出现于四阶）；四阶结果与近期 canonical MPM 及基于振幅（amplitude-based）的结果一致，并以精确算术验证至五阶、自旋十二阶，代码公开。

Conclusion: 正则 MPM 迭代无需有限部分正规化（finite-part regularisation），亦无需任何阶的调谐性修正（harmonicity correction）；该条件唯一确定递推解。

Abstract: We give in closed form the transformation from Boyer--Lindquist coordinates to the canonical harmonic coordinates of the Kerr metric, to all orders in Newton's constant and in the spin. This is the canonical harmonic chart selected by the MPM prescription and by the harmonic-gauge Einstein recursion sourced by the Kerr multipoles, with the residual homogeneous freedom fixed by the boundary conditions. The chart is the real, time-reversal-covariant combination of the two radial harmonic functions underlying the Cook--Scheel coordinates, which differ from it by a definite residual harmonic transformation. The proof rests on a classification of the residual harmonic freedom. Rotational covariance, parity, time reversal and analyticity in the spin allow a decaying homogeneous solution beyond the linear field only in the spatial components at third post-Minkowskian order, with angular degree $\ell=p+2$ at spin order $a^p$. This sector is a pure gauge mode, equivalent to an MPM gauge moment. The exact chart selects the representative in which this third-PM homogeneous mode vanishes; imposing the same condition on the recursion removes the only residual ambiguity and fixes its solution uniquely. As a consequence, the canonical MPM iteration needs no finite-part regularisation and no harmonicity correction at any order. We give the exact gothic metric and the post-Minkowskian coefficients in closed form through fifth order, where an arctangent function first appears at fourth order. We find agreement with recent canonical MPM and amplitude-based results at fourth order, and we verify the construction in exact arithmetic through fifth order and twelfth order in the spin, with code made publicly available.

</details>


### [125] [Banana D-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27546)
*Giacomo Brunello,Wojciech Flieger,Yoshiaki Goto,Saiei-Jaeyeong Matsubara-Heo,Nobuki Takayama*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出并研究“香蕉 $D$-模（banana $D$-module）”，其解为具复传播子幂与复时空维数的通用质量香蕉费曼积分；通过将其与 Lauricella $F_C$ 超几何系统相联系，确定全纯秩（holonomic rank）与单值表示（monodromy representation）的不可约性，并给出级数解与 $d\log$ 表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 香蕉费曼积分（banana Feynman integrals）在微扰量子场论与解析计算中具有基础地位，而其对应的全纯 $D$-模（holonomic $D$-module）的结构性质（全纯秩、单值表示不可约性）此前缺乏系统刻画，这构成本文的研究动机。

Method: 将香蕉 $D$-模与 Lauricella $F_C$ 超几何系统（Lauricella $F_C$ hypergeometric systems）建立联系，以此研究其全纯秩（holonomic rank）与单值表示（monodromy representation）的不可约性（irreducibility）。同时把通用质量香蕉费曼积分及相应的非连续性积分（discontinuity integral）展开为级数解（series solutions），并分析由该 $D$-模导出的 Pfaffian 系统（Pfaffian system）。

Result: 借助与 Lauricella $F_C$ 系统的对应，获得了关于香蕉 $D$-模全纯秩及单值表示不可约性的结论；并给出通用质量香蕉费曼积分与其非连续性积分的级数解展开。此外，在等质量特化（equal-mass specialization）情形下构造了该 Pfaffian 系统的显式 $d\log$ 表示。

Conclusion: 香蕉 $D$-模可通过 Lauricella $F_C$ 超几何系统加以有效刻画，从而揭示其全纯秩与单值结构；级数解与等质量情形下的显式 $d\log$ 表示为进一步的解析与符号计算提供了框架。

Abstract: We study the holonomic $D$-module whose solutions are generic-mass banana Feynman integrals with complex propagator powers and spacetime dimension. We call this the banana $D$-module. Two properties of the banana $D$-module are studied: its holonomic rank and the irreducibility of the monodromy representation. This is achieved by relating the banana $D$-module to Lauricella $F_{C}$ hypergeometric systems. Further, we expand the generic-mass banana Feynman integral and the associated discontinuity integral in terms of series solutions. Finally, we analyze the Pfaffian system arising from the banana $D$-module and construct an explicit $d\log$ representation in the equal-mass specialization.

</details>


### [126] [Cosmological Correlators with Triple Massive Exchanges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27611)
*Shuntaro Aoki,Lucas Pinol,Zhehan Qin,Fumiya Sano,Masahide Yamaguchi,Yuhang Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出了原初双谱（primordial bispectrum）中三重交换大质量标量场贡献的解析公式，形式为双重级数（double series），允许三个交换场具有不同质量，并在整个物理区域内收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在所有树级（tree-level）原初双谱贡献中，大质量标量场的三重交换（triple exchange）是最难处理的情形，缺乏覆盖全物理区域且允许不同质量的解析表达式。

Method: 以最软的动量（softest momentum）展开混合传播子（mixed propagator），将三重交换化为两个混合传播子积分的求和；每个积分满足一个微分方程（differential equation），并由等动量处的取值确定。

Result: 得到以双重级数表示的双谱解析公式，对三个交换场的不同质量成立且在整个物理区域收敛；挤压极限（squeezed limit）可由该公式直接导出，形式简单紧凑。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了处理最困难的三重交换双谱贡献的解析工具，并表明其挤压极限具有简洁紧凑的形式。

Abstract: Among all tree-level contributions to the primordial bispectrum, the triple exchange of massive scalars is the most challenging. In this paper we present an analytical formula for this bispectrum in the form of a double series, which allows the three exchanged fields to have different masses and converges throughout the whole physical region. We obtain it by expanding the mixed propagator with the softest momentum, so that the triple exchange becomes a sum of integrals over two mixed propagators. Each of these integrals satisfies a differential equation and is fixed by its value at equal momentum. We further show that the squeezed limit follows directly from this formula and takes a simple and compact form.

</details>


### [127] [Bulk Wilson Loops from $\mathscr{I}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27739)
*James Drummond,Bruno Giménez Umbert,Prahar Mitra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将杨-米尔斯理论（Yang-Mills theory）的卡罗尔描述（Carrollian description）从散射振幅扩展至零多边形威尔逊环（null polygonal Wilson loop）的期望值，并提出相应的边界算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Opreij、Skinner 与 Wang 在部分复化未来零无穷（partially complexified future null infinity）上给出了四维杨-米尔斯理论的卡罗尔描述，但其适用范围限于散射振幅，尚需推广至更一般的可观测量。

Method: 在实未来零无穷（real future null infinity）上给出卡罗尔杨-米尔斯动能作用量（kinetic action）的新形式，并研究闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）中零多边形威尔逊环的期望值；提出以多边形顶点对应良好割线（good cuts）上全纯标架（holomorphic frames）循环乘积之迹构造的边界算符。

Result: 重现了 arXiv:2604.09771 所得到的对数两点函数（logarithmic two-point function）；在 g² 阶解析证明边界计算可复现由自对偶拉格朗日量插入（self-dual Lagrangian insertion）给出的有理体被积函数（rational bulk integrand）。

Conclusion: 卡罗尔描述可超越散射振幅，一致地刻画零多边形威尔逊环的边界对偶计算，为杨-米尔斯理论的可观测量提供新的边界表述框架。

Abstract: A Carrollian description of four-dimensional Yang-Mills theory on partially complexified future null infinity was recently proposed by Opreij, Skinner and Wang in arXiv:2604.09771. In this paper, we give a new form of the Carrollian Yang-Mills kinetic action on real future null infinity, and recover the logarithmic two-point function found by arXiv:2604.09771. We then extend the Carrollian description of Yang-Mills beyond scattering amplitudes by studying the expectation value of a null polygonal Wilson loop in Minkowski spacetime. We propose a boundary operator given by the trace of a cyclic product of holomorphic frames on the good cuts associated with the polygon's vertices. At order $g^2$, we show analytically that the boundary calculation reproduces the rational bulk integrand obtained from a self-dual Lagrangian insertion.

</details>


### [128] [Brane Tilings and Polygons with Two Interior Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27861)
*Rak-Kyeong Seong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了环面图（toric diagram）为含两个内部格点的凸格多边形的环面 Calabi-Yau 3-流形所对应的膜铺砌（brane tilings）与 4d N=1 超对称颤动规范理论（quiver gauge theories），共 45 个多边形、323 个环面相位，并给出各自的 Hilbert 级数、介子模空间与超共形 U(1)_R 荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多集中于内部格点数为 0 或 1 的环面 Calabi-Yau 3-流形，内部格点为两个的情形尚缺乏系统完整的分类与数据库，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在 GL(2,Z) 变换与格平移下枚举含两个内部格点的凸格多边形，对每个环面相位构造膜铺砌及其颤动与超势（superpotential）、完美匹配矩阵（perfect matching matrix）和 F-/D-项荷矩阵；并通过 Hilbert 级数（Hilbert series）与 plethystic 对数研究阿贝尔理论的介子模空间（mesonic moduli space）。

Result: 共得到 45 个此类多边形：5 个三角形、19 个四边形、16 个五边形、5 个六边形；识别出全部 323 个环面相位，并对其逐一给出膜铺砌、完美匹配矩阵与荷矩阵、介子模空间的生成元与领头关系，以及介子整体对称性与超共形 U(1)_R 荷。

Conclusion: 该工作为内部格点为两个的环面 Calabi-Yau 3-流形提供了完整的膜铺砌与 4d N=1 颤动规范理论字典，可作为后续研究与模型构建的参考数据集。

Abstract: We fully classify the brane tilings and the 4d N=1 supersymmetric quiver gauge theories corresponding to toric Calabi-Yau 3-folds whose toric diagrams are convex lattice polygons with two interior lattice points. There are 45 such polygons up to GL(2,Z) transformations and lattice translations: 5 triangles, 19 quadrilaterals, 16 pentagons and 5 hexagons. We identify all 323 toric phases corresponding to these toric Calabi-Yau 3-folds. For every toric phase, we present the brane tiling with its quiver and superpotential, the perfect matching matrix and the F- and D-term charge matrices. For each brane tiling, we study the mesonic moduli space of the corresponding abelian 4d N=1 theory through its Hilbert series and plethystic logarithm, and identify its generators and their leading relations. We also identify the mesonic global symmetry for each brane tiling and determine the superconformal U(1)R charges.

</details>

### [129] [Receptor Abundance Alone Underpredicts Red Blood Cell Adhesion in Gaucher Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27960)
*Zhaojie Chai,Pierre A. Buffet,Melanie Franco,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究以随机结合模型模拟红细胞（RBC）表面Lu/BCAM–层粘连蛋白（laminin）键，发现戈谢病（GD）红细胞的异常黏附更多取决于受体的结合活性状态（磷酸化水平）而非受体总量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GD红细胞异常黏附于血管壁并携带更多Lu/BCAM（一种层粘连蛋白受体），但受体丰度是否决定该黏附尚缺乏检验。

Method: 构建携带随机Lu/BCAM–laminin键的红细胞模拟模型，在对照供体人群上校准，并据1.3倍受体升高预测GD黏附；另检验膜刚度与键亲和力的作用，并在镰状细胞病（sickle cell disease）中验证模型。

Result: 在laminin与内皮上实测黏附超出预测1.6–1.7倍，需2.4–4.6倍具结合能力受体方可匹配，与磷酸化受体约5.7倍升高相容；提高膜刚度或键亲和力均无法弥补高剪切应力（shear stress）下的差距。镰状细胞病中模型在1.4倍内预测羟基脲（hydroxyurea）下的黏附下降，尽管总受体升高。

Conclusion: 黏附更可能取决于受体状态而非丰度；膜硬化则使细胞滞留于脾 slit。

Abstract: Red blood cells (RBCs) in Gaucher disease (GD) adhere abnormally to vessel walls and carry more Lutheran/basal cell adhesion molecule (Lu/BCAM), a laminin receptor. Whether receptor abundance sets this adhesion is untested. We simulated RBCs carrying stochastic Lu/BCAM-laminin bonds, calibrated a population on control donors, and predicted GD adhesion from the 1.3-fold receptor rise. On laminin and endothelium the data exceed the prediction 1.6- to 1.7-fold. Matching them requires 2.4- and 4.6-fold more binding-competent receptors, compatible with the estimated 5.7-fold rise in phosphorylated receptors. Neither a stiffer membrane nor higher bond affinity removes the shortfall at high shear stress. In sickle cell disease, given active-receptor counts, the model predicts the fall in adhesion under hydroxyurea within 1.4-fold, while total receptors rise. Adhesion likely follows receptor state more than abundance. Stiffening instead retains cells at a splenic slit.

</details>

### [130] [Topologically Obstructed Kondo Hybridization: A Route to Kondo Semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26686)
*Yue Liu,Junze Deng,Haoyu Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出传导电子费米面的拓扑与对称性可阻碍近藤杂化能隙在特定动量处打开，导致在近藤相内出现c-f能带交叉的节点点或节点线，从而为近藤半金属（Kondo semimetal）提供了一条基于拓扑阻碍机制的形成途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重费米子系统中近藤效应（Kondo effect）通常使局域f磁矩与传导电子形成相干重电子能带并伴随近藤杂化能隙，但强关联、拓扑与对称性的相互作用可能改变这一低能电子结构图像，本文旨在探究费米面拓扑是否会阻碍杂化能隙的完全打开。

Method: 将三维系统的费米面视为等效二维流形（effective two-dimensional manifold），在该流形上定义非平凡拓扑不变量（topological invariant），论证其构成打开完整杂化能隙的拓扑阻碍（topological obstruction），并分析杂化在费米面特定动量处被迫消失的后果。

Result: 非平凡拓扑不变量迫使fc杂化在费米面某些动量处为零，这些杂化零点（hybridization zeros）引起c带与f带交叉，在近藤相内、费米能附近形成节点点（nodal points）或节点线（nodal lines）。

Conclusion: 研究确立了“近藤杂化的拓扑阻碍”作为通向近藤半金属的一条路径，揭示了费米面拓扑与关联电子相（correlated electronic phases）涌现之间的基本联系。

Abstract: The interplay of strong correlations, topology, and symmetry can reshape the low-energy electronic structure of quantum materials and drive the emergence of novel quantum phases. In heavy-fermion systems, the Kondo effect entangles localized $f$ moments with conduction electrons, giving rise to coherent heavy-electron bands at low temperatures, typically characterized by a Kondo hybridization gap. Here we show, however, that the topology and symmetry of the conduction-electron Fermi surface can obstruct the opening of the Kondo hybridization gap at certain momenta. Treating the Fermi surface of a three-dimensional system as an effective two-dimensional manifold, we demonstrate that nontrivial topological invariants defined on this manifold impose a topological obstruction to opening a full hybridization gap, forcing the $fc$ hybridization to vanish at certain momenta on the Fermi surface. These hybridization zeros lead to crossings between $c$ and $f$ bands, giving rise to nodal points or lines near the Fermi energy within the Kondo phase. Our results establish topological obstructions to Kondo hybridization as a route to Kondo semimetals, revealing a fundamental connection between Fermi-surface topology and the emergence of correlated electronic phases.

</details>


### [131] [Off-diagonal long-range order and $p$-wave pairing in one dimension from critical density fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26690)
*Matthew D. Thomson,Simon M. Martin,Frank Marsiglio,Joseph Maciejko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究一维无自旋费米子局域相互作用模型，发现由Luttinger液体到相分离态的量子相变临界点具有两体非对角长程序与p波配对，表明相分离不稳定性处的临界密度涨落可充当一维非常规超导的配对胶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规超导理论关注由晶格振动之外的玻色集体模式媒介的Cooper配对；但低维集体涨落也可能通过抑制长程序或破坏正常态Fermi液体准粒子而削弱超导，故需考察临界涨落能否在低维中起配对作用。

Method: 构建一维（1D）无自旋费米子（spinless fermions）局域相互作用模型，结合场论论证（field-theoretic arguments）、数值精确对角化（exact diagonalization）与矩阵乘积态（matrix-product-state）方法进行研究。

Result: 该模型呈现从Luttinger液体（Luttinger liquid）到相分离（phase-separated）态的连续量子相变；量子临界点（quantum critical point）表现出两体非对角长程序（two-particle off-diagonal long-range order）与p波配对（p-wave pairing）。

Conclusion: 相分离不稳定性处的临界密度涨落（critical density fluctuations）可作为一维非常规超导的配对胶（pairing glue），克服相位涨落的无序效应以及正常态中缺乏长寿命费米准粒子的困难。

Abstract: A central theme in the theory of unconventional superconductivity is that Cooper pairing can be mediated by bosonic collective modes other than lattice vibrations. However, collective fluctuations in reduced dimensions can also have a destabilizing effect on superconductivity, by suppressing long-range order or by destroying Fermi-liquid quasiparticles in the normal state. In this work, we study a one-dimensional (1D) model of spinless fermions with local interactions that exhibits a continuous quantum phase transition from a Luttinger liquid to a phase-separated state. Combining field-theoretic arguments with numerical exact diagonalization and matrix-product-state methods, we find that the quantum critical point exhibits two-particle off-diagonal long-range order and $p$-wave pairing. Our work shows that critical density fluctuations at a phase-separation instability can act as pairing glue for unconventional superconductivity in 1D, overcoming the disordering effect of phase fluctuations and the absence of long-lived fermionic quasiparticles in the normal state.

</details>


### [132] [Impact of spin fluctuations on spectral functions and transport in a model altermagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26725)
*Sourav Chakraborty,Manoranjan Kumar,Arun Paramekanti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作采用费米子蒙特卡洛（fermion Monte Carlo）方法研究棋盘格哈伯德模型（checkerboard lattice Hubbard model）中的交变磁性（altermagnetism），追踪自旋涨落（spin fluctuations）的完整空间依赖随温度的演化，给出自旋依赖单粒子谱函数（spin-dependent single particle spectral functions）与输运性质从有序基态到高温无序顺磁相的演化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将交变磁性（altermagnetism）纳入下一代自旋电子学（spintronics）技术需要理解其自旋依赖输运（spin dependent transport）；强关联量子材料（strongly correlated quantum materials）中交变磁性的发现进一步推动在理论上探索这一物理。

Method: 使用费米子蒙特卡洛（fermion Monte Carlo）方法求解棋盘格哈伯德模型（checkerboard lattice Hubbard model），并追踪自旋涨落（spin fluctuations）随温度变化的完整空间依赖。

Result: 给出了自旋依赖单粒子谱函数（spin-dependent single particle spectral functions）与输运性质从有序基态（ordered ground state）进入高温无序顺磁相（high temperature disordered paramagnetic phase）的演化结果。

Conclusion: 研究结果可在未来的自旋选择性角分辨光电子能谱（spin-selective ARPES）与自旋输运实验中检验，候选体系包括固态量子材料中实现的交变磁体以及提议的超冷原子系统（ultracold atomic systems）。

Abstract: Understanding spin dependent transport in altermagnets is important for incorporating them in next generation spintronic technologies. Motivated by the discovery of altermagnetism in strongly correlated quantum materials, we explore this physics in the checkerboard lattice Hubbard model using a fermion Monte Carlo approach which tracks the full spatial dependence of spin fluctuations as a function of temperature. We present results for the evolution of the spin-dependent single particle spectral functions and transport as we go from the ordered ground state into the high temperature disordered paramagnetic phase. Our results could be tested in future spin-selective angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and spin transport experiments in candidate altermagnets realized in solid state quantum materials or in proposed ultracold atomic systems.

</details>


### [133] [Emergent Long-Range Entanglement in the XXZ Quantum Spin Chain with Competing Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26741)
*Anthony Balchin,Eytan Grosfeld,Eran Ginossar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在自旋1/2 XXZ链中引入铁磁最近邻耦合与反铁磁幂律长程耦合的竞争，DMRG与自旋波理论（spin-wave theory）表明该竞争稳定了一个中心荷约等于1的无能隙相，具有二重简并基态与纠缠谱，且为长程纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短程与长程相互作用（short-range and long-range interactions）之间的竞争被视为探索一维量子物质（one-dimensional quantum matter）的一条尚未充分研究的途径。

Method: 构建自旋1/2 XXZ链，含铁磁最近邻耦合（ferromagnetic nearest-neighbour coupling）与反铁磁幂律耦合（antiferromagnetic power-law coupling），采用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）与自旋波理论进行分析。

Result: 该竞争稳定了一个无能隙相（gapless phase），中心荷（central charge）为 c=0.997(17)，基态与纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）呈二重简并，且在最近邻模型中不存在；有限量子条件互信息（quantum conditional mutual information）表明其具有长程纠缠（long-range entanglement）。模型还产生一个独特的能隙量子相（gapped quantum phase）和一个具有不衰减关联（non-decaying correlations）的无能隙反相（gapless antiphase）。

Conclusion: 短程与长程相互作用竞争可在一维量子自旋链中诱导出最近邻模型所不具备的新奇相，包括长程纠缠的无能隙相及具不衰减关联的反相。

Abstract: Competition between short-range and long-range interactions is a largely unexplored route to one-dimensional quantum matter. We introduce a spin-1/2 XXZ chain with ferromagnetic nearest-neighbour and antiferromagnetic power-law couplings. Using DMRG and spin-wave theory, we show this competition stabilises a gapless phase with central charge $c=0.997(17)$, a twofold-degenerate ground state and entanglement spectrum, absent in nearest-neighbour models, and is long-range entangled as diagnosed by finite quantum conditional mutual information. The model also generates a unique gapped quantum phase and gapless antiphase with non-decaying correlations.

</details>


### [134] [Geometric Contribution to the Electron-Phonon Coupling Constant: Checkerboard and Hyper-diamond Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26823)
*Thomas Caligiure,Daniel P. Arovas,Jiabin Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在棋盘晶格（checkerboard）与超金刚石（hyper-diamond）模型中，量子几何（quantum geometry）通过量子度规（quantum metric）对无量纲电子-声子耦合常数（EPC constant）产生可调控的独立贡献，其比例最高可达约 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究指出电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling, EPC）强度中存在源于电子能带量子几何的新贡献，但该几何贡献在具体晶格模型中的行为与可调控性尚需系统考察。

Method: 在 Gaussian 近似（Gaussian approximation）下，于棋盘晶格模型与超金刚石模型中计算由量子度规刻画的无量纲 EPC 常数几何贡献，并与能量部分（energetic part）分离比较。

Result: 棋盘晶格模型中，几何贡献可在保持能量部分不变的前提下独立调节，表明可通过量子几何增强 EPC 强度；超金刚石模型中，几何贡献在所有空间维度上约占总 EPC 常数的 50%，将石墨烯（graphene）的 d=2 结果推广至更高维度。

Conclusion: 量子几何是调控电子-声子耦合强度的独立自由度，其贡献在多个晶格模型中具有普遍性与维度无关性，为通过能带几何设计增强 EPC 相关量子相提供依据。

Abstract: Recent theoretical studies on electron-phonon coupling (EPC) - a key ingredient in the emergence of diverse quantum phases such as superconductivity and charge-density waves - have revealed a distinct contribution to its strength arising from the quantum geometry of electronic bands. Here we investigate the quantum geometric contribution, characterized by the quantum metric, to the dimensionless EPC constant in the checkerboard and hyper-diamond models, under the Gaussian approximation. We show that in the checkerboard model, the geometric contribution can be tuned independently while keeping the energetic part fixed, demonstrating the possibility of enhancing EPC strength through quantum geometry. In the hyper-diamond model, the geometric contribution accounts for roughly $50\%$ of the total EPC constant across all spatial dimensions, generalizing the $d=2$ result for graphene to higher dimensions.

</details>


### [135] [Majorana quasiparticles, mass hierarchies, and the phase diagram of the Gross-Neveu model in D=2+1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26872)
*Igor F. Herbut,Mathew Schneider*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出，含破缺反演对称质量项的2+1维O(2N)对称Gross-Neveu模型在零温相图中存在一系列O(2N-k)×O(k)对称基态（k=1,…,N），奇k时准粒子为巡游的、有质量的、双实分量马约拉纳费米子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示反演对称破缺质量项对O(2N)对称Gross-Neveu模型零温相图与准粒子性质的影响，并探索马约拉纳准粒子在其中出现的可能性。

Method: 采用平均场（mean-field）方法求解模型的基态与准粒子激发，分析不同对称性破缺模式对应的平均场方程解及其稳定性。

Result: 零温相图中出现一系列O(2N-k)×O(k)对称基态；奇k时准粒子为巡游、有质量的马约拉纳费米子（即狄拉克费米子的一半）。具有O(2N-k1-k2)×O(k1)×O(k2)对称性的解虽存在但不稳定。

Conclusion: 结果给出了该模型中马约拉纳准粒子的系统性刻画，并概述了k=1的“单轴向列相（uniaxial nematic）”态中的相关表现及与其他物理系统的类比。

Abstract: We show that the canonical $2+1$-dimensional $O(2N)$-symmetric Gross-Neveu model with the inversion-breaking mass-term features a sequence of $O(2N-k) \times O(k)$ - symmetric ground states in its zero-temperature phase diagram, with $k=1,...,N$. For odd $k$ the quasiparticle excitations are itinerant, massive, two-real-component Majorana fermions, i.e. half of Dirac fermions. The solutions of the mean-field equations symmetric under $O(2N-k_1-k_2) \times O(k_1) \times O(k_2)$ with $k_1, k_2 \geq 1$ also exist, but are found to be unstable. Some manifestations of Majorana quasiparticle in the particular ``uniaxial nematic" state with $k=1$ are outlined, and analogies to other physical systems are offered.

</details>


### [136] [Quantum order by disorder among lozenge tilings: a staircase of hub-free crystals between the Lieb ferrimagnet and the antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27076)
*Suhwan Yoo,Jeehoon Choi,Chaeyeon Kim,Hee Young Kwon,Changyeon Won*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作研究三角晶格上的菱形镶嵌（lozenge tilings）所定义的二分网络（bipartite networks）家族，指出经典海森堡反铁磁体（Heisenberg antiferromagnet）下这些网络简并，而量子涨落（quantum fluctuations）通过局域机制解除简并：零点能（zero-point energy）在主导阶为配位数（coordination）的凸函数，结合Jensen不等式在固定平均配位数时选出方形型镶嵌。磁场与Lieb磁矩竞争，量子蒙特卡洛与周期镶嵌完整枚举显示经典跃变被周期晶体的阶梯取代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱形镶嵌涵盖从具有最大子晶格失衡的Lieb铁磁体（Lieb ferrimagnet）骰子晶格（dice lattice）到补偿性反铁磁的方形型镶嵌等广泛网络。经典层面它们全部简并，但量子涨落如何以及选择哪个基态此前缺乏机制性解释，且磁场下的行为与有限尺寸/周期结构的选择尚不清楚。

Method: 采用局域机制分析：计算零点能主导阶，证明其对配位数z呈凸性，并以Ising极限展开解释（键更多带来更多涨落通道，但每次涨落使更多键受挫）。引入磁场与Lieb磁矩耦合项，结合Lieb–Mattis定理和键计数给出无枢纽（hub-free）镶嵌的界。数值上使用量子蒙特卡洛（Quantum Monte Carlo）及小单胞周期镶嵌的完整枚举，并在高度表示（height representation）中把配位数解释为离散高斯曲率。

Result: 固定⟨z⟩=4时Jensen不等式选出方形型镶嵌。磁场下无枢纽镶嵌被限制在骰子磁矩的3/5以内；数值显示经典跃变被周期晶体的阶梯（staircase of periodic crystals）取代，最宽台阶为饱和该界的无枢纽晶体，其下方三重与五重缺陷（defects）以浅序列越来越密地堆积。选择能标度（selection energies）为JS。

Conclusion: 量子涨落通过配位数的凸性机制在菱形镶嵌家族中解除经典简并，并将磁场驱动的转变塑造成周期晶体阶梯，其结构由等曲率缺陷间吸引与反曲率缺陷间排斥控制。高度表示给出配位数即离散高斯曲率的几何图像，并给出光谱学特征及在纳米石墨烯（nanographenes）、原子自旋阵列和量子模拟器中的可能实现。

Abstract: Lozenge tilings of the triangular lattice define an exponentially large family of bipartite networks, from the dice lattice, a Lieb ferrimagnet with the largest sublattice imbalance, to square-type tilings, which are compensated antiferromagnets. For the classical Heisenberg antiferromagnet all of them are degenerate. We show that quantum fluctuations lift this degeneracy through a local mechanism: the zero-point energy is, to leading order, a sum of site terms that are convex in the coordination $z$, so that Jensen's inequality at fixed $\langle z\rangle=4$ selects the square-type tiling. An expansion about the Ising limit explains the convexity: a site with more bonds has more channels for quantum fluctuations, but each fluctuation frustrates more bonds. A magnetic field couples to the Lieb moment and competes with this selection. Because the moment is carried most cheaply by threefold and fivefold sites, sixfold hubs are avoided, and bond counting together with the Lieb--Mattis theorem limits hub-free tilings to $3/5$ of the dice moment. Quantum Monte Carlo and a complete enumeration of periodic tilings with small unit cells show that the classical jump is replaced by a staircase of periodic crystals. Its widest step is a hub-free crystal that saturates the bound; below it, threefold and fivefold defects pack ever more densely in a shallow sequence of crystals, controlled by a short-ranged attraction between defects of equal curvature and a repulsion between defects of opposite curvature. Selection energies scale as $JS$. In the height representation the coordination is the discrete Gaussian curvature and the Lieb moment its sublattice-staggered part. We give spectroscopic signatures and discuss realizations in nanographenes, atomic spin arrays and quantum simulators.

</details>


### [137] [Kinetic Arrest of a Disordered First-Order Phase Transition in Strained V2O3 Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27168)
*Sindhunil Barman Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出一阶相变（first-order phase transition, FOPT）动力学停滞（kinetic arrest, KA）的连续介质唯象理论，以外延V2O3薄膜中的金属-绝缘体转变（MIT）为原型，结合TDGL方程、非局域弹性核与淬火无序，导出停滞边界TK(E)的动力学判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对一阶相变在低温下被动力学停滞、形成金属-绝缘相共存镶嵌结构的现象，现有描述缺乏能同时解耦热力学驱动力与动力学势垒、并解释外延衬底夹持效应的统一连续介质框架。

Method: 定义表征金属体积分数并耦合单斜结构畸变的粗粒化介观序参量，构建含非局域弹性相互作用核与Imry-Wortis淬火无序景观的时间依赖Ginzburg-Landau（Time-Dependent Ginzburg-Landau, TDGL）框架；通过分析序参量弛豫时间相对于观测时间尺度的发散，导出TK(E)的显式动力学判据。

Result: 外延衬底夹持（substrate-induced clamping）抬高局部弹性势垒、钉扎畴壁，稳定了直至低温的非均匀金属-绝缘镶嵌态；模拟转变动力学呈现Avrami指数抑制至n<1，反映畴壁传播受阻；电场作为等温机制降低动力学势垒，可描述阈值去停滞（de-arrest）与多级电阻态。

Conclusion: 该框架严格解耦热力学驱动力与动力学激活势垒；作者将电子势垒降低机制与电热焦耳加热（Joule heating）对比，提出区分二者的诊断判据，并将该非平衡动力学与无序超导薄膜中的相关现象进行类比讨论。

Abstract: We develop a continuum phenomenological theory for the kinetic arrest (KA) of a first-order phase transition (FOPT), taking the thermal and electric field-driven metal-insulator transition (MIT) in epitaxial V2O3 thin films as a prototype. By defining a coarse-grained mesoscopic order parameter representing the metallic volume fraction coupled to the primary structural monoclinic distortion, we formulate a Time-Dependent Ginzburg-Landau (TDGL) framework integrated with a non-local elastic interaction kernel and an Imry-Wortis quenched disorder landscape. We derive an explicit dynamic criterion for the kinetic arrest boundary TK(E) by analyzing the divergence of the intrinsic order-parameter relaxation time relative to experimental observation timescales. Our framework rigorously decouples the thermodynamic driving force from the kinetic activation barrier. We demonstrate that epitaxial substrate-induced clamping elevates local elastic barriers, pinning domain boundaries and stabilizing an arrested, inhomogeneous mosaic of metallic and insulating phases down to cryogenic temperatures. The simulated transformation kinetics exhibit an Avrami exponent suppression to values n < 1, reflecting slow, hindered domain-wall propagation. Furthermore, we examine the effect of an external electric field as an isothermal mechanism that lowers this kinetic barrier, providing a phenomenological description of threshold de-arrest and multi-level resistance states. We critically discuss this electronic barrier-lowering mechanism in comparison with electrothermal Joule heating, establish diagnostic criteria to distinguish them, and contextualize these non-equilibrium dynamics with related phenomena in disordered superconducting films.

</details>


### [138] [Quantum Many-Body Scars from Operator Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27982)
*Debarghya Chakraborty,Dario Rosa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在系统双拷贝中构造近似多体疤痕态（approximate many-body scar states）的通用方案：采用双局域尺寸相互作用（bilocal size interaction）将一侧格点与另一拷贝的对应伙伴耦合，从而得到等差能级塔与相干复活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多体疤痕态（many-body scars）缺乏通用构造框架的问题，作者希望在双拷贝体系（two copies）中给出不依赖具体模型的疤痕态构造方法，并借助尺寸算符（size operator）的代数结构获得可解析的能谱规律。

Method: 对未耦合的双侧哈密顿量（two-sided Hamiltonian），要求其在算符基下各项具有固定尺寸（fixed size），据此识别出多项式多个被哈密顿量湮灭的 Krylov 基态（Krylov basis states）；这些态在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下集中于单一尺寸扇区（size sector）。再引入双局域尺寸相互作用实现耦合，并在一般 q-体最近邻自旋链（q-body nearest-neighbor spin chain）双拷贝与 SYK 模型中给出具体实现。

Result: 尺寸算符（size operator）将这些态分裂为等差能级塔（equally spaced tower），产生相干复活（coherent revivals），表现为两拷贝间能量的集体振荡（collective oscillations）。该构造在 q-体最近邻自旋链双拷贝与 SYK 模型中得到具体实现。

Conclusion: 双局域尺寸相互作用为双拷贝体系提供了构造近似多体疤痕态的通用机制，且其能谱与动力学特征（等差塔、相干复活、跨拷贝能量振荡）具有模型无关的普适性，可推广至自旋链与 SYK 等具体模型。

Abstract: We present a general construction of approximate many-body scar states in two copies of a system using a bilocal size interaction that couples one site to its partner on the other copy. For an uncoupled two-sided Hamiltonian whose terms have fixed size in an operator basis, we identify polynomially many Krylov basis states that it annihilates and that, in the thermodynamic limit, become concentrated on a single size sector. The size operator splits them into a tower of equally spaced levels that gives rise to coherent revivals characterized by collective oscillations of energy across the two copies. We provide a concrete realization of this construction in two copies of a general $q$-body nearest-neighbor spin chain, and the SYK model.

</details>


### [139] [Nonmonotonic Magnetic Correlations in Decorated Lieb-Lattice Anderson Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27247)
*Raymond Hmuifoo,Edwin W. Huang,Annabelle Ancheta,Richard T. Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用行列式量子蒙特卡洛（determinant quantum Monte Carlo）模拟研究Lieb晶格上选择性杂化构建的两种周期Anderson型模型，发现长程磁关联随杂化强度呈现显著非单调演化：中间杂化下被强烈抑制，随后在未修饰格点上大幅恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子关联（electronic correlations）与晶格几何的相互作用可强烈改变多轨道哈密顿量（multiorbital Hamiltonians）中磁关联与局域单态（local singlet）形成之间的竞争，需厘清选择性杂化与平带（flat band）结构如何影响该竞争。

Method: 采用行列式量子蒙特卡洛（determinant quantum Monte Carlo）模拟，将局域轨道选择性杂化到Lieb晶格不等价格点上，构建两种周期Anderson型模型（periodic Anderson-like models）；这些几何结构具有部分填充平带与不同子晶格失衡（sublattice imbalance）。通过调节杂化强度，考察局域自旋关联与轨道分辨磁结构因子（orbital-resolved magnetic structure factors）的演化。

Result: 杂化轨道间的关联相对平滑地趋向局域单态形成，而长程磁关联对杂化表现出显著的非单调依赖：在中间杂化区被明显压制，随后在未修饰格点（undecorated lattice sites）上大幅恢复。

Conclusion: 结果与以往耗竭型周期Anderson模型（depleted periodic Anderson models）研究及Lieb定理（Lieb's theorem）的约束相联系，表明选择性杂化与平带结构可在强关联多轨道体系中产生磁关联的异常重分布。

Abstract: The interplay between electronic correlations and lattice geometry can strongly modify the competition between magnetic correlations and local singlet formation in multiorbital Hamiltonians. Here we use determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations to investigate two periodic Anderson-like models constructed by selectively hybridizing localized orbitals to the inequivalent sites of a Lieb lattice. The resulting geometries possess partially filled flat bands and different sublattice imbalances. We examine the evolution of local spin correlations and orbital-resolved magnetic structure factors as the hybridization is increased. Although correlations between hybridized orbitals evolve relatively smoothly toward local singlet formation, the longer-range magnetic correlations exhibit a striking nonmonotonic dependence on hybridization. In particular, we observe a pronounced suppression at intermediate hybridization followed by a substantial recovery on undecorated lattice sites. We discuss these results in relation to earlier studies of depleted periodic Anderson models and the constraints imposed by Lieb's theorem. Our findings illustrate how selective hybridization and flat-band structure can produce unusual redistributions of magnetic correlations in strongly interacting multiorbital systems.

</details>


### [140] [Role of antisite disorder in dictating the Ising anisotropy and ferromagnetic ground state of NdGaSi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27334)
*Anyesh Saraswati,Sourav Kanthal,Subhajit Nandy,Jhuma Sannigrahi,Matthias Gutmann,Nitesh Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: Ga/Si反位无序使NdGaSi稳定于中心对称I4₁/amd结构，将Nd³⁺位点对称性提升至D₂d，从而禁戒CEF哈密顿量中所有m=±2 Stevens算符，使基态双重态近乎纯净（约97%），进而选择出k=0的铁磁基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土金属间化合物的磁基态通常由单离子各向异性（single-ion anisotropy）与交换相互作用共同决定，但配体子晶格上的化学无序（chemical disorder）所起作用研究甚少，本文即探讨反位无序如何影响晶体电场（crystalline electric field, CEF）与磁基态选择。

Method: 结合磁化测量、比热、单晶中子衍射与X射线吸收谱确定无序结构与磁有序；并通过对两轴磁化率与磁比热同时拟合的CEF分析，提取基态双重态的纯度，并与Al/Si有序的NdAlSi（Nd³⁺为C₂v位点对称性）对比。

Result: NdGaSi在T_C≈11 K以下呈共线铁磁序，沿c轴具Ising各向异性；CEF拟合给出约97%纯净的基态双重态，而NdAlSi的基态则强烈混合。

Conclusion: 基态纯度使有序磁矩对阻挫交换引起的调制具有刚性，从而在NdGaSi中选择k=0铁磁体，而非NdAlSi中的公度外铁磁体（incommensurate ferrimagnet）；反位无序由此成为调控CEF基态纯度并进而选择不同磁基态的手段。

Abstract: Single-ion anisotropy and exchange interactions together set the magnetic ground state of rare-earth intermetallics, yet the role of chemical disorder on the ligand sublattice remains little explored. Here, we show that Ga/Si antisite disorder in NdGaSi stabilises the centrosymmetric $I4_1/amd$ structure and raises the Nd$^{3+}$ site symmetry to $D_{2d}$, which forbids all $m=\pm2$ Stevens operators in the crystalline electric field (CEF) Hamiltonian. Magnetization, specific heat, single-crystal neutron diffraction, and x-ray absorption spectroscopy establish the disordered structure and a collinear ferromagnetic order below $T_\mathrm{C}\approx11$~K with Ising anisotropy along $c$. A CEF analysis that fits the susceptibilities along both axes and the magnetic specific heat simultaneously yields a ground-state doublet that is $\sim97\%$ pure, in contrast to the strongly mixed ground state of the Al/Si-ordered NdAlSi ($C_{2v}$ site symmetry of Nd$^{3+}$). We argue that this ground-state purity makes the ordered moment rigid against the modulation due to frustrated exchange, selecting a $\mathbf{k}=0$ ferromagnet in NdGaSi instead of the incommensurate ferrimagnet of NdAlSi. Antisite disorder, thus, acts as a handle on CEF ground-state purity and can, thereby, select between fundamentally different magnetic ground states.

</details>


### [141] [Lattice-geometry dependence of Type-I and Type-II magnetic flux responses in strongly correlated bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27749)
*Takayuki Yokoyama,Yasuhiro Tada*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）研究半满（half filling）扩展玻色-哈伯德模型（extended Bose-Hubbard model）在方形与三角梯子（square/triangular ladders）上的磁通响应，指出磁通响应属于第一类（Type-I）还是第二类（Type-II）取决于晶格几何与几何阻挫（geometric frustration）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超流（superfluid）与超导（superconductor）按对磁通的响应分为第一类与第二类，但该分类在磁通下的晶格玻色子（lattice bosons）体系中鲜有讨论，此前迈斯纳相（Meissner phase）与涡旋相（vortex phase）多在梯子几何中研究。

Method: 在半满条件下，采用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）研究含最近邻相互作用 V 的扩展玻色-哈伯德模型，在二至四腿的方形与三角梯子上计算磁通响应，并将梯子结果外推至二维（two dimensions）。

Result: 方形梯子上，涡旋相（vortex phase）仅局限于 V=0 附近；有限 V 时，磁通诱导的电荷密度波（charge-density-wave, CDW）序取代涡旋相，响应趋于第一类。三角梯子上，涡旋相在所研究整个 V 范围内持续存在，响应保持第二类特征。

Conclusion: 磁通响应的第一类或第二类性质依赖晶格几何，提示几何阻挫（geometric frustration）在其中起作用；该结论由梯子结果向二维外推得出。

Abstract: Superfluids and superconductors are classified into Type-I and Type-II according to their response to a magnetic flux, but this classification has hardly been addressed for lattice bosons under magnetic flux, where the Meissner and vortex phases have been studied mostly in ladder geometries. Here, we study the flux response of the extended Bose-Hubbard model with nearest-neighbor interaction $V$ at half filling on square and triangular ladders with two to four legs by using the density-matrix renormalization group. On the square ladders, the vortex phase is confined to a region near $V=0$. At finite $V$, a magnetic flux-induced charge-density-wave (CDW) order replaces it, and the response becomes Type-I-like. In contrast, on the triangular ladders, the vortex phase persists over the whole range of $V$ studied, and the response remains Type-II-like. By extrapolating the ladder results to two dimensions, we argue that the Type-I or Type-II character of the flux response depends on the lattice geometry, which suggests a role of geometric frustration.

</details>


### [142] [Magnetic-Field Response of a Hyperbolic Kitaev Spin Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27819)
*Tao Yang,Baigeng Wang,Rui Wang,Z. Y. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作在双曲（hyperbolic）Kekulé 着色 {8,3} 格子上，用无限投影纠缠对态（iPEPS）方法研究可解 Kitaev 自旋液体在打破通量守恒的磁场扰动下的多体响应，发现均匀场与交错 [111] 场引发截然不同且尺度不同的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲几何可产生具有不同通量结构与 Majorana 谱的 Kitaev 自旋液体（Kitaev spin liquids），但这些分数化态（fractionalized states）在超越精确可解极限后的行为尚不清楚。

Method: 在 Kekulé 着色的双曲 {8,3} 格子上发展无限投影纠缠对态（infinite projected entangled-pair state, iPEPS）方法，配合按通量分辨的 loop-gas 初始化（flux-resolved loop-gas initialization）与几何适配的张量网络收缩（geometry-adapted tensor-network contraction）；并在固定通量背景下构造三阶有效 Majorana 模型。

Result: 均匀 [111] 磁场在弱场下引起竞争性变分分支（variational branches）之间的突变；交错 [111] 磁场下极化（polarization）与平均 plaquette 通量则在更宽的场范围内演化。交换能（exchange-energy）竞争可解释两种响应尺度的差异，固定通量背景下的三阶有效 Majorana 模型显示出不同的低能谱。

Conclusion: 结果将可解双曲自旋液体的几何依赖分数化结构，与其在破坏通量守恒的物理扰动下的多体响应联系起来。

Abstract: Hyperbolic geometry can give rise to Kitaev spin liquids with distinct flux structures and Majorana spectra, raising the question of how these fractionalized states respond beyond exactly solvable limits. We address this question on the Kekulé-colored hyperbolic {8,3} lattice by developing an infinite projected entangled-pair-state approach with flux-resolved loop-gas initialization and geometry-adapted tensor-network contraction. Under uniform and staggered [111] magnetic fields, we find markedly different many-body responses. The uniform field induces a sharp change between competing variational branches at weak field, whereas the polarization and mean plaquette flux evolve over a much broader field range under the staggered field. Exchange-energy competition helps explain the different response scales, while third-order effective Majorana models in a fixed-flux background exhibit contrasting low-energy spectra. Our results connect the geometry-dependent fractionalized structure of a solvable hyperbolic spin liquid to its many-body response under physical perturbations that break flux conservation.

</details>

### [143] [nuSTORM: a unique GeV electron-(anti)neutrino machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27549)
*Xianguo Lu*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 该会议报告综述了利用加速器中微子（accelerator neutrinos）测量狄拉克 CP 相位（Dirac CP phase，δCP）的现有实验计划，并论证基于 μ 子储存环（muon storage ring）的 nuSTORM 方案可提供高质量电子（反）中微子束，从而为 δCP 测量所需的截面输入（cross-section inputs）提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: δCP 测量依赖精确的中微子截面（cross-section）输入，而现有加速器中微子实验在这一环节存在需求缺口；因此需要评估可提供相应束流质量的新概念方案。

Method: 采用会议综述与论证性评述的方式：梳理当前测量 δCP 的加速器中微子实验项目，并对 nuSTORM 概念（由 μ 子储存环产生中微子束）进行定性分析。

Result: 论证指出 nuSTORM 能够产生高质量的电子中微子与反中微子束（electron-(anti)neutrino beams），可满足 δCP 测量对截面输入束流品质的要求。

Conclusion: nuSTORM 可作为支撑 δCP 测量的关键束流技术途径，为相关截面测量提供所需的高质量电子（反）中微子束。

Abstract: In this conference contribution, I briefly review current experimental programmes measuring the Dirac CP phase δCP using accelerator neutrinos. I argue that the nuSTORM concept---neutrino beams from a muon storage ring---could provide high-quality electron-(anti)neutrino beams needed for the cross-section inputs to δCP measurements.

</details>


### [144] [Heat cleaning kinetics and thermal degradation of MOCVD-grown GaAs photocathodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27833)
*Gregory Blume,Adam Masters,Colin Kirk,Sushil Poudel,John Hill,Alimohammed Kachwala,Joseph Grames,Marcy L Stutzman,Sylvain Marsillac,Matt Grau*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 在Jefferson Lab的低压Mott极化仪中评估了集成分布布拉格反射镜的MOCVD生长GaAs/GaAsP应变量子超晶格光阴极，并建立了以表面污染覆盖度描述量子效率随加热时间变化的模型。550°C加热时量子效率上升更快、平台值约为450°C的近两倍，极化度维持在约83%；过度加热则使两者下降，反射谱共振蓝移与表面材料损失一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无砷保护帽（arsenic cap）的光阴极在运输与安装过程中会产生表面污染，热清洗可去除污染但过度加热会损伤光阴极，因此需明确热清洗过程中污染去除与光阴极材料损失之间的权衡关系。

Method: 在Jefferson Lab低压Mott极化仪（Mott polarimeter）中测试MOCVD生长的GaAs/GaAsP应变超晶格（strained-superlattice）光阴极（集成分布布拉格反射镜，distributed Bragg reflector）；建立以表面污染覆盖度（surface contaminant coverage）为变量的量子效率（quantum efficiency, QE）模型；比较450°C与550°C加热，并进行反射率（reflectance）测量与高压光阴极枪（photogun）验证。

Result: 550°C下QE上升更快且平台值约为450°C的近两倍，极化度（polarization）保持在约83%；继续加热使QE与极化度均下降。反射率测量显示光学共振（optical resonance）向短波方向移动，与表面材料损失一致；高压光阴极枪中观察到相同的QE先升后降趋势。

Conclusion: 热清洗工艺需在污染物去除与光阴极材料损失之间取得平衡，加热温度与时间应优化以使QE最大化同时保持高极化度。

Abstract: We evaluated metalorganic chemical vapor deposition (MOCVD)-grown GaAs/GaAsP strained-superlattice photocathodes with integrated distributed Bragg reflectors in a low-voltage Mott polarimeter at Jefferson Lab. Without a protective arsenic cap, surface contamination can develop during transport and installation. Heat cleaning removes this contamination by thermally desorbing material bound to the surface, but excessive heating can also damage the photocathode. We modeled quantum efficiency (QE) in terms of surface contaminant coverage to describe its increase with heating time. QE rose more rapidly and reached a plateau nearly twice as high at $550^\circ\text{C}$ as at $450^\circ\text{C}$, while polarization remained near 83%. Further heating reduced QE and polarization. Reflectance measurements showed a shift of the optical resonance to shorter wavelengths, consistent with surface material loss. We also tested the cleaning method in a high-voltage photogun, where QE showed the same initial increase and subsequent decline. The cleaning procedure must therefore balance contaminant removal against loss of photocathode material.

</details>

### [145] [Conformally flat Mercator equation and conserved quantities of conformal circles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27190)
*Prim Plansangkate,Lenka Zalabová*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究共形球面（conformal sphere）上共形墨卡托方程（conformal Mercator equation）解的性质，聚焦共形圆（conformal circles），从变分（variational）与拖拉（tractor）两条路径导出其守恒量，并建立两者之间的直接关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于摘要：共形圆守恒量可由共形基灵向量场（conformal Killing vector fields）给出，作者希望用两种不同方法加以刻画并厘清其联系。

Method: 基于摘要：在共形球面上研究共形墨卡托方程的解，针对共形圆采用变分方法与拖拉方法，由共形基灵向量场求取守恒量（conserved quantities）。

Result: 基于摘要：两种方法各自给出共形圆的守恒量集合，并发现两组量之间存在直接关系（direct relation）。

Conclusion: 基于摘要：变分与拖拉两种途径在共形圆守恒量上相互对应，其直接关系为共形墨卡托方程解的研究提供了统一视角（具体形式摘要未给出）。

Abstract: We study properties of solutions of the conformal Mercator equation on the conformal sphere. We focus on conformal circles and give two different approaches of finding their conserved quantities from conformal Killing vector fields - variational and tractorial. We find a direct relation between the two sets of quantities.

</details>


### [146] [Submanifolds, restriction of differential operators and Schouten-Nijenhuis bracket](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27912)
*J. Queva,J. Renaud*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文给出将霍奇算子（Hodge operator）、余微分（codifferential）与霍奇-拉普拉斯算子（Hodge Laplacian）限制到子流形（submanifold）上的新公式，并借助微分形式工具使公式形式简洁、便于显式计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中对霍奇算子及其相关算子限制到子流形的表述往往冗长或不便计算；本文旨在提供更简洁且可直接用于实际计算的公式。

Method: 采用微分形式（differential forms）工具，特别是斯豪滕-奈恩黑斯括号（Schouten-Nijenhuis bracket）与广义内积（generalized interior product），推导子流形上霍奇算子、余微分与霍奇-拉普拉斯算子的限制表达式。

Result: 所得公式形式简洁，且有利于在实际应用中进行显式计算。

Conclusion: 借助 Schouten-Nijenhuis 括号与广义内积可系统而简洁地刻画霍奇类算子在子流形上的限制，为相关几何计算提供实用工具。

Abstract: In this article, we present new formulas for restricting the Hodge operator, the codifferential and the Hodge Laplacian to a submanifold. These formulas are concise, thanks to the use of tools for differential forms (Schouten-Nijenhuis bracket and generalized interior product). They also facilitate explicit calculations for practical applications.

</details>
