### [147] [Beyond the Peak: Constraining early dark energy with the damping tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27069)
*Hao Wang,Gen Ye,Yun-Song Piao*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文提出“早期暗能量尾巴（EDE tail）”，即能量注入峰值之后的演化阶段，可作为探测标量场动力学的额外观测量，并显著影响其缓解哈勃张力（Hubble tension）的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期暗能量通常仅以主能量注入峰值的幅度与位置来刻画，峰后演化所携带的标量动力学信息未被充分利用，而这一信息可能与哈勃张力（Hubble tension）的缓解程度相关。

Method: 构建一个唯象框架（phenomenological framework），用多尺度标量势（multiscale scalar potential）的不同区域主导不同场尺度上的动力学；并以结合类轴子（axion-like）与α-attractor类机制的势作为代表性实现，使EDE尾巴可通过反射与不对称振荡被重塑，随后与宇宙微波背景（CMB）、重子声学振荡（BAO）及超新星观测进行对比。

Result: 与CMB、BAO和超新星数据对照后，该框架对高多极矩（high-ℓ）CMB数据的拟合有所改善，同时反演得到的最佳拟合H0更高。

Conclusion: EDE尾巴保留了传统峰值参数无法捕捉的、关于底层标量势的可观测信息，为构建和观测上区分早期暗能量情景提供了互补途径，并有望进一步缓解哈勃张力。

Abstract: Early dark energy is commonly characterized by the amplitude and location of its primary energy-injection peak. We show that its post-peak evolution, which we call the \emph{EDE tail}, provides an additional observable probe of the underlying scalar dynamics and can materially affect its ability to alleviate the Hubble tension. We construct a simple phenomenological framework in which distinct regions of a multiscale scalar potential govern the dynamics at different field scales. As a representative realization, we consider a potential that combines axion-like and $α$-attractor-like regimes, thereby allowing the EDE tail to be reshaped through reflection and asymmetric oscillations. Confronting this framework with cosmic microwave background, baryon acoustic oscillation, and supernova observations, we find an improved fit to the high-$\ell$ CMB data together with a higher inferred bestfit $H_0$. Our results show that the EDE tail retains observable information about the underlying scalar potential that is not captured by the conventional peak parameters alone. This provides a complementary route for constructing and observationally distinguishing EDE scenarios, and potentially for further alleviating the Hubble tension.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Entropy production from crossing a quantum separatrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26737)
*Peter Stabel,James R. Anglin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在大准连续能谱的极端半经典极限下，所有量子分界线（quantum separatrix）构成一个普适的单参数族，穿越分界线的演化具有高效遍历性；对参数时间依赖作平均后，分界线穿越按普适公式产生对角熵（diagonal entropy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量子系统的参数依赖能谱常形成密集的避免交叉（avoided crossings）晶格，参数缓慢变化时演化会在一窄能量区间内由绝热（adiabatic）转为非绝热（diabatic），该区间此前被定义为量子分界线，但其在半经典极限下的普适性质与统计后果尚不明确。

Method: 将系统置于准连续稠密能谱的极端半经典极限，分析穿越避免交叉的分界线演化；并对参数时间依赖的微小变化作现实的平均（unitary evolution 的平均化）以提取普适行为，辅以数值算例检验有限系统。

Result: 证明所有量子分界线在该极限下形成一个普适单参数族，穿越分界线的演化变得高效遍历化（ergodizing）；对参数时间依赖作平均后，分界线穿越按一普适公式生成对角熵。数值算例表明准连续谱的结论在有限系统中仍清晰可辨，仅存在较小的有限尺寸效应（finite-size effects）。

Conclusion: 量子分界线穿越具有普适的统计结构，可由单参数族与普适熵公式刻画；该结果在有限系统中近似成立，为理解慢驱动大量子系统的绝热—非绝热转变及熵产生提供了普适框架。

Abstract: If the Hamiltonian of a large quantum system depends on a variable parameter, its parameter-dependent energy spectrum may often form a dense lattice of avoided crossings. If the parameter slowly changes, the system's time evolution through the avoided crossings tends in the semi-classical limit to change from adiabatic to diabatic across a narrow energy range that has recently been defined as a quantum separatrix. Here we show that in the extreme semi-classical limit of a quasi-continuously dense spectrum, all quantum separatrices form a universal one-parameter family, and that evolution through the separatrix becomes efficiently ergodizing. If the unitary evolution is realistically averaged over small variations in the parameter time dependence, quantum separatrix crossing generates diagonal entropy according to a universal formula. Numerical examples confirm that the results for quasi-continuous spectra remain clearly recognizable in finite systems, with only small finite-size effects.

</details>


### [149] [Consecutive Supersymmetric Quantum Mechanical Algorithms for Resolving the Spectra of Legendre-type Differential Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27295)
*Tianchun Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于连续超对称量子力学（supersymmetric quantum mechanics, SUSYQM）启发式地构造了若干迭代算法，用于求解勒让德算子（Legendre operator）及其关联算子在多种坐标参数化下的本征谱，且不依赖任何传统解析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解勒让德型算子本征谱多依赖幂级数法（power series approach）等传统解析手段；作者希望建立不依赖这些传统方法、仅以算子内在对称性为原理的独立算法框架。

Method: 将算子中隐藏的两种对称性——超对称（supersymmetry）与形状不变性对称（shape invariance symmetry）——作为唯一主导原理，辅以新设计的代数技巧：主动超对称化变换（active supersymmetrization transformations）、动量算子映射（momentum operator maps）以及普通与高阶自旋矩阵映射（spin matrix maps），据此启发式构造连续迭代算法。

Result: 所构造的算法在代数上简洁优雅、效率高，且独立于传统解析方法；部分算法有望替代广泛使用的幂级数法来处理勒让德型算子的本征谱。

Conclusion: 超对称与形状不变性可作为求解勒让德型算子本征谱的自足代数原理，配合所述映射技术可形成高效、不依赖传统方法的候选算法体系。

Abstract: Quite comprehensive iterative algorithms based on consecutive supersymmetric quantum mechanics (SUSYQM) have been constructed heuristically, for resolving the eigenspectra of the Legendre operator and its associates parametrized in various coordinates, \emph{without receiving any input from traditional analytical methods}. On building these algorithms, the two symmetries \text{--} supersymmetry and shape invariance symmetry \text{--} hidden in these operators are brought to the fore as the only two governing principles, with the aids of the newly devised algebraic techniques such as the \emph{active supersymmetrization transformations}, \emph{momentum operator maps} as well as plain and \emph{higher-order spin matrix maps}. In view of their algebraic simplicity and elegance, high efficiency, as well as independence of traditional methods, some of these algorithms become the promising candidates to supplant the popular power series approach to the eigenspectra of Legendre-type operators.

</details>


### [150] [The inverted harmonic oscillator in Segal-Bargmann space: Time-asymmetric propagator reconstruction and bi-coherent states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27458)
*Hongze Ding,Ni Liu,J. -Q. Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究倒谐振子（inverted harmonic oscillator, IHO）的实时动力学与共振量子化，给出其精确费曼传播子（Feynman propagator），并构造双相干态（bi-coherent states）以复现经典双曲轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倒谐振子的自伴哈密顿量具有实连续谱，但其共振态位于 $L^2(\mathbb{R})$ 之外且能量为纯虚数，实时动力学与共振量子化之间的关系尚需系统刻画。

Method: 将 Moriconi 的坐标-时间变换推广至实时路径积分，结合 Mehler 公式推导精确费曼核；并在复伸缩的 Segal-Bargmann 表示中分析两个哈密顿量的单侧压缩半群结构。

Result: 共振态以复 Hermite 多项式表达并在 Fresnel 高斯配对下双正交；离散展开在各自时间半轴上单独重构完整酉核，衰减的 Gamow-Siegert 分支在 $T>0$ 收敛、增长的反共振分支在 $T<0$ 收敛。

Conclusion: 所构造的双相干态其位置与动量二阶累积量为纯虚且互为共轭、乘积为 $\hbar^2/4$，Heisenberg 期望值对任意实初值遵循精确经典双曲轨迹。

Abstract: We investigate the real-time dynamics and resonance quantization of the inverted harmonic oscillator (IHO). Its self-adjoint Hamiltonian on $L^2(\mathbb{R})$ has a real continuous spectrum, while generalized resonance states outside $L^2(\mathbb{R})$ have purely imaginary energies $\pm i\hbarω(n+1/2)$. In coordinate space, these states are expressed in terms of complex Hermite polynomials and are biorthonormal under the Fresnel Gaussian pairing. Adapting Moriconi's coordinate-time transformation to the real-time path integral, we derive the exact Feynman propagator. Using Mehler's formula, we show that each discrete expansion alone reconstructs the full unitary kernel on its time half-axis, with the decaying Gamow-Siegert branch converging for $T>0$ and the growing antiresonant branch converging for $T<0$. In the complex-scaled Segal-Bargmann representation, the two Hamiltonians take the form $\pm i\hbarω(z(d/dz)+1/2)$, and their monomial eigenfunctions map back to the dual complex Hermite states. The two convergence domains correspond to one-sided contraction semigroups of dilations. Finally, we construct bi-coherent states whose biorthogonal position and momentum second cumulants are purely imaginary and complex conjugate, with product $\hbar^2/4$. The corresponding Heisenberg expectation values follow the exact classical hyperbolic trajectories for arbitrary real initial positions and momenta.

</details>


### [151] [Emergent Causality of Macroscopic Transport and Equilibration in Long-Range Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27650)
*Hongchao Li,Marius Lemm*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明长程量子多体系统在热力学极限下涌现出严格的有效光锥，并给出最优标度及平衡时间的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程耦合（long-range couplings）挑战了传统的局域性（locality）与因果性（causality）观念，因此需要厘清宏观粒子输运是否仍受某种因果约束。

Method: 针对 D 维空间中衰减指数 α∈(D, D+1) 的幂律跃迁（power-law hopping），分析宏观粒子输运所对应的涌现因果锥（emergent causality cone）标度，并构造 α∈(1, 3/2) 的长程自由费米子链（free-fermion chain）实例。

Result: 涌现因果锥呈超弹道式（super-ballistically）扩张且该标度被证明最优；结果给出长程系统平衡时间（equilibration time）的下界；所构造实例的宏观密度在亚线性于系统尺寸的时间内完成平衡。

Conclusion: 长程跃迁可导致加速平衡（accelerated equilibration），与“长程相互作用因能垒增大而减慢平衡”的通常认识形成对照。

Abstract: Long-range couplings in quantum many-body systems challenge conventional notions of locality and causality. Here, we show that macroscopic particle transport nevertheless exhibits emergent causality --- an effective light cone that becomes strict in the thermodynamic limit. For power-law hopping with a decay exponent $α\in(D,D+1)$ in $D$ spatial dimensions, the emergent causality cone expands super-ballistically, and we establish that its scaling is optimal. Our results imply lower bounds on the equilibration time of long-range systems. Finally, we construct an example of a long-range free-fermion chain with $α\in (1,3/2)$, whose macroscopic density equilibrates super-ballistically, i.e., in a time sub-linear in the system size. While long-range interactions are usually associated with slow equilibration due to increased energy barriers, our example demonstrates that long-range hopping can lead to accelerated equilibration.

</details>


### [152] [Detection of genuine multipartite entanglement by means of entanglement monotones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27660)
*Sylvain Carrozza,Johann Chevrier,Luca Lionni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义“不可约纠缠（irreducible entanglement）”这一强形式真多体纠缠，即D体态（D≥3）无法分解为至多(D−1)体纠缠态的乘积；并证明不存在同时满足LOCC单调性与次可加性的合理真多体纠缠熵，转而构造LO单调的单调量以探测三量子比特系统中的各类真三体纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是寻找可作为“不可约纠缠”证书的纯态纠缠单调量（entanglement monotones），即为一种更强形式的真多体纠缠（genuine multipartite entanglement）提供可操作的判据。

Method: 先建立一个不可行性（no-go）结果，证明任何合理的真多体纠缠熵不能同时满足LOCC单调性（LOCC-monotonicity）与次可加性（subadditivity）；随后将前者放宽为LO单调性（LO-monotonicity），给出生成具备所需性质的真多体纠缠单调量的简单方法，并聚焦其子族进行分析。

Result: 得到一项不可行性定理：不存在同时满足LOCC单调性与次可加性的合理真多体纠缠熵。在放宽为LO单调性后，所构造的单调量子族能够探测三量子比特（three-qubit）系统中任意形式的真三体纠缠（genuine tripartite entanglement）。

Conclusion: 不可约纠缠的证书需在LOCC单调性与次可加性之间作出取舍；采用LO单调性可系统构造有效的真多体纠缠单调量，并在三量子比特情形下实现完备的真三体纠缠探测。

Abstract: We look for pure state entanglement monotones that may serve as certificates of a strong form of genuine multipartite entanglement, that we refer to as irreducible entanglement: a $D$-partite state (with $D\geq 3$) is irreducibly $D$-partite entangled if it cannot be decomposed as a product of states that are at most $(D-1)$-partite entangled. We start by establishing a no-go result proving that no reasonable genuine multipartite entanglement entropy can simultaneously obey LOCC-monotonicity and subadditivity. After relaxing the first condition to LO-monotonicity, we provide a simple method generating genuine multipartite entanglement monotones with the desired properties. Focusing on a subfamily of these monotones, we show that they are able to detect any form of genuine tripartite entanglement in three-qubit systems.

</details>


### [153] [Quantum conditional relative entropy and quasi-factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27769)
*Angelo Lucia,Ángela Capel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该综述聚焦量子系统缺乏条件期望映射（conditional expectation map）的问题，梳理量子条件相对熵（quantum conditional relative entropy）的多重非等价定义及其性质，并回顾其强、弱形式的准因子化（quasi-factorization）结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态一般无法定义保持期望值的条件期望映射，这使量子条件相对熵缺乏唯一自然定义；而经典条件相对熵的准因子化性质是证明对数索伯列夫不等式（logarithmic Sobolev inequalities）等自旋系统结果的关键，故需在量子情形下建立类似工具。

Method: 采用综述与理论分析方式：讨论在条件期望映射不存在时，量子条件相对熵存在多种非等价定义，分析其性质，并系统回顾近期关于量子条件相对熵强、弱形式准因子化（近似张量化，approximate tensorization）的研究结果。

Result: 指出量子条件相对熵不存在唯一定义，存在多种非等价刻画；并总结近期文献中已获得的强形式与弱形式准因子化结论，为量子自旋系统中量子对数索伯列夫不等式等应用提供基础。

Conclusion: 量子条件相对熵定义的非唯一性构成额外复杂性，但准因子化结果仍可作为经典方法向量子情形推广的关键途径，支撑量子对数索伯列夫不等式等应用。

Abstract: One of the characteristic features that distinguishes quantum systems from classical ones is the inability, except for some special cases, of defining a conditional expectation map for a given quantum state, namely a map that restricts observables to a given sub-region (or more generally, a sub-algebra of observables) while preserving expectation values. Of the many consequences of this lack of conditional expectation, we consider the problem of defining the quantum conditional relative entropy, i.e., the relative entropy between two quantum states conditioned to a sub-region or sub-algebra. We discuss how, when the conditional expectation map is not defined, there are multiple, non-equivalent ways to define a quantum conditional relative entropy quantity, and we present some of their properties.
  Classical conditional relative entropy has played a fundamental role in many applications to spin systems, and in particular in proving logarithmic Sobolev inequalities. A key feature of conditional relative entropy that intervenes in these results is its quasi-factorization or approximate tensorization property: informally, the relative entropy is almost super-additive over weakly-dependent sub-algebras. It is then not surprising that the key to obtaining similar applications in the quantum setting, such as quantum logarithmic Sobolev inequalities in spin systems, has been to obtain approximate factorization results for the quantum conditional relative entropy, with the added complication that there is not a unique definition of conditional relative entropy to work with. We review the recent results regarding quasi-factorization of the quantum conditional relative entropy, in strong and weak forms.

</details>


### [154] [Complex chaotic Boltzmann machines as deterministic representations of quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27779)
*J. Montes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种复数混沌玻尔兹曼机（complex chaotic Boltzmann machine），将纯量子态映射为确定性动力系统，通过超立方体轨迹几何统一刻画量子态的概率与相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络与张量网络（tensor networks）为量子体系波函数（wave function）提供了参数化途径，但将其扩展到动力学表示、并同时容纳玻恩概率（Born probabilities）与相对相位（relative phases）的框架尚待建立。

Method: 构建复数混沌玻尔兹曼机，将确定性的动力系统（dynamical system）指派给纯量子态；推广 Suzuki 的构造，使玻恩概率决定超立方体（hypercube）中的轨迹速度，相位则定义提升其庞加莱截面（Poincare sections）的复场；演化由网络参数与初始条件确定，飞行与位形变化中不含随机选择。

Result: 对两量子比特（two qubits）的分析及三量子比特族（three-qubit families）的研究表明：概率之间的关系组织出规则曲线（regular curves）、岛（islands）与不稳定区（unstable regions）；相位可区分具有相同玻恩动力学的不同量子态。

Conclusion: 该架构将波函数参数化转化为轨迹几何（geometry of trajectories），提供了在态族（families of states）层面共同探索概率、相位与对称性（symmetries）的工具。

Abstract: Neural networks and tensor networks have opened new ways to explore quantum systems by parameterizing families of wave functions through the structure and parameters of a network. Here we extend this perspective to a dynamical representation: we introduce a complex chaotic Boltzmann machine that assigns a deterministic system to a pure quantum state. We extend Suzuki's construction to incorporate both Born probabilities and relative phases. The probabilities set the trajectory velocities in a hypercube, while the phases define a complex field that lifts its Poincare sections. The evolution is determined by the network parameters and initial conditions, without random choices during flights or configuration changes. Our analysis of two qubits and study of three-qubit families show how relations between probabilities organize regular curves, islands, and unstable regions, and how phases distinguish states that share the same Born dynamics. This architecture turns a wave-function parameterization into a geometry of trajectories, providing a tool to explore probabilities, phases, and symmetries together across families of states.

</details>


### [155] [Tackling Many-Body Coulomb Singularity by Randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27851)
*Di Fang,Wook Yoon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出，库仑奇异性（Coulomb singularity）会使Trotter乘积公式与qDRIFT在含连续库仑相互作用的多体积分中丧失标称收敛阶，甚至误差随步数仅以对数速率下降；为此提出supersmoothing qDRIFT，可在无光滑性与能量假设下对所有初态实现误差随时间步线性下降，并在端到端门数上优于qubitization与高阶Trotter方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体库仑动力学（many-body Coulomb dynamics）在量子化学与分子模拟中处于核心地位，但库仑奇异性同时破坏了标准误差分析与乘积公式（product formulas）的性能，使得即便氢原子基态这类简单情形，Trotter公式也达不到名义阶数，且提高阶数无济于事；随机化（randomization）本身同样无法解决该问题。

Method: 论文先证明qDRIFT与Trotter公式存在同类精度损失，且对一般初态二者误差可慢于时间步数的任意幂次（即对数级）。随后提出supersmoothing qDRIFT——一种随机化乘积公式，将自由粒子传播（free particle propagation）的物理性质转化为算法资源，并结合空间离散化（spatial discretization）分析给出端到端门数估计。

Result: supersmoothing qDRIFT获得随时间步线性下降的误差界，且该界对所有初态一致成立，不依赖光滑性或能量假设；结合空间离散化后，尽管时间上仅为一阶收敛，其端到端门数（gate count）仍优于直接作用于离散化哈密顿量的qubitization与高阶Trotter公式。

Conclusion: 结果表明，连续动力学（continuum dynamics）的物理特性可被转化为算法优势，为克服库仑奇异性导致的收敛性与复杂度瓶颈提供了新路径。

Abstract: Many-body Coulomb dynamics is central to quantum chemistry and molecular simulation, yet the Coulomb singularity undermines both the standard error analysis and performance of product formulas. Even for the simple hydrogen ground state, the celebrated Trotter formulas converge more slowly than their nominal order, and going to higher order does not help. Randomization by itself does not resolve this. We show that conventional qDRIFT suffers the same loss of accuracy, and that for general initial states, the errors of both Trotter formulas and qDRIFT can decrease more slowly than any power of the number of time steps (logarithmically). To overcome these limitations, we introduce supersmoothing qDRIFT, a randomized product formula that turns a physical property of free particle propagation into an algorithmic resource. Our algorithm achieves an error bound that decreases linearly with the time step, uniformly over \textit{all} initial states and without any smoothness or energy assumptions. Combined with spatial discretization analysis, the resulting end-to-end gate count improves on those of qubitization and high-order Trotter formulas applied directly to the discretized Hamiltonian, despite first-order convergence in time. This work demonstrates new ways of utilizing continuum dynamics to achieve algorithmic advantage.

</details>


### [156] [Quantum computing for gearing down complex many-fermion problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27402)
*Weijie Du,Filippo Fornetti,James P. Vary*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种量子-经典混合框架，通过识别紧凑单粒子基（compact single-particle basis）将难解的多费米子问题拉回经典计算可及范围，并以模拟退火（simulated annealing）驱动的自适应搜索联合优化自然轨道变换与活性空间选择，在氧同位素核结构上完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管算法与原理性实验已有进展，在含噪中等规模量子（noisy intermediate-scale quantum, NISQ）设备上开展大规模第一性原理（ab initio）计算仍具挑战；论文试图在保留精确基态描述的前提下，使原本不可行的多费米子问题变得经典可解。

Method: 构建量子-经典框架，设计由模拟退火（simulated annealing）驱动的自适应搜索方案，联合优化自然轨道引导的变换（natural-orbital-guided transformations）与活性空间选择（active-space selections）；并以低深度电路（low-depth circuits）的量子辅助基态估计器（quantum-assisted ground-state estimator）在放宽精度要求下为搜索提供指引。

Result: 该框架在氧同位素（oxygen isotopes）的核结构问题上展示了适用性；量子辅助估计器以低深度电路和较低的精度要求即可为基组搜索提供有效引导。

Conclusion: 该工作为借助量子辅助的基组约化（quantum-assisted basis reduction）处理跨学科的复杂第一性原理多费米子问题开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum computing unlocks exciting opportunities to extend first-principles calculations of quantum many-body systems beyond classical computing limits. Despite advances in algorithms and proof-of-principle experiments, large-scale {\it ab initio} calculations on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices remain challenging. We introduce a quantum-classical framework that identifies compact single-particle bases to bring otherwise intractable many-fermion problems within classical reach while preserving an accurate ground-state description. We develop an efficient adaptive search scheme driven by simulated annealing that jointly optimizes natural-orbital-guided transformations and active-space selections. A quantum-assisted ground-state estimator provides search guidance using low-depth circuits and relaxed accuracy requirements, making the framework promising for NISQ devices. We demonstrate the framework's applicability for the nuclear structure of the oxygen isotopes. Our framework opens a new route to tackling complex {\it ab initio} many-fermion problems across disciplines through quantum-assisted basis reduction.

</details>


### [157] [The moments and asymptotic spectral distribution of the Grassmannian ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27914)
*Aryan Gadhave,Asghar Ghorbanpour,Liam Hickey,Masoud Khalkhali,Allison Klingler,Nathan Pagliaroli,Andrew Parks*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由双分体希尔伯特空间（bipartite Hilbert space）中随机子空间投影的部分迹（partial trace）构造的随机密度矩阵（random density matrices）集合，利用 Weingarten 演算显式计算其各阶迹矩（tracial moments），并给出谱分布收敛、渐近自由性（asymptotic freeness）及平均纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）展开等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该集合旨在刻画“典型”量子子空间（typical quantum subspace）对应的随机密度矩阵，从而为量子信息中典型子空间模型的谱性质与纠缠性质提供严格数学基础。

Method: 在 Grassmannian 上按酉群（unitary group）作用的推前测度（pushforward measure）取随机子空间，对其投影取部分迹得到密度算子；借助 Weingarten 演算（Weingarten calculus）显式计算密度算子的全部迹矩及约化密度算子的混合迹矩。

Result: 证明密度算子的经验谱分布（empirical spectral distribution）几乎必然收敛到重标度的 Marchenko-Pastur 律；并证明该集合可写成渐近自由随机密度算子之和，由此给出谱分布收敛的替代证明；最后计算平均纠缠熵的渐近展开。

Conclusion: 该工作通过矩方法将随机子空间密度矩阵的谱行为与自由概率论（free probability）框架联系起来，并给出纠缠熵的渐近刻画，为典型量子子空间的统计性质提供了解析结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we study random density matrices constructed by taking partial traces of projections onto random subspaces of a bipartite Hilbert space $\mathcal{H}_A \otimes \mathcal{H}_{B}$ distributed according to the pushforward measure of the action of the unitary group on the Grassmannian $\text{Gr}_{n}(\mathcal{H}_A \otimes \mathcal{H}_{B})$. This ensemble models a `typical' quantum subspace. All tracial moments of the density operator, as well as mixed tracial moments of reduced density operators, are computed explicitly using Weingarten calculus. There are several consequences of these results. In particular, we prove that the empirical spectral distribution of the density operator converges almost surely to a rescaled Marchenko-Pastur law. We also prove that this ensemble can be written as a sum of asymptotically free random density operators, leading to an alternative proof of the convergence of the spectral distribution. Finally, we compute the asymptotic expansion of the average entanglement entropy.

</details>


### [158] [Thermalization and quantum chaos in a pure gauge discrete non-Abelian lattice gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26695)
*Marco Vaia,Edoardo Ballini,Alberto Bottarelli,Matteo M. Wauters,Philipp Hauke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于离散非阿贝尔规范群（non-Abelian gauge group）$D_3$ 的格点规范理论（lattice gauge theory）的热化性质，通过能谱统计、局域算符期望值与纠缠谱实时动力学，发现其行为与本征态热化假说（Eigenstate Thermalization Hypothesis, ETH）基本一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范理论（gauge theories）是微观物理规律的基础，但远离平衡时系统如何热化（thermalize）仍是统计物理、高能物理与凝聚态物理交叉处的开放问题；且 $D_3$ 群适合当前基于量子比特/量子多态（qudit）的量子模拟器（quantum simulators）实现。

Method: 采用去除非物理自由度的规范固定（gauge-fixed）表述，研究一维线性 plaquette 链；对哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）能谱与局域算符（local operators）期望值进行数值分析，并考察纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）的实时动力学演化。

Result: 能谱与本征态期望值的行为总体符合 ETH 预言；纠缠谱动力学显示，在初始瞬态（transient）之后出现随机矩阵（random-matrix）能级统计。

Conclusion: 结果扩展了二面体（dihedral）非阿贝尔规范系统的现象学；在恰当分辨相关对称结构（symmetry structure）后，该模型展现出与阿贝尔规范模型及一般不可积量子多体系统相同的基于 ETH 的热化图像。

Abstract: Gauge theories constitute the basis of our microscopic description of fundamental physical laws, and they are a cornerstone of contemporary scientific research. Yet, how such systems thermalize far from equilibrium remains a fundamental open question at the crossroads of statistical, high-energy, and condensed-matter physics. In this work, we investigate the thermalization properties of non-Abelian lattice gauge theories, focusing on the discrete gauge group $D_3$, which is ideally suited for current qudit-based quantum simulators. Specifically, we study a linear plaquette chain in a gauge-fixed formulation that removes unphysical degrees of freedom. A numerical analysis of the Hamiltonian spectrum and expectation values of local operators shows a behavior broadly aligned with the Eigenstate Thermalization Hypothesis (ETH). We also study thermalization through the real-time dynamics of the entanglement spectrum, confirming the emergence of random-matrix level statistics after an initial transient. Our results expand the rich phenomenology of dihedral non-Abelian gauge systems. Once the relevant symmetry structure is properly resolved, the model displays the same qualitative ETH-based thermalization scenario observed in Abelian gauge models and generic non-integrable quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [159] [Resonance-controlled post-selected mode entanglement in one-dimensional quantum scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26960)
*Víctor M. Villalba*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文以一维量子散射模型中两个独立电子穿越对称双势垒结构、后选择（post-selection）单反射单透射扇区为对象，用指数局域尖点势（cusp barriers）替代理想化δ势，借助修正贝塞尔函数（modified Bessel functions）精确求解单垒散射，并用Fabry-Pérot组合与全双中心薛定谔方程验证，表明共振控制的模式纠缠（mode entanglement）是良好分离双垒几何的稳健特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作中的理想化δ函数势垒（delta-function barriers）为零程近似，其对共振控制的局域后选择纠缠结果是否只是理想化产物尚不清楚，需用有限宽度势垒检验结论的稳健性。

Method: 采用指数局域尖点势 U(x)=U_0 exp(-|x∓d/2|/a)，其单垒散射问题可由虚阶修正贝塞尔函数精确求解；推导反射/透射振幅并检验电流归一化与数值积分；再以Fabry-Pérot组合构建双垒，并以完整双中心薛定谔方程量化其精度。

Result: 良好分离情形下该近似高精度重现全解的共振位置；并发度（concurrence）零点由共振相位条件 δ(κ)+κd=nπ 控制，δ为连续展开的单垒反射相位。后选择概率 P_RT 的计算显示，大的条件并发度可出现在低概率扇区；对垒宽、积分强度和参考动量的系统扫描表明，有限宽度可移动非共振并发度极大值位置，并视参考动量降低其可达高度。

Conclusion: 共振控制的条件纠缠是良好分离双垒几何的稳健特征，而非零程理想化的假象。

Abstract: We study resonance-controlled post-selected mode entanglement in a one-dimensional quantum-scattering model. Two incoming electrons scatter independently from a symmetric double-barrier structure and the sector with one reflected and one transmitted electron is selected. The idealised delta-function barriers used in earlier work are replaced by exponentially localised cusp barriers, $U(x)=U_0\exp(-|x\mp d/2|/a)$, for which the single-barrier scattering problem is exactly solvable in terms of modified Bessel functions of imaginary order. We derive the corresponding reflection and transmission amplitudes, verify their current normalisation and validate them independently by direct numerical integration. Two separated cusp barriers are then combined through a Fabry-Pérot composition, whose accuracy is quantified by solving the full two-centre Schrödinger equation. In the well-separated regime the approximation reproduces the full resonance positions to high accuracy. The concurrence zeros are controlled by the resonance phase condition $δ(κ)+κd=nπ$, with a continuously unwrapped single-barrier reflection phase $δ$. We also calculate the post-selection probability $P_{RT}$, showing explicitly that large conditional concurrence can occur in a low-probability sector. Systematic scans in barrier width, integrated strength and reference momentum show that finite width can shift off-resonant concurrence maxima and, depending on the reference momentum, reduce their attainable height. The results identify resonance-controlled conditional entanglement as a robust feature of the well-separated double-barrier geometry rather than an artefact of the zero-range idealisation.

</details>


### [160] [Augmenting Quantum Neural Networks with Contractive Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27019)
*Bolin Huang,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种“收缩层（contractive layer）”，由单一可训练参数控制全连接耦合，可在仅增加极少参数的情况下提升量子神经网络（quantum neural networks）的表达能力（expressivity），并在可观测学习与图像识别任务上优于单纯加深砖墙电路（brick-wall circuits）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典神经网络通过互补功能的多层结构实现强表达能力，受此启发，量子神经网络亟需通过新型量子层设计来增强表达能力；尤其需要一类能在仅引入少量额外可训练参数的前提下扩展可及函数空间的层。

Method: 引入收缩层作为量子神经网络的基础构建模块，该层由单一可训练参数支配全连接耦合（all-to-all couplings），可高效调控量子信息流，并保持与里德堡原子阵列（Rydberg atom arrays）的实验相关性；以可观测学习（observable learning）为基准，并与加深传统砖墙电路的做法进行对比。

Result: 收缩层比单纯增加砖墙电路深度更高效地提升表达能力，其优势源于每个可训练参数所对应的可及目标空间维度（target-space dimension）系统性增加；在图像识别任务中也观察到类似改进。

Conclusion: 收缩层为在近期量子设备（near-term quantum devices）上构建高表达能力量子神经网络提供了切实可行的途径。

Abstract: Classical neural networks combine diverse layers with complementary roles to achieve rich expressive capabilities across complex learning tasks. Rapid advances in quantum science and technology have motivated growing interest in their quantum counterparts, quantum neural networks, raising the question of how their expressivity can be enhanced through the design of new quantum layers. Of particular interest are layers that enrich the accessible function space while introducing only a small number of additional trainable parameters. In this Letter, we introduce the contractive layer as a promising building block for quantum neural networks. This layer comprises all-to-all couplings governed by a single trainable parameter, enabling efficient control of quantum information flow while maintaining experimental relevance to Rydberg atom arrays. Using observable learning as a benchmark, we show that contractive layers enhance expressivity more efficiently than simply increasing the depth of conventional brick-wall circuits. This advantage stems from a systematic increase in the accessible target-space dimension per trainable parameter. We further demonstrate similar improvements in image recognition tasks. These results highlight contractive layers as a practical route to expressive quantum neural networks on near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [161] [Physical Learning for Autonomous Tuning of Spin Qubits in Electrostatically Defined Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27140)
*Leonardo Gaytan,Bradley Lloyd,Paul Kliewer,Chandler Wilburn,Lincoln D. Carr,Adam McCaughan,Sonia Buckley,Meenakshi Singh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于现场可编程门阵列（FPGA）的在环学习范式，利用多路复用梯度下降（MGD）实现量子点自主调节，无需数字计算开销，适用于未来低温CMOS集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静电定义量子点的调谐是半导体自旋量子比特（spin qubits）可扩展性的主要瓶颈；现有机器学习方案依赖全局信息、引入延迟瓶颈，并消耗低温级有限的制冷功率。

Method: 在FPGA与仿真量子点器件上实现在环学习系统，采用多路复用梯度下降（multiplexed gradient descent, MGD）进行调节，绕过数字计算开销，且无需预先知晓器件的交叉电容矩阵（cross-capacitance matrix）。

Result: 系统可自主跟踪环境漂移、从离散电荷跳变中动态恢复、在低信噪比（low SNR）下维持稳态稳定，并能遍历电荷稳定图（charge-stability landscape）将量子点稳定在最优工作点。

Conclusion: 结果展示了一种“就地计算（compute-in-place）”平台，其逻辑与功耗规模适合低温CMOS（cryo-CMOS）集成，为自旋量子比特提供对静电漂移、噪声变化及二能级涨落具有鲁棒性的自动调谐系统。

Abstract: Tuning electrostatically defined quantum dots remains a primary bottleneck in the scalability of semiconductor spin qubits. While existing solutions rely on machine learning algorithms, these approaches require global information, introduce latency bottlenecks, and tax the limited cooling power available at cryogenic stages. Here, we demonstrate an FPGA-in-the-loop learning paradigm that bypasses digital computing overhead by utilizing multiplexed gradient descent (MGD) for tuning. Implemented on an FPGA and an emulated quantum dot device, our system autonomously tracks environmental drift, dynamically recovers from discrete charge jumps, and maintains steady-state stabilization in low signal-to-noise ratio regimes, without prior knowledge of the device's cross-capacitance matrix. The system also navigates the charge-stability landscape to stabilize the quantum dot around optimal operating points. These results demonstrate a ``compute-in-place'' platform, with a logic and power footprint suited to future cryo-CMOS integration, that provides autonomous stabilization of quantum dots at desired operating points, creating an auto-tuning system for spin qubits that is resilient to electrostatic drifts, changing noise profiles, and two-level fluctuations.

</details>


### [162] [geodesiq: a Python package for geometric diabatic-adiabatic optimal state transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27535)
*David Fernández-Fernández,Christian Ventura-Meinersen,Maximilian Rimbach-Russ,Gloria Platero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文介绍开源 Python 包 geodesiq，其以几何框架设计控制脉冲（control pulses），在多个能级量子系统（multilevel quantum systems）中实现高保真态转移（high-fidelity state transfer）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多能级量子系统中的态转移需兼顾抑制非目标激发与保留选定的透热通道（diabatic passage），现有方法缺乏统一的几何描述与便捷工具。

Method: 将绝热（adiabatic）与透热（diabatic）控制整合进单一度规（metric），把脉冲求解约化为求解一个一阶常微分方程（first-order ordinary differential equation），并以 Python 包形式实现。

Result: 论文说明 geodesiq 的使用方式，并在 Landau-Zener 跃迁（Landau-Zener transitions）、自旋量子比特初始化（spin-qubit initialization）、绝热多体基态制备（adiabatic many-body ground-state preparation）以及四能级系统中的透热-绝热转移（diabatic-adiabatic transfer）等情形中给出示例。

Conclusion: geodesiq 提供了基于几何方法、使用简便的控制脉冲设计工具，可覆盖从单跃迁到多体基态制备的多种量子控制任务。

Abstract: We introduce the geodesiq Python package, which implements a geometric framework for shaping control pulses that achieve high-fidelity state transfer in multilevel quantum systems. The underlying method combines adiabatic and diabatic control into a single metric, suppressing unwanted excitations while allowing selected diabatic passages. Finding the pulse thereby reduces to solving a single first-order ordinary differential equation. We explain how to use geodesiq and illustrate its use explicitly with Landau-Zener transitions, spin-qubit initialization, adiabatic many-body ground-state preparation, and a diabatic-adiabatic transfer in a four-level system.

</details>


### [163] [Autonomous quantum machine learning via Riemannian gradient-based optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27815)
*Kasidit Srimahajariyapong,Thiparat Chotibut,Suwannachad Suwannajitt,Tithivich Paka,Ricard Puig,Yudai Suzuki,Marek Gluza,Zoë Holmes,Supanut Thanasilp*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于黎曼梯度优化（Riemannian gradient optimization）的自主自训练量子机器学习（autonomous self-training quantum machine learning）框架，使训练完全在量子域内进行，无需量子测量或经典反馈，并给出指数收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子机器学习模型（quantum machine learning models）依赖重复量子测量与经典优化，量子到经典的信息提取过程（quantum-to-classical information-extraction）成为可扩展性的根本瓶颈。

Method: 基于黎曼梯度优化推导一大类学习任务的黎曼梯度更新规则（Riemannian gradient update rules），并给出训练动力学可完全自主实现的充分条件；在理论层面建立训练迭代上的指数收敛保证（exponential convergence guarantee），并分析电路深度（circuit depth）增长带来的训练代价。

Result: 在步长足够小时证明指数收敛；训练所得模型可在无先验指定（a priori specification）的情况下自动发现训练数据中隐藏的对称性（symmetries）；在量子相分类（quantum phase classification）与含隐藏对称性的幺正学习（unitary learning）数值实验中，仅需少量不同训练样本即可在低电路深度下完成学习。

Conclusion: 自主量子机器学习（autonomous quantum machine learning）构成一种有前景的范式：去除经典优化循环（classical optimization loop），同时保留较强的理论保证与实际性能。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning models typically rely on repeated quantum measurements and classical optimization during training, making the quantum-to-classical information-extraction process a fundamental bottleneck for scalability. We propose a framework for autonomous self-training quantum machine learning, based on Riemannian gradient optimization, in which the optimization proceeds entirely within the quantum domain without requiring quantum measurements or classical feedback. We derive the Riemannian gradient update rules for a broad class of learning tasks and identify sufficient conditions under which the resulting training dynamics can be realized fully autonomously. Within this regime, we establish an exponential convergence guarantee, in the training iterations, for sufficiently small step sizes, and further analyze the training cost in terms of the growth of the circuit depth. Importantly, we further show that the trained model can automatically discover symmetries hidden in the training data without $\textit{a priori}$ specification. For the canonical examples of quantum phase classification and unitary learning with a hidden symmetry, our numerical study indicates that learning can be achieved at low circuit depths with only a few distinct training examples. Our results thus establish autonomous quantum machine learning as a promising paradigm that removes the classical optimization loop while retaining strong theoretical guarantees and practical performances.

</details>


### [164] [Optimised 8th-Order Trotter-Suzuki Schemes for Quantum Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27896)
*Marko Maležič,Johann Ostmeyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将优化框架扩展至8阶，构造出更高效的组合方案及首批8阶通用乘积公式（product formulas），在理论效率与含噪模拟中评估其相对现有方案的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体量子动力学模拟是量子计算机的重要应用，乘积公式可直接映射为量子门；提高阶数n有望降低门深度，但此前6阶以上方案仅为二阶方案组合，通用乘积公式仅至6阶，缺乏更高阶的高效构造。

Method: 将既有优化框架（optimisation framework）扩展至n=8阶，搜索更高效的组合方案并构造首批8阶通用格式；在Heisenberg XZ链动力学模拟中与Morales等人的方案比较，并借助新开发的Qiskit插件qiskit-omelyan进行含噪电路模拟以评估硬件适用性。

Result: 所得公式在理论效率上优于Morales等人的最先进方案，并明确推荐两种方案；在Heisenberg XZ链模拟中，其中一个推荐方案始终优于Morales等人方案（最优者并非固定同一方案）。对分解为多个非对易算符的哈密顿量，Trotter分解的均匀性（uniformity）成为方案性能的主要指标。

Conclusion: 在现有噪声水平下Leapfrog方案仍为最佳选择，但随着噪声率下降趋近容错（fault tolerance），所推荐方案将具备竞争力；8阶乘积公式的优化构造为低门深度量子模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Simulating quantum dynamics in many-body systems is one of the most promising applications of quantum computers. Product formulas are a natural choice for this task, since they map directly onto quantum gates, and increasing their order $n$ could lead to reduced gate depths, provided the parameters are chosen efficiently. So far, schemes beyond $6^{\textrm{th}}$ order have only been built as compositions of second-order schemes, while more general product formulas are known only up to $6^{\textrm{th}}$ order. In this work, we extend our optimisation framework to order $n = 8$, and find both more efficient compositions and the first $8^{\textrm{th}}$ order general schemes. Our formulas improve on the theoretical efficiency of the state-of-the-art schemes by Morales et al and we recommend two schemes explicitly. In simulations of the Heisenberg XZ chain one of our recommended schemes always outperforms those by Morales et al, though it is not always the same scheme that performs best. More generally, we observe that for Hamiltonians split into many non-commuting operators the uniformity of a Trotter decomposition becomes the main indicator of a scheme's performance. To assess the schemes' use on quantum hardware, we simulate noisy circuits with our new Qiskit plugin qiskit-omelyan. At current noise levels, the Leapfrog scheme remains the best choice, but as noise rates decrease toward fault tolerance, our recommended schemes become competitive.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Dijet production in $e^+e^-$ collisions at NNLO+PS accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26718)
*Florian König,Marius Wiesemann,Giulia Zanderighi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出了首个用于轻子对撞（lepton collisions）过程、具备次次领头阶（next-to-next-to-leading order, NNLO）QCD 精度的完整蒙特卡罗事件产生器（Monte Carlo event generator），并将其应用于双喷注（dijet）产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前缺少针对轻子对撞过程、达到 NNLO QCD 精度的完整蒙特卡罗事件产生器，该文旨在填补这一空白，并为 LEP 及未来正负电子对撞机提供高精度模拟工具。

Method: 基于 MiNNLO 形式体系（MiNNLO formalism）的新表述，从三喷注分辨变量（three-jet resolution variable）$y_3$ 的重求和（resummation）出发，给出相应的 MiNNLO 主公式（master formula），并在 Powheg 框架内实现。

Result: 实现了首个轻子对撞 NNLO 精度的完整蒙特卡罗事件产生器，并在双喷注产生中给出与 LEP 及未来正负电子对撞机相关的现象学（phenomenological）结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将 MiNNLO 方法拓展至轻子对撞，为双喷注过程提供 NNLO 精度的蒙特卡罗模拟能力，可服务于 LEP 数据分析与未来正负电子对撞机的现象学研究。

Abstract: We present the first complete Monte Carlo event generator with next-to-next-to-leading order QCD accuracy for lepton collisions. The approach is based on a new formulation of the MiNNLO formalism and is applied to dijet production. Starting from the resummation of the three-jet resolution variable $y_3$, we present the corresponding MiNNLO master formula and implement it within the Powheg framework. We discuss phenomenological results relevant for LEP and future electron-positron colliders.

</details>


### [166] [Study of Radiative Muon Capture Experimental Data and its Implications for Muon-Conversion Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26817)
*Yury Kolomensky,Michael MacKenzie,Ryan Plestid,Matthew Stortini,Ajla Trumic*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作针对μ子无中微子转换搜寻中辐射μ子俘获（RMC）高能光子尾部建模不足的问题，提出以不同核子敲出末态分别建模的新框架，并利用TRIUMF光子数据与SINDRUM II正电子数据检验，结果优于传统闭合近似（closure approximation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RMC是μ子—电子转换搜寻，尤其是轻味与轻数破坏的μ子—正电子转换（muon-to-positron conversion）实验的重要本底，其高能尾部在理论上研究不足，导致对即将运行的μ子转换实验本底预测存在较大不确定性。

Method: 重新分析已发表的RMC数据，构建按不同核子敲出末态（nucleon-knockout final states）区分的模型，每个末态配有相应的多体相空间（multibody phase space）与运动学端点；将该框架应用于TRIUMF的光子数据和SINDRUM II的正电子数据，并与先前使用的闭合近似对比。

Result: 分别建模不同核子敲出末态能更好地描述端点附近可获得的RMC数据，相比闭合近似改进了对高能尾部的刻画。

Conclusion: 改进后的RMC高能尾部建模可降低本底预测不确定性，论文并讨论了其对COMET与Mu2e实验的影响。

Abstract: Radiative muon capture (RMC) is an important background in searches for the neutrinoless conversion of a muon into an electron in the field of a nucleus, and in particular in searches for the lepton flavor and lepton number violating process of muon-to-positron conversion. The high-energy tail of the RMC photon spectrum remains theoretically understudied, leading to large uncertainties in background predictions for upcoming muon-conversion experiments. In this work, we revisit previously published RMC data to investigate improved modeling of the high-energy tail. We develop a model characterized by distinct nucleon-knockout final states, each with its associated multibody phase space and kinematic endpoint. This framework is applied to photon data from TRIUMF and positron data from SINDRUM II, and the results are compared to the previously used closure approximation. We find that separately modeling the distinct nucleon-knockout final states provides an improved description of the available RMC data near the endpoint, and we discuss the implications for the COMET and Mu2e experiments.

</details>


### [167] [Weight-space programming and mechanistic interpretability for model editing in particle physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27079)
*Raphaël Bonnet-Guerrini,Johann Ioannou-Nikolaides,Troels Petersen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究证明，基于稀疏自编码器（sparse autoencoder）引导的权重空间编程（weight-space programming）可直接编辑粒子物理基础模型（foundation model）的权重，从而移除其不期望的行为（违反红外安全性），且几乎不改变模型其余响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在喷注（jet）上训练的基础模型存在违背红外安全性（infrared safety）的缺陷：加入单个软中性粒子即导致喷注分类大幅变化，需要一种能精准定位并修复该缺陷、同时尽量少改参数的方法。

Method: 先用机制分析（mechanistic analysis）定位负责的注意力头（attention heads），并将缺陷归因于预训练数据重构阈值不同；随后提出两种针对性修复：移除由稀疏自编码器识别的三个潜在方向（latent directions），以及让语言模型智能体（language-model agents）在无梯度、仅 50 次查询的条件下直接编辑权重。

Result: 两种编辑各自几乎完全消除了该缺陷，且模型其余响应基本不变；相较微调（fine-tuning），二者改动的参数量远少。编辑效果可迁移到所有信号类别、预训练生成器（generator）及新训练的回归头（regression head）。

Conclusion: 结论：权重空间编辑与稀疏自编码器结合，可作为修复科学基础模型特定缺陷的高效、低参数干预手段，并具备良好的泛化性。

Abstract: We demonstrate that weight-space programming and edits guided by sparse autoencoders can remove unwanted aspects of a particle-physics foundation model through direct edits to its weights. In a foundation model trained on jets, we observe that adding a single soft neutral particle causes large changes in jet classification, violating infrared safety. We locate the responsible attention heads with mechanistic analysis and trace the flaw's origin to pretraining on datasets with different reconstruction thresholds. We then propose two targeted approaches to repair the flaw: by removing three latent directions identified with sparse autoencoders, and by letting language-model agents edit the weights directly, without gradients and with only 50 queries to the model. Both their edits individually remove almost the entire flaw and leave the rest of the model's response nearly unchanged. Compared to fine-tuning, both methods change far fewer parameters. Although built only on $W$ and QCD jets, the edits carry over to all signal classes, the pretrained generator, and a newly trained regression head.

</details>


### [168] [Mass Spectra and Radiative Decay Widths of \(D_{s0}^{*}(2317)^+\), \(D_{s1}(2460)^+\) and \(D'_{s1}(2536)^+\)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27363)
*Shirui Zhao,Yuxuan Du,Manying Pan,Hongxia Huang,Jialun Ping*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于手征夸克模型（chiral quark model）结合高斯展开法（Gaussian Expansion Method），计算了 \(D_{s0}^{*}(2317)^+\)、\(D_{s1}(2460)^+\) 与 \(D_{s1}'(2536)^+\) 的质量谱与辐射衰变宽度，并据与最新实验的对比推断 \(D_{s1}(2460)^+\) 为 \(1^1P_1\)-\(1^3P_1\) 混合态且可能同时含 \(c\bar{s}\) 与四夸克（tetraquark）成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验报道了 \(D_{s1}(2460)^{+}\to D_s^{*+}\gamma\) 的辐射衰变及分支比（branching-fraction ratio）测量结果，为检验这些粲奇异态的内部结构提供了新契机，作者据此开展质量谱与辐射衰变宽度的理论计算。

Method: 采用手征夸克模型（chiral quark model）与高斯展开法（Gaussian Expansion Method）计算三个态的质量谱及辐射衰变宽度，并将所得分支比与宽度同最新实验测量进行比较分析。

Result: 质量谱与实验数据符合较好；得到 \(\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+}\to D_s^{*+}\gamma]/\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+}\to D_s^{+}\gamma]=0.57\)、\(\Gamma(D_{s0}^{*}(2317)^{+}\to D_s^{*+}\gamma)=3.80\,\mathrm{keV}\)，以及将 \(D'_{s1}(2536)^+\) 视为介子态（meson state）时的 \(\Gamma(D'_{s1}(2536)^{+}\to D_s^{*+}\gamma)=9.14\,\mathrm{keV}\) 与 \(\Gamma(D'_{s1}(2536)^{+}\to D_s^{+}\gamma)=8.59\,\mathrm{keV}\)。

Conclusion: \(D_{s1}(2460)^{+}\) 可视为 \(1^1P_1\) 与 \(1^3P_1\) 的混合态（mixed state），且可能同时包含 \(c\bar{s}\) 成分与四夸克（tetraquark）成分；确定这些态的内部结构仍需更精确的实验数据。

Abstract: Inspired by recent experimental reports on the radiative decay \(D_{s1}(2460)^{+}\to D_s^{*+}γ\) and the branching-fraction ratio \(\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+}\to D_s^{*+}γ]/\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+}\to D_s^{+}γ]\), we investigate the mass spectra and radiative decay widths of \(D_{s0}^{*}(2317)^+\), \(D_{s1}(2460)^+\) and \(D_{s1}'(2536)^+\). Within the chiral quark model combined with the Gaussian Expansion Method, the mass spectra obtained agree reasonably well with the experimental data.
  Besides, the branching-fraction ratio obtained in this work is \(\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+}\to D_s^{*+}γ]/\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+}\to D_s^{+}γ] = 0.57\) and \(Γ(D_{s0}^{*}(2317)^{+}\to D_s^{*+}γ) = 3.80\,\mathrm{keV}\).
  By analyzing our theoretical results against the latest experimental measurements, we conclude that the \(D_{s1}(2460)^{+}\) can be treated as a mixed state of \(1^1P_1\) and \(1^3P_1\), and it may contain both the \(c\bar{s}\) component and the tetraquark component.
  In addition, we also obtain \(Γ(D'_{s1}(2536)^{+}\to D_s^{*+}γ) = 9.14\,\mathrm{keV}\) and \(Γ(D'_{s1}(2536)^{+}\to D_s^{+}γ) = 8.59\,\mathrm{keV}\) by treating \(D'_{s1}(2536)^+\) as a meson state.
  More precise experimental data are required to pin down the internal structure of these states.

</details>


### [169] [Probing grand unification in the dark dimension: proton decay and collider signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27602)
*Waqas Ahmed,George K. Leontaris*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在暗维度（dark dimension）启发的区间模型中，将拉长的（stretched）大统一态、质子衰变与电弱 Kaluza–Klein（KK）共振联系起来，给出一组可量化的有效模型基准数值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究引力长度与规范区间长度分离的几何设定下，大统一态质量、质子衰变寿命与电弱 KK 共振之间是否存在统一的几何联系，并检验其是否满足质子寿命限制与高能标匹配条件。

Method: 方法包括：构建归一化的费米子与希格斯剖面（profiles）、对角化中性矢量部分并计算其费米子宽度；采用双算符、SU(5) 启发的有效匹配方案评估质子分寿命；用单圈四维 MSSM 与分裂超对称（split supersymmetry）参考跑动确定电弱耦合交叉能标及残余强耦合失配。

Result: 选定共同阈值基准给出重态质量 1.137×10^16 GeV、参考交叉能标 1.139×10^16 GeV 及条件性质子分寿命 8.72×10^35 年；微米尺度引力长度下，受抑制的重子破坏匹配振幅可满足质子寿命限制，但高能标质量匹配条件选择扫描几何的另一区域；5 TeV 中性矢量基准在 13.6 TeV 对撞能量下、4.5–5.5 TeV 范围内的包容性（inclusive）领头阶双电子截面为 0.183 fb（探测器选择前）。

Conclusion: 分析给出定量有效模型基准，并指出仍需解决的高能标匹配问题与可行的实验检验途径。

Abstract: We study the geometric connection between a stretched grand-unified state, proton decay, and electroweak Kaluza--Klein (KK) resonances in a dark-dimension-inspired interval model. The gravitational length sets the five-dimensional Planck scale, while a shorter gauge interval controls the KK spectrum and the stretched-state mass. We construct normalized fermion and Higgs profiles, diagonalize the neutral-vector sector, and determine its fermionic widths. Proton partial lifetimes are evaluated using a two-operator, SU(5)-inspired effective matching prescription. One-loop four-dimensional MSSM and split-supersymmetric reference running determines electroweak coupling-crossing scales and the residual strong-coupling mismatch. A selected common-threshold benchmark gives a heavy-state mass of $1.137\times10^{16}$ GeV, a reference crossing of $1.139\times10^{16}$ GeV, and a conditional proton partial lifetime of $8.72\times10^{35}$ years. At a micron-scale gravitational length, suppressed baryon-violating matching amplitudes can satisfy proton limits, although the high-scale mass-matching condition selects a distinct region of the scanned geometry. Independently, a $5$ TeV neutral-vector benchmark yields an inclusive leading-order dielectron cross section of $0.183$ fb over $4.5$--$5.5$ TeV at a collider energy of $13.6$ TeV, before detector selection. The analysis provides quantitative effective-model benchmarks and identifies the remaining high-scale matching and experimental tests.

</details>


### [170] [Balancing heavy flavour: Quantum number compensation in the hadronisation of charm and beauty particles in proton-proton collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27786)
*Iñaki Pardo Zambrana,Paul Veen,K. Keri Vos,Panos Christakoglou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 重夸克强子化（hadronisation）仍是量子色动力学（QCD）中理解最不充分的环节之一。本文基于PYTHIA-8模拟的质子-质子对撞事件，考察粲夸克与底夸克量子数在末态中的平衡方式，比较Monash调谐、含弦结（string junction, SJ）的色重联模型与密堆积（close-packing, CP）三种强子化配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重夸克对（heavy quark pair）的产生了微扰计算，但其后强子形成属于非微扰过程，描述高度依赖模型，并受色重联（colour reconnection）与弦碎裂（string fragmentation）机制影响。此外，味、重子数与电荷的守恒对重味强子的形成与末态平衡构成约束，需系统刻画量子数补偿过程。

Method: 利用PYTHIA-8事件产生器模拟质子-质子对撞，从微扰产生的c cbar与b bbar对出发，分析D^0、B^+、Λ_c^+、Λ_b^0等重味介子与重子的形成及其在别处对应的补偿粒子。研究聚焦已鉴别重味强子与负责平衡其守恒量子数的强子系统之间的关联，并对比Monash、SJ与CP三种强子化配置。

Result: SJ与CP调谐在粲与底量子数平衡产额中给出的重子贡献显著高于Monash调谐，且该增强在高多重数事件（high-multiplicity events）中更为明显。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一套刻画重味产生中量子数补偿（quantum-number compensation）的框架，并提供了通过已鉴别粲、底强子关联检验强子化动力学的新途径，可在当前与未来对撞机实验中加以探索。

Abstract: The hadronisation of heavy quarks remains one of the least understood processes of Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD). While the production of heavy quark pairs can be calculated perturbatively in QCD, the subsequent formation of hadrons involves non-perturbative processes. The description of these processes is predominantly model dependent and sensitive to the underlying mechanisms of colour reconnection and string fragmentation. In particular, the conservation of flavour, baryon number and charge imposes constraints on how heavy-flavour hadrons are formed and balanced within the final state.
  In this work, we study how the quantum numbers associated with charm and beauty production are balanced in proton-proton collisions, using events simulated with the PYTHIA-8 event generator. Starting from perturbatively produced ($c\overline{c}$) and ($b\overline{b}$) pairs, we examine how the formation of heavy-flavour mesons and baryons, including $D^0$, $B^+$, $Λ_c^+$, and $Λ_b^0$, is accompanied by compensating particles elsewhere. The analysis focuses on correlations between the identified heavy hadron and the hadronic system responsible for balancing its conserved quantum numbers. We compare three hadronisation configurations that are implemented in PYTHIA: the Monash tune, colour-reconnection models incorporating string junctions (SJ), and close-packing (CP) approaches. We find that the SJ and CP tunes show a substantially enhanced contribution of baryons to the charm and beauty balancing yields compared with the Monash tune; this enhancement becomes more pronounced in high-multiplicity events.
  This study introduces a framework for characterising quantum-number compensation in heavy-flavour production and provides a new way to test hadronisation dynamics using identified charm- and beauty-hadron correlations, which can be explored at current and future collider experiments.

</details>


### [171] [Is Neutrino Disappearance Sensitive to T-Violation for Asymmetric Matter Profiles ?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27985)
*Gabriela Barenboim,Stephen J. Parke,Alberto Sánchez Vargas*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出文献中“中微子消失道中T破坏为零”的常见说法并不普遍成立：该结论仅对对称物质分布或Jarlskog不变量为零（δ=0或π）时成立，在非对称物质中ν_μ与ν_τ存活概率含有sinδ依赖项，构成真实的T不对称。文章厘清了正确与错误的T检验以及CP、CPT检验的区别，并用双层常密度玩具模型探讨探测可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献普遍断言中微子消失道（disappearance channel）中T破坏（T violation）为零，但作者认为该断言源于一个不恰当的T检验——将概率与自身相减的数学恒等式，而非物理结果。因此有必要澄清正确与错误T检验的差异，并给出一般任意物质分布下的正确结论。

Method: 在一般任意物质分布（arbitrary matter profile）下，区分正确的与错误的T检验以及正确的与错误的CP、CPT检验；并构造由两层不同常密度层组成的玩具模型（toy model）进行分析。

Result: ν_μ与ν_τ（而非ν_e）的存活概率（survival probability）含有sinδ依赖项，在非对称物质中非零，构成真实的T不对称；该结论仅在对称物质分布或Jarlskog不变量（Jarlskog invariant）为零时不成立。玩具模型中，当一层接近太阳共振（solar resonance）、另一层接近大气共振（atmospheric resonance）时T破坏最大。文章还评估了DUNE与Hyper-Kamiokande实验中物质不对称是否足以在ν_μ存活概率中探测sinδ依赖。

Conclusion: “消失道中T破坏为零”的说法仅适用于对称物质分布或Jarlskog不变量为零的情形；在非对称物质中消失道存在真实的T不对称，可通过ν_μ存活概率中的sinδ依赖项探测，文中并讨论了未来可探测T/CP破坏的实验方案。

Abstract: It is commonly stated in the literature that T violation is zero in neutrino disappearance channels. We show this claim is not general: it holds only for symmetric matter profiles or when the T/CP-violating phase ($δ$) is 0 or $π$, that is, when the Jarlskog invariant is zero. The $ν_μ$ and $ν_τ$, but not the $ν_e$, survival probabilities carry a $\sinδ$-dependent term that is non-zero in asymmetric matter and constitutes a genuine T asymmetry. The confusion arises from an improper T test that subtracts a probability from itself: a mathematical identity, not a physics result. In this paper, we clarify the distinctions between a proper and an improper T test as well as the proper and improper CP and CPT tests, for a general, arbitrary matter profile.
  For a toy model consisting of two different layers of constant density, we find that the maximum T-violation is obtained when one layer is close to the solar resonance, and the other is close to the atmospheric resonance. We address whether the matter asymmetry for DUNE and Hyper-Kamiokande experiments is sufficiently large to allow the $\sinδ$ dependence to be probed in $ν_μ$ survival probability. We also discuss future experimental scenarios that could probe T/CP-violation in $ν_μ$ survival probability.

</details>


### [172] [Agent-guided construction of Feynman integral bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26688)
*Pau Petit Rosas,William J. Torres Bobadilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用“寻找合适基”这一任务的高度可验证性，为Claude Opus 5.5设计两项技能，使其能自主连续工作数日并产出新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找费曼积分（Feynman integrals）的基（basis），使其微分方程（differential equations）适用于唯象学（phenomenology），是一个可高效验证的任务；据此可将其转化为可由AI智能体自主完成的研究流程。

Method: 设计两项“技能（skills）”，驱动Claude Opus 5.5自主运行数日；在两类对象上检验：含两个质量、18个椭圆扇区（elliptic sectors）的两圈五点族（two-loop five-point family），以及含K3扇区的辐射喷注函数（radiative jet function）两圈超族（superfamily）。

Result: 智能体找到了使微分方程在维数正规化参数ε中无极点（free of poles）、且至多为ε二次的基；并将喷注函数对应的结果推进到ε-因子化形式（ε-factorized form）。

Conclusion: 研究表明，凭借任务的可验证性，AI智能体可在高复杂度费曼积分基的构造中自主取得新颖且可检验的结果，涵盖椭圆与K3扇区等困难情形。

Abstract: Finding bases of Feynman integrals whose differential equations are suitable for phenomenology is a remarkably verifiable task. We exploit this to design two \emph{skills} with which Claude Opus~5.5 produces novel results autonomously, working for several days. For a two-loop five-point family (with two masses and 18 elliptic sectors) and a two-loop superfamily for the radiative jet function with a K3 sector, the agent finds bases whose differential equations are free of poles in the dimensional regulator $\varepsilon$ and at most quadratic in it, and takes the jet-function one to $\varepsilon$-factorized form.

</details>


### [173] [Moment bounds and UV reconstruction from curved positivity cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26692)
*Fu-Ming Chang,Shi-Lin Wan,Shuang-Yong Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于矩的方法（moment-based framework），用于解析地刻画由S-矩阵基本原理给出的正性锥（positivity cones），尤其是SMEFT中与反常四规范耦合相关的弯曲锥边界，并可由锥边界反推紫外（UV）模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S-矩阵（S-matrix）基本原理对EFT（有效场论）及SMEFT的Wilson系数施加正性界（positivity bounds），当不可约两粒子低能态存在简并时，这些界构成边界弯曲的凸锥；此前仅用数值近似评估此类弯曲锥，缺乏解析刻画手段。

Method: 发展矩（moment）框架，对正性锥进行解析刻画，重点处理SMEFT中反常四规范耦合（anomalous quartic gauge couplings）对应的锥，并利用该框架从锥的弯曲边界反推紫外（UV）模型。

Result: 实现了对弯曲正性锥的解析求解（此前多用数值近似），并表明锥边界信息可用于反向构造UV模型。

Conclusion: 矩框架为正性锥提供了解析刻画途径，适用于SMEFT反常四规范耦合情形，并建立了正性锥边界与UV模型之间的可逆联系。

Abstract: The fundamental principles of the S-matrix impose positivity bounds on the Wilson coefficients of EFTs, including the SMEFT. These bounds form convex cones that often have curved boundaries when there are degeneracies among the irreducible two-particle low-energy states. Numerical approximations have previously been used to evaluate these curved cones. We develop a moment-based framework to analytically carve out these cones, particularly those associated with anomalous quartic gauge couplings in the SMEFT. The framework also allows us to reverse engineer UV models from the curved boundaries of the positivity cones.

</details>


### [174] [Soft-photon resummation for low-energy $e^+e^-$ colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27883)
*Thomas Dave,Lois Flower,Jérémy Paltrinieri,William J. Torres Bobadilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在多光子软极限（multi-photon soft limit）下给出 $e^+e^-\to\mu^+\mu^-(\gamma)$ 与 $\pi^+\pi^-(\gamma)$ 的重求和（resummation）预言，并匹配至精确 NLO。计算基于 Yennie-Frautschi-Suura 框架、在振幅层面（amplitude level）进行，已实现为快速 Fortran 蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）积分器，并与四个生成器对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能 $e^+e^-$ 实验（能量扫描与辐射返回实验）中，蒙特卡罗生成器（Monte Carlo generators）的精确性需要独立验证，尤其是多光子软辐射的重求和效应与质量有限项的 NLO 精度。

Method: 在 Yennie-Frautschi-Suura 框架内于振幅层面进行重求和，相干地（coherently）纳入初态与末态贡献，达到含有限电子、μ 子与 π 介子质量的 NLO 精度，并写入快速的 Fortran 蒙特卡罗积分器。

Result: 在模拟 CMD 能量扫描以及 KLOE、BES III、B 工厂辐射返回情形的场景中，与含部分子簇射（parton shower）高阶修正的 BabaYaga 在截面（cross-sections）上达到千分（per-mille）量级一致，与 KKMC 的软光子重求和结果差异可达百分之二。

Conclusion: 该工作为低能实验所用蒙特卡罗生成器的验证迈出重要一步，并将在 Phokhara 的新版本中发布。

Abstract: We present resummed predictions for $e^+e^- \to μ^+μ^-(γ)$ and $π^+π^-(γ)$ in the multi-photon soft limit, matched to exact NLO. The resummation is performed within the Yennie-Frautschi-Suura framework at the amplitude level. The initial and final-state contributions are included coherently, and NLO accuracy with finite electron, muon and pion masses is achieved. We present our calculation, its implementation in a fast Fortran Monte Carlo integrator, and compare our predictions with the generators BabaYaga, KKMC, Phokhara and McMule, in scenarios inspired by the energy-scan (CMD) and radiative-return (KLOE, BES III, B factories) experiments. We find agreement with BabaYaga, which includes higher-order corrections through a parton shower, at the per-mille level in the cross-sections, and differences of up to two percent with the soft-photon resummation of KKMC. This work represents an important step towards the validation of Monte Carlo generators for low-energy experiments, and will be released in the upcoming new version of Phokhara.

</details>


### [175] [Ohmic warm inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27986)
*Anish Ghoshal,Alessandro Strumia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文指出 Warm Little Inflation 存在反常、缺乏合适的暴胀子势且无法维持热浴，问题源于准静态近似高估了耗散功率；为此提出基于额外维中单个 U(1) 规范群荷电费米子的新摩擦机制与无反常模型，其四维格点版本为 N 个 U(1) 群构成的环，大 N 极限下可得到合适的单值性暴胀势并与观测相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原 Warm Little Inflation 方案被证明是反常的（anomalous），缺乏合适的暴胀子势（inflaton potential），且无法维持热浴（thermal bath），其根源是准静态近似（quasi-static approximation）高估了耗散功率（dissipated power）。因此需要构建无反常且能自洽维持温暖的替代模型。

Method: 提出一种新的摩擦机制（friction mechanism）与无反常模型（anomaly-free models），基于一个额外维（one extra dimension）中在 U(1) 规范群下荷电的单个费米子；其四维格点版本为 N 个 U(1) 群组成的环、每格点一个费米子，N=2 时退化为无反常版的 warm little inflaton 模型，暴胀子为 Wilson 线（Wilson line），在 N≥5 时受保护。

Result: 大 N 情形允许存在合适的单值性暴胀势（monodromy inflaton potential）；在连续极限 N→∞ 下，暴胀子速度对应五维电场（five-dimensional electric field），摩擦转化为欧姆耗散（Ohmic dissipation），该机制似乎能够在与观测相容的前提下维持温暖暴胀（warm inflation）。

Conclusion: 格点化的 U(1) 环构造为 Warm Little Inflation 提供了无反常的推广，并通过大 N 与连续极限把摩擦机制与欧姆耗散联系起来，初步显示其可与观测相容；但摘要以"seems able to"表述，结论仍具探索性与待验证性质。

Abstract: We show that Warm Little Inflation is anomalous, lacks a suitable inflaton potential, and cannot sustain the thermal bath, as the quasi-static approximation over-estimated the dissipated power. To address these issues, we propose a new friction mechanism and new anomaly-free models based on one fermion charged under a U(1) gauge group in one extra dimension. The four-dimensional lattice version is a ring of $N$ U(1) groups with one fermion at each site, reducing to an anomaly-free version of the warm little inflaton model for $N=2$. The inflaton is a Wilson line, protected for $N\ge5$. Large $N$ allows a suitable monodromy inflaton potential. In the continuum limit, $N\to\infty$, the inflaton velocity becomes a five-dimensional electric field and friction becomes Ohmic dissipation, which seems able to sustain warm inflation compatibly with observations.

</details>

### [176] [Disrupted stars and captured compact objects: Combining observations from multiple messengers to infer the population of massive black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27742)
*Shashwat Singh,Luca Broggi,Christopher P. L. Berry,Marta Volonteri*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文构建将LISA探测的极端质量比旋近（EMRIs）与ZTF等电磁巡天探测的潮汐瓦解事件（TDEs）联合起来的分层贝叶斯推断框架，用于测量大质量黑洞（MBHs）族群性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBHs的形成与演化仍不清楚，而EMRIs与TDEs同源于核星团恒星动力学，却分别由引力波与电磁手段观测；联合探测事件稀缺，因此需要统计层面（而非单个同时探测）的联合分析方法。

Method: 基于EMRIs与TDEs的二维Fokker-Planck模拟，建模MBH—宿主标度关系、核化比例与MBH占据比例对两族群的影响，并在考虑各自选择偏差（selection bias）的前提下进行联合分层推断（hierarchical inference）。

Result: 可探测TDEs与EMRIs之比很高（ZTF探测数为LISA的2.7–84.3倍）；LSST可将特征MBH质量的90%可信宽度约束到0.31 dex、红移演化幂指数精度0.11；EMRIs约占联合星表3.4%，但信息量超过整个ZTF样本，且是唯一可探测MBH自旋与轨道偏心率的手段。

Conclusion: TDEs可在LISA发射前约束低质量MBH丰度，EMRIs则测量电磁巡天无法获取的性质；二者互补，共同揭示MBH的增长与演化。

Abstract: Massive black holes (MBHs) are found in galactic centres, but their formation and evolution remain elusive. Tidal disruption events (TDEs) and extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) both arise from stellar dynamics in the nuclear star clusters around MBHs, and are observed with electromagnetic surveys and gravitational waves, respectively. To overcome the scarcity of coincident detections, we formulate how to combine gravitational-wave detections from LISA-EMRIs with observations from ZTF-TDEs to measure properties of the MBH population. Building on two-dimensional Fokker-Planck simulations of EMRIs and TDEs, we model how their populations are affected by the MBH-host scaling relation, the nucleation fraction and the MBH occupation fraction, and analyse the resulting catalogues using joint hierarchical inference accounting for their individual selection biases. With current ZTF-TDE detections, we can already identify combinations of MBH formation favoured by and consistent with the observed TDE population, and derive corresponding predictions for the EMRI population detectable by LISA. Across a range of astrophysical assumptions, the ratio of detectable TDEs to EMRIs is high: ZTF detecting 2.7-84.3 times as many TDEs as LISA detects EMRIs. TDEs, through their numbers, set the shared mass and redshift distributions: LSST constrains the characteristic MBH mass to a 90% credible width of 0.31 dex and the power-law index of the redshift evolution to a precision of 0.11. EMRIs make up ~3.4% of the joint catalogue, yet carry more information than the whole ZTF sample and are the only probe of MBH spin and orbital eccentricity. TDEs will constrain the abundance of low-mass MBHs before LISA flies, while EMRIs will measure properties that electromagnetic surveys cannot. EMRI and TDE observations offer complementary insights into MBH growth and evolution that neither can provide alone.

</details>


### [177] [What determines the properties of Population III binaries?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27820)
*Neha Singh,Tomasz Bulik*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究用StarTrack对约2×10^6个初始双星、1152组参数配置进行种群合成，构建迄今配置数最多的Population III孤立双星模型网格，并比较全部双黑洞（BBH）与在宇宙年龄内并合的BBH的性质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Population III双星可作为探测第一代恒星族群的引力波探针，但初始双星参数分布与演化处方不确定，导致对遗迹质量、诞生轨道与形成自旋的预测困难；需联合变化多个模型演化参数（MEPs）以识别哪些参数塑造各性质、以及这些效应是否在并合双星中延续。

Method: 采用StarTrack，对每个模型演化2×10^6个初始双星，覆盖8个MEP的1152种配置；比较全部BBH与在采用宇宙年龄内并合系统的性质及相关性，并用基于方差（variance-based）的敏感性分析量化初始双星参数分布与演化处方对BBH性质预测的影响。

Result: 全部BBH（并合BBH）的模型中位数范围为：总质量34.3–171.0 M⊙（23.1–94.8 M⊙），形成时有效对齐自旋χ_eff为0.039–0.106（0.018–0.129），诞生偏心率0.075–0.639（0.039–0.983）。并合筛选同时改变了BBH性质与MEP取值组合及处方之间的相关性。

Conclusion: 并合选择效应会重塑BBH性质分布及其与模型参数的相关结构，因此仅依据并合样本推断Population III双星族群可能带有选择性偏差；基于方差的敏感性分析可辨识主导各BBH性质的参数与处方。

Abstract: Population~III binaries may provide a gravitational-wave probe of the first stellar populations. Uncertain initial binary parameter distributions and evolutionary prescriptions complicate predictions of their remnant masses, birth orbits and formation spins. Varying these model evolutionary parameters (MEPs) jointly is needed to identify which MEPs shape each property and whether their effects persist among binaries that merge. We use \textsc{StarTrack} to evolve $2\times10^6$ initial binaries per model across 1152 configurations spanning eight MEPs. To our knowledge, this is the largest grid of Population~III isolated-binary population-synthesis models to date by number of distinct parameter configurations. We compare the properties and correlations of all binary black holes (BBHs) with those of systems that merge within the adopted age of the Universe. The all-BBH (merging-BBH) model medians span $34.3$--$171.0 M_{\odot}$ ($23.1$--$94.8 M_{\odot}$) in total mass, $0.039$--$0.106$ ($0.018$--$0.129$) in effective aligned spin $χ_{\mathrm{eff}}$ at formation, and $0.075$--$0.639$ ($0.039$--$0.983$) in birth eccentricity. Merger selection also changes correlations between BBH properties and the combinations of MEP values and prescriptions associated with them. We use variance-based sensitivity analysis to quantify how the initial binary parameter distributions and evolutionary prescriptions affect the predicted BBH properties.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Shear thinning confines the wall forcing: laminar chaotic mixing of Carreau fluids in a rotating arc-wall mixer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27150)
*Teodor Burghelea*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 通过二维 Carreau 流体模拟（幂律指数 N 由 1 降至 0.4），研究剪切变稀（shear thinning）对开口通道内层流混沌混合（chaotic mixing）的影响；结果表明剪切变稀主要通过移动壁面弧片周围的低黏度薄层缩短壁面驱动的几何穿透长度（penetration length），从而削弱拉伸并降低混合效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪切变稀非牛顿流体在壁面驱动混合器（wall-driven mixer）中的混合行为尚缺乏定量认识，需要厘清黏度变化究竟通过整体流动还是局部机制影响混沌混合。

Method: 采用二维 Carreau 流体模拟，幂律指数 N 取 1 至 0.4；混合器为开口通道，由三段往复旋转的弧形壁面搅拌，上方一段与下方两段反相（anti-phase）驱动；并结合层状模型（lamellar model）分析。

Result: 出口混合效率（mixing efficiency）随 N 由 1 降至 0.4 单调下降 14%，主要发生在 N=0.85–0.6 区间；整体流动与混沌鞍（chaotic saddle）几乎不受影响。壁面强迫的穿透长度按时间平均壁面黏度的 0.3 次幂缩短（四倍强迫范围内误差 4% 以内），且可预测混合效率（r²=0.97）。最优 Strouhal 数在各类流体中均接近 0.7，但剪切变稀缩小了高效周期范围；层状模型显示剪切变稀的相对损失随 Peclet 数增大。

Conclusion: 单位能量混合效率由 N=1 到 0.4 提升 2.6 倍；在单位体积等能量且转速更快条件下，N=0.4 流体与牛顿流体混合效果相当，高能量时更优。壁面驱动混合器中应控制强迫的作用范围（reach of the forcing）而非整体黏度。

Abstract: With two-dimensional simulations of Carreau fluids of power-law index $N$ from 1 to 0.4, I study how shear thinning modifies laminar chaotic mixing in an open channel stirred by three arc-shaped wall segments rotating back and forth periodically, the upper one in anti-phase with the two lower ones. The outlet mixing efficiency falls monotonically, by 14 percent from $N=1$ to 0.4, mostly between $N=0.85$ and 0.6. The bulk flow and its chaotic saddle are nearly unaffected. Shear thinning acts instead through a thin layer of reduced viscosity around the moving arcs: the geometric penetration length of the wall forcing shortens as the 0.3 power of the time-mean wall viscosity, within 4 percent over a fourfold range of forcing. This confinement weakens the stretching, and the penetration length predicts the mixing efficiency ($r^2=0.97$). The optimal Strouhal number stays close to 0.7 for all fluids, but shear thinning narrows the range of efficient periods. A lamellar model indicates that the relative penalty of shear thinning grows with the Peclet number. As the injected power scales with the wall viscosity, the mixing efficiency per unit energy rises 2.6-fold from $N=1$ to 0.4, and at equal energy per unit volume, with a faster rotation, the fluid with $N=0.4$ mixes as well as the Newtonian one, and better at larger energies. In wall-driven mixers, the reach of the forcing, not the bulk viscosity, is the quantity to control.

</details>
