# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [High-Sensitivity Magnetometry with Scalable Mixed SQUID Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26959)
*Anjishnu Bose,Jonathan Marenkovic,Giuseppe C. Tettamanzi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作针对混合超导量子干涉仪阵列（mixed-SQUID array），建立了经全数值模拟验证的解析框架，推导其设计规则，并证明该结构可规避传统 SQIF 的感性屏蔽损耗，通量灵敏度（flux sensitivity）提升约 2–4 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 SQIF 架构在提升信号功率的同时受限于感性屏蔽损耗（inductive screening losses），而新兴的混合 SQUID 阵列虽具潜力，却缺乏严格的理论优化与性能标度分析，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建解析理论框架（analytical framework），并与完整的数值模拟（full numerical simulations）相互校验；系统考察关键量子传感参数随环路电感（loop inductance）、寄生耦合（parasitic coupling）及阵列整体尺寸的变化规律，从而确立基本设计规则。

Result: 在符合实际的器件参数下，混合 SQUID 阵列规避了传统 SQIF 设计固有的严重感性屏蔽损耗，通量灵敏度获得约 2–4 倍的性能提升（$\sim 2-4\times$）；同时给出了各参数对传感性能的标度关系。

Conclusion: 混合 SQUID 阵列可作为传统 SQIF 技术的高性能替代方案；所建立的解析框架与设计规则为该几何结构的理论优化提供了依据。

Abstract: The utilisation of Superconducting Quantum Interference Devices (SQUIDs), a loop of superconducting material hosting two Josephson junctions, has long been at the forefront of quantum sensing. To enhance performance and signal power, modern architectures connect multiple SQUIDs into two-dimensional series-parallel arrays called Superconducting Quantum Interference Filters (SQIFs). A major recent advancement, called a mixed-SQUID array, integrates junction-free ``bare" loops alongside active SQUID loops, offering a promising strategy to engineer high-performance alternatives to traditional SQIF technology. However, a rigorous theoretical optimization and performance scaling analysis for this novel geometry remains lacking. In this work, an analytical framework, validated against full numerical simulations, is developed to establish fundamental design rules for this technology. This work systematically examines how key quantum sensing parameters evolve as a function of loop inductance, parasitic coupling, and overall array dimensions. Crucially, it is demonstrated that these mixed SQUID arrays circumvent the severe inductive screening losses inherent to conventional SQIF designs, achieving a performance boost in flux sensitivity in the range of $2-4\:\times$ under realistic device parameters.

</details>


### [180] [Chemical Programming of Stacking Order and Superconductivity Dimensionality in NbSe2-xTex Polytypic Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27220)
*Xiangqi Liu,Haonan Wang,Ziguang Liu,Peihan Sun,Xiang Xu,Jing Jiang,Zhengyang Li,Ziyi Zhu,Wei Xia,Xia Wang,Leiming Chen,Ke Qu,Yulin Chen,Cheng Chen,Kai Liu,Zhenzhong Yang,Yanfeng Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文以 NbSe2-xTex 为模型体系，利用 Te 替代驱动的连续多型（polytypism）转变，建立了从堆垛序列到超导维度性的直接相图，为堆垛工程（stacking-sequence engineering）设计低维超导器件提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenides）的电子态对晶格对称性高度敏感，但单一材料体系内缺乏连续的结构演化，使结构-超导关系难以厘清。

Method: 在全组分范围内通过 Te 替代实现从 2H 经 1H/1T 异质结构化的 4H、6R 到理想 1T 与畸变 1T' 的序列化多型转变；结合综合表征与自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）能带计算，追踪费米能级附近谱权重与轨道特征的变化，并进行电输运测量。

Result: 超导维度性呈显著相依赖：4H 相（NbSe1.4Te0.6）各向异性增强且面内上临界场异常提升；6R 相（NbSe1.2Te0.8）Tc 受抑（约 2.8 K）并表现类三维行为。SOC 能带计算解析了 4H 与 6R 电子态所受的不同对称性约束。

Conclusion: 堆垛序列通过影响有效层间相干性与局域自旋轨道环境调控超导维度性；该机制区别于常规手段，依托本征的组分驱动多型性，并给出多型性与超导维度性直接关联的相图。

Abstract: Layered transition-metal dichalcogenides exhibit electronic states highly sensitive to lattice symmetry, yet elucidating the structure-superconductivity relationship has been challenging due to the lack of a continuous structural evolution within a single material system. Here, we establish NbSe2-xTex as a model system, where Te substitution drives a sequential polytypic transition across the full composition range-from the low-Te 2H phase, through mixed 1H/1T heterostructured 4H and 6R polytypes at intermediate substitution, to ideal 1T and distorted 1T' structures at high Te content. Combining comprehensive characterizations, we map both the structural evolution and the accompanying changes in spectral weight and orbital character near the Fermi level. Electrical transport measurements reveal a striking phase-dependent superconducting dimensionality: the 4H phase (NbSe1.4Te0.6) exhibits enhanced anisotropy and an anomalous boost in the in-plane upper critical field, while the 6R phase (NbSe1.2Te0.8)shows suppressed Tc (~ 2.8 K) and three-dimensional-like behavior. The combined electronic-structure and superconducting results connect the stacking sequence to effective interlayer coherence and the local spin-orbit environment. SOC band calculations resolve the distinct symmetry constraints on the 4H and 6R electronic states. These mechanisms fundamentally differ from conventional approaches by leveraging intrinsic, composition-driven polytypism to modify interlayer coupling and band structure. Thus, by establishing a definitive phase diagram that directly correlates polytypism with superconducting dimensionality, our work provides a clear roadmap for the rational design of low-dimensional superconducting devices through stacking-sequence engineering.

</details>


### [181] [Normal-state exponential muon-spin relaxation and its implications for time-reversal symmetry breaking in Sr$_2$RuO$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27240)
*Rustem Khasanov,Thomas J. Hicken,Jonas A. Krieger,Maxime Lamotte,Toni Shiroka,Jake S. Bobowski,Vadim Grinenko,Thomas Johnson,Naoki Kikugawa,Hans-Henning Klauss,Shubhankar Paul,Benjamin M. Huddart,Stephen J. Blundell,Hubertus Luetkens,Yoshiteru Maeno*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文通过高质量Sr$_2$RuO$_4$单晶的μSR测量表明，零场μSR中低温弛豫增强的起始温度$T_\Lambda$低于$T_{\rm c}$，且类似弛豫在超过上临界场的横场中依然存在，故该信号不能单独作为超导时间反演对称破缺（TRSB）的唯一判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，Sr$_2$RuO$_4$零场μ子自旋弛豫率（muon-spin relaxation rate）的低温上升被视为超导态自发内场即时间反演对称破缺（time-reversal-symmetry-breaking, TRSB）超导的关键证据。作者意在直接检验这一解读是否成立。

Method: 对四块高质量Sr$_2$RuO$_4$单晶开展零场与横场μ子自旋旋转/弛豫（μSR）测量：确定额外指数弛豫的起始温度$T_\Lambda$并与$T_{\rm c}$比较；在沿$c$轴施加80 mT（超过横场几何下的上临界场、抑制体超导）条件下检验该弛豫是否仍存在；并在上临界场以下测量混合态贡献。

Result: 全部四块样品的$T_\Lambda$均低于体超导转变温度$T_{\rm c}$，即使未施加应变亦如此；在80 mT沿$c$轴的横场中观测到定量相似的指数弛豫，该场超过上临界场并抑制体超导，说明该特征弛豫在正常态下仍持续存在。上临界场以下的测量显示，在该正常态通道之上叠加了额外的超导混合态贡献。

Conclusion: Sr$_2$RuO$_4$的零场μSR弛豫本身不能被视为超导TRSB的唯一特征，需重新评估该体系TRSB的关键实验判据，并强调在其它非常规超导体（unconventional superconductors）中区分超导态与正常态弛豫通道的必要性。

Abstract: The low-temperature increase of the zero-field muon-spin relaxation rate in Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ has long been regarded as evidence for spontaneous internal fields associated with time-reversal-symmetry-breaking (TRSB) superconductivity. Here we directly test this interpretation using muon-spin rotation/relaxation ($μ$SR) measurements on high-quality Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ single crystals. For all four samples, the onset temperature of the additional exponential relaxation in the zero-field$μ$SR data, $T_Λ$, lies below the bulk superconducting transition temperature $T_{\rm c}$, even in the absence of applied strain. More importantly, in transverse-field $μ$SR measurements we find a quantitatively similar exponential relaxation in a field of 80 mT applied along the crystallographic $c$ axis. This field exceeds the upper critical field of the crystals studied in transverse-field geometry and suppresses bulk superconductivity, demonstrating that the characteristic relaxation persists in the normal state. Measurements below the upper critical field reveal an additional superconducting mixed-state contribution superposed on this normal-state channel. Our results show that the zero-field $μ$SR relaxation in Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ cannot, by itself, be regarded as a unique signature of superconducting TRSB. They call for a reassessment of a key experimental signature of TRSB in Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ and highlight the need to distinguish superconducting and normal-state relaxation channels in other unconventional superconductors.

</details>


### [182] [Annular Terahertz Near-Field of a Breathing Charged Vortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27532)
*I. Tolbatov,L. Salasnich*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究太赫兹驱动的希格斯振幅模在带电BCS超导体单量子涡旋周围产生的径向电场。在局域密度近似下，涡旋轴上配对能隙恒为零，使中性希格斯振荡转化为轴上为零的环形交变径向电场，并对氮化铌给出约140 V/m的静电场及约30 V/m的调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯振幅模本质上是电中性的，如何在实验中对其进行电学探测缺乏清晰图像；本文旨在说明涡旋的非均匀配对结构可将该中性振荡转换为可观测的径向电场。

Method: 采用局域密度近似（local-density approximation）处理单量子涡旋（singly quantized vortex），并沿用前期工作中的静电透镜形式（electrostatic-lens formalism），在静电屏蔽长度远短于涡旋芯尺寸的极限下求得电场的闭式解（closed form）。

Result: 所得电场呈环形分布，在距轴线约为相干长度（coherence length）处达到极大，轴上为零；希格斯驱动以比例2η调制该场，η为能隙的相对振荡幅度。对氮化铌（niobium nitride）取η=0.1，涡旋静电场约140 V/m、调制约30 V/m，且与载流子密度和介电常数无关。

Conclusion: 涡旋轴的能隙节点与密度剖面的曲率差异，使中性希格斯振荡可转化为交变径向电场，提供了电学探测希格斯模的途径；文中还讨论了该电场中的热力学因子（thermodynamic factor）向BCS-BEC渡越（BCS-BEC crossover）演化时的变化趋势。

Abstract: We study the radial electric field around a singly quantized vortex in a charged BCS superconductor, when the Higgs amplitude mode is driven by a uniform terahertz (THz) electromagnetic pulse. In a local-density approximation the pairing gap vanishes on the vortex axis at all times, so the particle density on the axis cannot oscillate, whereas the curvature of the density profile around the axis does. Starting from the electrostatic-lens formalism of our previous work, we show that this converts the electrically neutral Higgs oscillation into an alternating radial electric field that vanishes on the axis. When the electrostatic screening length is much shorter than the vortex core, we obtain the field in closed form: it has a ring-shaped profile, with a maximum at a distance of the order of the coherence length from the axis, and the Higgs drive modulates it by a fraction $2η$, where $η$ is the relative oscillation of the gap. For niobium nitride and $η=0.1$ the static field of the vortex is about $140$~V/m and its modulation about $30$~V/m, independently of the carrier density and of the dielectric constant. We also discuss how the thermodynamic factor entering the field changes toward the BCS-BEC crossover.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Critical Ising and Temperley-Lieb-Jones States: Cone Haag Duality and the Trivial Algebra Object](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27892)
*David E Evans*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文在融合范畴对称性的算子代数框架下计算两个临界 Ising 态的代数对象（algebra object）。边界子代数为 Pauli 链的偶数部分，其 DHR 范畴为环面码（toric code）；结果表明二维经典 Ising 模型（经行转移矩阵）与横场 Ising 链的临界态代数对象恰为张量单位（tensor unit），并将结论推广至临界 Temperley–Lieb–Jones 链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于用融合范畴对称性（fusion category symmetry）的算子代数框架刻画临界 Ising 态的对称性结构，考察其代数对象是否平凡，并为更一般的临界链模型提供统一处理。

Method: 方法上，将边界子代数取为 Pauli 链（Pauli chain）的偶数部分，其 DHR 范畴为环面码（toric code）；分别通过二维经典 Ising 模型的行转移矩阵（row transfer matrix）与横场 Ising 链（transverse field Ising chain）的基态构造临界态，并计算其代数对象。第二部分处理 δ=2cos(π/(k+2)) 处的临界 Temperley–Lieb–Jones 链，取真空块（vacuum block）所选基态，在偶数部分的 Drinfeld 中心（Drinfeld centre）中计算代数对象。

Result: 结果显示：临界转移矩阵态与临界链基态的代数对象恰为张量单位（tensor unit）。对每个 k≥2，真空块所选基态为纯态（pure），且在偶数部分的 Drinfeld 中心中代数对象平凡；k=2 时回到临界 Ising 链。

Conclusion: 结论是临界 Ising 态在给定算子代数框架下的代数对象为平凡（即张量单位），该性质在临界 Temperley–Lieb–Jones 链族中普遍成立，并可将 Ising 链视为 k=2 的特例。

Abstract: We compute the algebra object of two critical Ising states in the operator algebraic framework of fusion category symmetry. The boundary subalgebra is the even part of the Pauli chain, and its DHR category is the toric code. We treat two models. The first is the classical Ising model in two dimensions, through its row transfer matrix. The second is the Ising chain in a transverse field. For the critical transfer matrix state and for the ground state of the critical chain, the algebra object is exactly the tensor unit. In a second part we treat the critical Temperley--Lieb--Jones chains at $δ=2\cos(π/(k+2))$. For every $k\ge2$ the ground state selected by the vacuum block is pure and has trivial algebra object in the Drinfeld centre of the even part. At $k=2$ this recovers the critical Ising chain.

</details>


<div id='physics.hist-ph'></div>

### [184] [Unified Yet Non-fundamental: The Origins of Steven Weinberg's Early Conception of Effective Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26890)
*Sébastien Rivat*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文追溯温伯格（Steven Weinberg）20世纪60年代有效场论（Effective Field Theories, EFTs）早期构想的起源，主张其"统一性承诺"（unificatory commitment）决定性塑造了首个EFT原型的诞生，并首次将其设想为以特征尺度展开的协变模型展开（covariant model expansions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在厘清温伯格EFT思想的史前史，解释其为何走向一种不以基本性为前提、而依赖"第一原理"（无论是否基本）来整理粒子现象多样性的理论路径。

Method: 采用科学史与思想史的概念溯源方法，围绕1960年代温伯格的统一性诉求，重构其在1966—1968年间提出体现非基本对称原理（non-fundamental symmetry principle）的新型场论的过程。

Result: 温伯格于1966—1968年间引入该场论，以最完备方式实现非基本对称原理，从而系统描述低能核子—π介子相互作用（low-energy nucleon-pion interaction）过程。

Conclusion: 由此温伯格首次将EFT理解为在某一特征尺度上的协变模型展开，独立于其（更）基本的对应理论，并简要延伸至1970年代末该构想的发展。

Abstract: This article traces the origins of Steven Weinberg's early conception of Effective Field Theories (EFTs) in the 1960s. I argue that Weinberg's path to his first EFT prototype was decisively shaped by his longstanding unificatory commitment. From his early days as a physicist onward, he strove to rely on first principles, whether fundamental or not, to bring order to the diversity of particle phenomena. This explains why he ultimately came in 1966-68 to introduce a new kind of field theory that instantiates a non-fundamental symmetry principle in the most complete manner in order to systematically account for low-energy nucleon-pion interaction processes. In doing so, Weinberg was, for the first time, led to conceive of EFTs as covariant model expansions in some characteristic scale, independently of their (more) fundamental counterpart(s). I conclude by giving a brief sketch of the evolution of his conception up until the late 1970s.

</details>


<div id='physics.atom-ph'></div>

# physics.atom-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Test of gravitational redshift by optical clocks at different latitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27588)
*Ingo Nosske,Chetan Vishwakarma,Tim Lücke,Martin Steinel,Melina Filzinger,Jochen Kronjäger,Alexander Kuhl,Shambo Mukherjee,Erik Benkler,Heiner Denker,Ronald Holzwarth,Thomas Udem,Sören Dörscher,Nils Huntemann,Christian Lisdat*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 利用两台位于不同纬度（相隔4°）的地面光钟（optical atomic clocks），测量它们之间的频移并与广义相对论（general relativity）的引力红移（gravitational redshift）预言比较，在4.6×10⁻⁵不确定度水平上验证了引力红移，将地面检验精度提升两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论预言钟频比较同时受引力时间膨胀（gravitational time dilation）与运动学时间膨胀（kinematic time dilation）影响；运动学贡献已被高精度检验，而引力红移的地面实验检验相对不够严格，因此需要提升地面检验的灵敏度。

Method: 测量两台固定于地球（Earth-fixed）、处于不同引力势的光钟之间的频移，并与基于独立大地测量（geodetic measurements）数据由广义相对论给出的红移预言进行比较；实验采用纬度相隔4°的钟对布局，利用地球自转（Earth's rotation）带来的离心势（centrifugal potential）效应。

Result: 观测频移与预言一致，在4.6×10⁻⁵不确定度水平上检验了引力红移，较此前地面检验改善两倍；4°的纬度间隔使对引力红移违反的灵敏度提升三倍，等效于小纬度间隔下约1200 m的高度差。

Conclusion: 地球自转可作为一种此前未被利用的资源（unexploited resource），推动地面引力红移检验向此前天基实验（space-based experiments）的灵敏度水平迈进。

Abstract: General relativity predicts that frequency comparisons between clocks are affected by both gravitational and kinematic time dilation. While the kinematic contribution has been tested with high accuracy, experimental tests of the gravitational redshift remain comparatively less stringent. Here, we measure the frequency shift between two Earth-fixed optical atomic clocks at different potentials and compare it to the redshift predicted by general relativity using independent geodetic measurements. The observed shift agrees with the prediction, testing the gravitational redshift at the $4.6 \times 10^{-5}$ uncertainty level, improving ground-based tests by a factor two. Previous ground-based tests separated the clocks mainly vertically. For clocks at different latitudes, their height difference is governed not only by the gravitational potentials at their locations but also by the centrifugal potentials caused by Earth's rotation. As both contributions can partially cancel, the gravitational contribution can be considerably larger than their height difference suggests. In this work, a separation of the clocks by $4^\circ$ in latitude enhances their sensitivity to a gravitational redshift violation threefold, to the equivalent of an approximately 1200 m difference in height for small latitudinal separation. Our results establish Earth's rotation as a so far unexploited resource for advancing ground-based tests of the gravitational redshift towards the sensitivity of previous space-based experiments.

</details>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Chromatic calorimetry using nanocomposites: status and future challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27636)
*Anne-Mazarine Lyon,Etiennette Auffray,Marco Caporale,Katerina Decka,Michael Doser,Jean-Louis Faure,Greta Gajdan,Yacine Haddad,Jan Kral,Joseph Mariano,Loris Martinazzoli,Emanuele Mazzola,Eva Mihokova,Solangel Rojas Torres,Matteo Salomoni,Vojtech Zabloudil*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该文综述了色度量热学（chromatic calorimetry）这一新兴电磁量热器概念，内容涵盖原理验证型原型机的实验结果、基于量子点（quantum-dot）构型的模拟研究、纳米复合材料（nanocomposites）开发进展与挑战，以及未来展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来高能物理实验（如FCC-ee）对先进探测器技术有迫切需求；色度量热学有望兼得均匀量热器（homogeneous calorimeters）的精确能量测量能力与粒子簇射（particle showers）的纵向重建能力。

Method: 采用综述（review）方式，梳理色度量热学的当前研究现状，包括原理验证型原型机（proof-of-concept prototypes）的实验结果与量子点基构型的模拟研究，并讨论纳米复合材料的相关进展。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量结果；所综述的证据包括原型机实验测量与模拟研究两类，并指出纳米复合材料开发中已取得近期进展但仍存在当前挑战。

Conclusion: 色度量热学是一种具潜力的电磁量热方案，但纳米复合材料等方面仍面临挑战；文末对其未来发展前景作出展望。

Abstract: The development of advanced detector technologies will play a key role in future high-energy physics experiments, such as the FCC-ee. In this context, chromatic calorimetry is an emerging concept for electromagnetic calorimeters, with the potential to combine the precise energy measurement of homogeneous calorimeters with longitudinal reconstruction of particle showers. The state of the art in chromatic calorimetry is reviewed, including experimental results from proof-of-concept prototypes and simulation studies investigating quantum-dot-based configurations. Recent progress and current challenges in the development of nanocomposites are also discussed. Finally, future prospects for chromatic calorimetry are outlined.

</details>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Thermal photon production in massless boost-invariant gases: hydrodynamization without thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27332)
*Caio V. P. de Brito,Gabriel S. Denicol*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该文用弛豫时间近似下的相对论动理学理论研究重离子碰撞中的流体化与热化，发现能量-动量张量在远离局部平衡时仍可由相对论流体动力学描述，而单粒子分布函数则不能，表明流体行为的出现并不要求热化；非平衡修正会显著影响热光子发射率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超相对论重离子碰撞（ultrarelativistic heavy-ion collisions）中流体化（hydrodynamization）与热化（thermalization）的关系，特别是厘清流体动力学描述成立是否需要系统达到局部热平衡。

Method: 将夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）建模为经历 boost-invariant 纵向膨胀的无质量粒子经典气体，在弛豫时间近似（relaxation-time approximation）下基于矩方法（method of moments）的半解析方法求解玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation）。

Result: 即使系统远离局部平衡（local equilibrium），能量-动量张量（energy-momentum tensor）仍能被相对论流体动力学（relativistic fluid dynamics）很好地描述；但单粒子分布函数（single-particle distribution function）不能被流体动力学截断良好近似。在前向散射近似（forward scattering approximation）下，超出流体动力学极限的非平衡修正对热光子发射率（thermal photon emission rate）有显著影响。

Conclusion: 流体行为的出现并不要求热化，即流体化与热化可解耦；同时非平衡效应对热光子等观测量不可忽略，需在相关预言中予以考虑。

Abstract: We study hydrodynamization and thermalization in ultrarelativistic heavy-ion collisions using relativistic kinetic theory. Modelling the quark-gluon plasma as a classical gas of massless particles undergoing a boost-invariant longitudinal expansion, we solve the Boltzmann equation in the relaxation-time approximation via a semi-analytical method based on the method of moments. We show that the energy-momentum tensor is well described by relativistic fluid dynamics even when the system is far from local equilibrium. The single-particle distribution function, on the other hand, is not well approximated by a hydrodynamic truncation, suggesting that the onset of hydrodynamic behaviour does not require thermalization. We then quantify how the departure from equilibrium affects the emission rate of thermal photons in the forward scattering approximation, and show that non-equilibrium corrections beyond the hydrodynamic limit can have a significant impact on this observable.

</details>

### [188] [Numerical tests of 5D APS $η$-invariant phases for 4D chiral fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27307)
*Tatsuhiro Misumi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该工作通过五维 Atiyah–Patodi–Singer（APS）η-不变量数值计算四维手征费米子行列式的相位，并在无反常的 SU(5) 表示 10⊕5̄ 及非阿贝尔子群背景上验证其与独立四维相位的吻合，误差在 0.03%–0.5% 量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四维手征费米子行列式的相位（chiral phase）难以直接计算，而反常消除后相位仍可能非零。作者希望检验以五维 APS η-不变量（APS η-invariant）作为相位定义能否在格点式背景上重现四维结果。

Method: 在四维环面（four-torus）上取与某一坐标无关的规范场背景，使四维相位可由三维狄拉克谱（3D Dirac spectrum）独立算出；再构造两种不同的五维延拓（5D bulk extensions），其中一种需改变第五方向的空间规范场并做耦合的五维谱计算，采用热核外推（heat-kernel extrapolation）提取 APS 相位。

Result: SU(5) 阿贝尔背景（Abelian background）下两种五维延拓均以 0.03% 精度重现四维相位；单电荷检验以 0.3% 精度重现 Chern–Simons 反常系数（anomaly coefficient）。SU(4)×U(1) 非阿贝尔背景在生成完整 su(4) 代数时，固定空间场的五维相位与四维相位在六个热核参数上吻合至 0.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，反常消除（anomaly cancellation）之后，五维 APS 计算能够重现非零的手征相位，支持 APS η-不变量作为四维手征行列式相位的可行定义，并适用于含变场延拓及嵌入 SU(2) 背景的情形。

Abstract: We calculate the phase of a 4D chiral fermion determinant from the 5D Atiyah--Patodi--Singer (APS) $η$-invariant. We study the anomaly-free $SU(5)$ representation $\mathbf{10}\oplus\overline{\mathbf 5}$ on a four-torus, using gauge fields independent of one coordinate. For these backgrounds, the 4D phase can be calculated independently from a 3D Dirac spectrum. For an Abelian background embedded in $SU(5)$, two different 5D bulk extensions reproduce the 4D phase within $0.03\%$ in the tested heat-kernel extrapolations. One extension changes the spatial gauge field along the fifth direction and requires a coupled 5D spectral calculation. An independent single-charge check reproduces the Chern--Simons anomaly coefficient within $0.3\%$ using quadratic extrapolations. We also calculate 5D APS phases for non-Abelian backgrounds in the $SU(4)\times U(1)$ subgroup. For a background generating the full $\mathfrak{su}(4)$ algebra, the 5D phase with a fixed spatial field agrees with the independent 4D phase within $0.5\%$ over the six heat-kernel parameters examined. We further study a varying-field 5D extension for an embedded $SU(2)$ background. These results show that the 5D APS calculation reproduces a nonzero chiral phase after anomaly cancellation.

</details>


### [189] [Solving fermionic sign problems with physics-informed kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27768)
*Kenji Fukushima,Friederike Ihssen,Renzo Kapust,Jan M. Pawlowski*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该论文将物理信息核（physics-informed kernels, PIKs）的生成式采样方法推广至具有复杂分布且结构与费米子系统（fermionic systems）类似的一般物理系统，通过两步PIK变换把复杂系统的采样问题约化为实高斯的采样问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标系统具有复杂分布，并存在加权复高斯和（weighted sums of complex Gaussian distributions）的符号问题（sign problems），直接采样困难，因此需要一种能规避符号问题并保持可接受采样成本的方法。

Method: 分两步进行：第一步用PIK将实正态分布（real normal distribution）映射为加权复高斯和，该步骤称为PIKfan；第二步用第二个PIK将加权和演化为目标理论的分布；两步结合后，主要计算任务变为计算PIK本身。

Result: PIKfan的采样成本与实正态分布相当（the sampling cost of a real normal distribution），从而将一般复系统的采样任务约化为高斯采样任务，并解决了相应加权复高斯和的符号问题。

Conclusion: 方法在一维哈伯德模型（one-site Hubbard model）任意化学势情形及其φ^4形变扩展上得到了示例验证，表明该框架可用于一类复杂分布、费米子型结构的物理系统。

Abstract: We extend the generative sampling approach with physics-informed kernels (PIKs) [1,2] to a general class of physics systems with complex distributions and a structure also encountered in fermionic systems. The resolution of sign problems of generic weighted sums of complex Gaußian distributions with the PIK approach is central to this application. In a first step, PIKs map a real normal distribution to such a weighted sum, and this procedure is called the PIKfan. The PIKfan has the sampling cost of a real normal distribution. In a second step, this weighted sum is evolved with a second PIK into the distribution of the theory at hand. In combination, we reduce the sampling task for a general class of complex systems to a Gaußian one, and the main computational task is that of computing the PIK. We illustrate the approach with the one-site Hubbard model at arbitrary chemical potential and with $φ^4$-deformed extensions of it.

</details>
